*Marvin Hofer,Erhard Rahm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出KGI-Bench基准，用于评估知识图谱集成管线的质量，通过覆盖率、正确性和一致性三个指标分析12种管线的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱集成涉及多种任务和管线，但目前缺乏通用的方法来评估这些管线的整体质量和性能，以确定最佳选择。

Method: 构建KGI-Bench基准，包含种子KG、三种格式的输入数据、参考KG作为真值，利用覆盖率、正确性和一致性三个互补指标评估管线输出（更新后的KG）。

Result: 对12种不同的集成管线进行了比较评估，分析了它们在不同输入数据格式和设计选择下的行为表现。

Conclusion: KGI-Bench能够有效评估知识图谱集成管线的质量，为选择最优管线提供了系统性方法。

Abstract: Integrating new data into knowledge graphs (KG) typically involves different tasks that are executed within workflows or pipelines There are many possible pipelines for a specific integration problem but there is not yet a general approach to evaluate the overall quality and performance of such pipelines to be able to determine the best choices. We therefore propose a new benchmark KGI-Bench to evaluate integration pipelines that ingest different kinds of input data into an existing KG. We evaluate pipelines by analyzing their output, i.e., the updated KG, with the three complementary quality metrics coverage, correctness and consistency. We also provide benchmark datasets (seed KG, overlapping input data of three formats, reference KG as a ground truth) for the movie domain. To demonstrate the applicability and usefulness of the proposed benchmark, we comparatively evaluate 12 pipelines and analyze their behavior across different input data formats and design choices.

</details>


### [36] [Scaling Observation-aware Planning in Uncertain Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22364)
*Adrian Zvizdenco,Arthur Conrado Veiga Bosquetti,Alberto Lluch Lafuente,Christoph Matheja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在不确定领域中为智能体选择传感能力以平衡任务可达性与硬件/处理成本的问题，提出了一种基于POMDP分解的新求解方法，将传感器选择问题（SSP）和位置可观测性问题（POP）的求解规模提升了3个数量级、运行时间提升了5个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不确定领域中，为智能体部署传感能力需要在任务可达性与硬件/处理成本之间进行权衡，这是一个基础性的工程挑战。

Method: 提出了一种基于POMDP分解的新求解方法，用于识别合理的观测函数，改进了原有的参数合成方法。

Result: 在实例规模上提升了3个数量级，在运行时间上提升了5个数量级。

Conclusion: 所提出的符号化/子符号化技术能够有效扩大最优可观测性问题（OOP）中可判定片段的求解规模。

Abstract: Deciding which sensing capabilities to deploy on an agent in uncertain domains is a fundamental engineering challenge, in which one balances task achievability against the high costs of hardware and processing. This problem has previously been formalized as the Optimal Observability Problem (OOP), based on the well-known Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) model for decision-making. This work studies (sub-)symbolic techniques to scale solving of decidable fragments of the OOP, namely the Sensor Selection Problem (SSP) and the Positional Observability Problem (POP). Besides improving the original approach based on parameter synthesis, we develop a new solving method that identifies sensible observation functions via decomposition of POMDPs, improving performance by 3 and 5 orders of magnitude for instance size and runtime, respectively.

</details>


### [37] [Epicure: Navigating the Emergent Geometry of Food Ingredient Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22391)
*Jakub Radzikowski,Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Epicure是一个多语言跳元食材嵌入模型族，基于11个来源、7种语言的414万条食谱训练，通过LLM增强管道标准化为1790种规范食材，并利用共现图与化合物图生成三种不同随机游走策略的嵌入模型（Cooc、Chem、Core），分别侧重食谱上下文、化学属性和两者混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食材嵌入模型通常局限于单一语言或配方来源，缺乏对食材化学属性与食谱上下文关系的系统整合，且缺乏多语言、大规模食谱数据的统一标准化处理方法。

Method: 收集414万条来自11个来源、跨7种语言的多语言食谱数据，通过LLM增强管道将原始食材字符串标准化为1790个规范条目；构建203,508条边的食材共现NPMI图和80,019条边的FlavorDB食材-化合物类型图（含2247个类型化合物节点、15个类别）；基于Metapath2Vec框架，设计三种随机游走变体：Cooc（仅共现图）、Chem（仅化合物元路径）、Core（共现与化合物混合游走）。

Result: 成功构建了Epicure族三个嵌入模型，分别位于化学属性与食谱上下文光谱的不同位置，实现了食材的化学性质与烹饪语境的多维度表征。

Conclusion: Epicure提供了多语言、大规模食材嵌入的基准框架，通过灵活融合化学与食谱信息，为食材表示学习提供了可调节的上下文-化学平衡方案。

Abstract: We present Epicure, a family of three sibling skip-gram ingredient embeddings retrained from scratch on a multilingual recipe corpus. We aggregate 4.14M recipes from 11 sources spanning seven languages, English, Chinese, Russian, Vietnamese, Spanish, Turkish, Indonesian, German, and Indian-English, and normalise the raw ingredient strings to 1,790 canonical entries via an LLM-augmented pipeline. A 203,508-edge ingredient-ingredient NPMI graph and an 80,019-edge typed FlavorDB ingredient-compound graph, 2,247 typed compound nodes across 15 categories, seed three Metapath2Vec variants that share architecture and hyperparameters and differ only in the random-walk schema: Cooc walks the co-occurrence graph only, Chem walks the typed compound metapaths only, and Core blends both via injected ingredient-ingredient walks at controlled mixing, placing each model at a distinct point on the chemistry-vs-recipe-context spectrum.

</details>


### [38] [S2ED: From Story to Executable Descriptions for Consistency-Aware Story Illustration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22448)
*Sijing Yin,Jiamou Liu,Xiao Tang,Yaser Shakib,Qian Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出S2ED框架，将故事文本转化为可执行描述序列，通过多智能体协作实现多帧故事插图的一致性生成，无需训练且兼容多种生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多帧故事插图需要跨帧的长程一致性（包括叙事分解、角色身份、布局和情感持续性），而单图像文生图模型无法直接满足这一需求，现有方法存在训练成本高或一致性不足的问题。

Method: S2ED是一个无训练、模型无关的提示层框架，包含三个智能体：1) 叙事分割智能体（将故事分段）；2) 角色属性锚定智能体（规范化角色特征）；3) 空间与情感线索丰富智能体（增强空间和情感信息）。框架通过可解释的提示承载状态传播和局部编辑来修复漂移，无需重训练生成器。

Result: 在Flintstones和Shakoo Maku数据集上的实验表明，S2ED在序列级一致性和角色保真度方面优于强提示方法、大模型规划方法以及基于参考训练的方法，在自动评估指标和人工评判中均表现更好。

Conclusion: S2ED作为训练免费、模型无关的框架，能有效提升多帧故事插图的一致性，并已部署到端到端的故事书生成系统中，用于儿童插图故事创作。

Abstract: Multi-frame story illustration requires long-horizon coherence beyond single-image text-to-image generation, including narrative decomposition and persistent character identity, layout, and affect across frames. We propose Story-to-Executable Descriptions (S2ED), a training-free, model-agnostic, prompt-layer framework that converts a full story into a sequence of explicit, editable executable descriptions for more consistent rendering. S2ED coordinates three agents to segment the narrative, ground canonical character attributes, and enrich spatial and affective cues, enabling interpretable prompt-carried state propagation and local edits to repair drift without retraining the generator. Experiments on Flintstones and Shakoo Maku show that S2ED improves sequence-level consistency and character fidelity over strong prompting, large-model planning, and a reference training-based method, under both automatic metrics and human judgments. We also deploy S2ED in an end-to-end story-to-storybook system for children's illustrated stories, with a supplementary video.

</details>


### [39] [KAPPS: A knowledge-based CPPS Architecture for the Circular Factory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22457)
*Etienne Hoffmann,Jan-Felix Klein,Sören Weindel,Max Goebels,Sebastian Behrendt,Daniel Hernández,Ratan Bahadur Thapa,Jürgen Fleischer,Kai Furmans,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对循环制造中产品状态异质、过程动态重配等挑战，设计了一个基于知识图谱的CPPS架构KAPPS。该架构以本体知识图作为统一数据骨干，结合语义接口层实现异构系统间的数据集成、推理与通信，并包含约束执行与事件驱动规划模块，支持执行计划的增量自适应与人机知识交换。通过异常检测与约束执行两个用例验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性制造依赖均质材料和预定义流程，而循环制造需处理状态不确定的回收产品，现有制造IT架构无法表达和管理组件的运行时独特性，亟需新型制造系统架构以应对可变产品状态、动态重构流程及人机知识融合的需求。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法论，从五个互补视角推导出14项需求，据此设计KAPPS架构。核心是以本体知识图作为统一数据骨干，结合语义接口层实现数据集成与通信，并集成约束执行与事件驱动规划模块，支持不确定性下的执行计划增量调整。

Result: 通过两个用例验证：（i）基于知识图中介服务的异常检测与学习；（ii）模块化传送系统中的运行时约束执行。随后基于14项需求对架构进行了评估。

Conclusion: KAPPS架构能够将知识图从集成层转变为工厂的权威写入时状态，有效支持循环制造中异构系统的数据一致性、推理与通信，以及不确定性下的自适应规划。

Abstract: While linear manufacturing relies on homogeneous materials and predefined process sequences, circular manufacturing reintroduces used products with heterogeneous and uncertain conditions. This shift demands manufacturing systems capable of handling variable product states, dynamically reconfigurable processes, and the integration of human and machine knowledge. Conventional manufacturing IT architectures, designed for stable structures and deterministic execution, are unable to meet these requirements, as they cannot adequately represent and manage the uniqueness of individual components at runtime. Following a design science methodology for developing a Cyber Physical Production System for circular manufacturing, we derive 14 requirements from five complementary perspectives. Based on these requirements, we design KAPPS, a knowledge-based architecture that uses an ontology-grounded knowledge graph as a unifying data backbone, combined with a semantic interface layer to enable consistent data and information integration, reasoning, and communication across heterogeneous systems and services, turning the knowledge graph from an integration layer into the factories authoritative write-time state. KAPPS incorporates modules for constraint enforcement and event-driven planning, enabling incremental adaptation of execution plans under uncertainty and human-machine knowledge exchange. The applicability of KAPPS is demonstrated through two implemented use cases: (i) Anomaly detection and learning through knowledge graph mediated services and (ii) runtime constraint enforcement in a modular conveyor system. Subsequently, the architecture is evaluated against the 14 requirements (ed. abstract shortened)

</details>


### [40] [Compiling Agentic Workflows into LLM Weights: Near-Frontier Quality at Two Orders of Magnitude Less Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22502)
*Simon Dennis,Rivaan Patil,Kevin Shabahang,Hao Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了当前智能体编排框架（如LangGraph、CrewAI等）普遍采用外部编排器架构的局限性，并论证了将程序直接编译到小型微调模型权重中（"地下智能体"）的可行性与优势。作者通过三个实际场景（旅行预订、Zoom支持、保险理赔）的实验，回应了开发者对替代方案的三项主要顾虑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体编排框架（超290,000 GitHub星标）均采用外部编排器架构，已被证明在程序性任务中不如直接将程序写入前沿模型系统提示词的方法，但后者存在上下文窗口消耗、依赖前沿模型、暴露专有程序等问题。将程序编译到小型微调模型权重中可解决这些问题，但开发者仍偏向编排架构。本文旨在识别并实证回应开发者采用的三大障碍。

Method: 识别开发者采纳地下智能体方法的三个感知障碍，并通过三个实际场景（14节点的旅行预订、14节点含产品知识的Zoom支持、55节点6个决策中心的保险理赔）进行实证研究。

Result: 通过三个实际场景（旅行预订、Zoom支持、保险理赔）的实证评估，回应了开发者对地下智能体方法的三个主要顾虑。

Conclusion: 将程序编译到小型微调模型权重中的"地下智能体"方法在技术上是可行的，能够解决外部编排架构的多个问题，但当前开发者仍因三个感知障碍而倾向编排架构，本文通过实证研究回应了这些顾虑。

Abstract: Agent orchestration frameworks have proliferated, collectively exceeding 290,000 GitHub stars across LangGraph, CrewAI, Google ADK, OpenAI Agents SDK, Semantic Kernel, Strands, and LlamaIndex. All follow the same pattern: an external orchestrator above the LLM, injecting instructions and routing decisions every turn. Recent work has shown this architecture is dominated for procedural tasks by simply providing the procedure in a frontier model's system prompt [Dennis et al., 2026a], at the cost of consuming the context window, requiring a frontier model for every conversation, and exposing proprietary procedures to third-party providers. Compiling the procedure into the weights of a small fine-tuned model -- creating a subterranean agent -- should resolve all of these concerns, and prior work (SimpleTOD, FireAct, SynTOD, WorkflowLLM, Agent Lumos) has shown the technique works. Yet developer adoption has overwhelmingly favored orchestration. We identify three perceived barriers and address each empirically across travel booking (14 nodes), Zoom support (14 nodes, product-specific knowledge), and insurance claims (55 nodes, 6 decision hubs).

</details>


### [41] [LACO: Adaptive Latent Communication for Collaborative Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22504)
*Tianhao Chen,Yuheng Wu,Dongman Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LACO，一种无需训练的潜在通信范式，通过迭代潜在推理、跨视野显著性归因和结构化语义知识蒸馏，解决协同驾驶中语言通信的高延迟和信息丢失问题，在CARLA实验中显著降低通信和推理延迟，同时保持强协同驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同驾驶方法从共享视觉特征发展到基于语言推理的行为协调，但语言通信存在自回归解码导致的高延迟以及将丰富内部表征压缩为离散token导致的信息丢失两大挑战。

Method: 提出LACO（潜在通信范式），包含三个核心组件：1）迭代潜在推理（ILD）用于潜在空间中的推理；2）跨视野显著性归因（CHSA）用于通信高效的信息选择；3）结构化语义知识蒸馏（SSKD）用于稳定以自我为中心的决策。该方法无需额外训练，可直接适配预训练驾驶模型至协同设置。

Result: 在CARLA中的闭环实验表明，LACO显著降低了通信和推理延迟，同时保持了强大的协同驾驶性能。

Conclusion: 潜在通信范式LACO有效解决了语言通信在协同驾驶中的延迟和信息损失问题，为多智能体协作提供了一种高效、无需训练的替代方案。

Abstract: Collaborative driving aims to improve safety and efficiency by enabling connected vehicles to coordinate under partial observability. Recent approaches have evolved from sharing visual features for perception to exchanging language-based reasoning through foundation models for behavioral coordination. Though communicating in language provides intuitive information, it introduces two challenges: high latency caused by autoregressive decoding and information loss caused by compressing rich internal representations into discrete tokens. To address these challenges, we analyze latent communication in collaborative driving under inherent limitations of multi-agent settings. Our analysis reveals agent identity confusion, where direct fusion of latent states entangles decision representations across vehicles. Motivated by this, we propose LACO, a training-free \textbf{LA}tent \textbf{CO}mmunication paradigm that seamlessly adapts pretrained driving models to collaborative settings. LACO introduces Iterative Latent Deliberation (ILD) for latent reasoning, Cross-Horizon Saliency Attribution (CHSA) for communication-efficient information selection, and Structured Semantic Knowledge Distillation (SSKD) to stabilize ego-centric decision making. Closed-loop experiments in CARLA show that LACO notably reduces communication and inference latency while maintaining strong collaborative driving performance.

</details>


### [42] [Towards Direct Evaluation of Harness Optimizers via Priority Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22505)
*Kai Tzu-iunn Ong,Minseok Kang,Dongwook Choi,Junhee Cho,Seungju Kim,Seungwon Lim,Geunha Jang,Minwoo Oh,Bogyung Jeong,Sunghwan Kim,Taeyoon Kwon,Jinyoung Yeo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出优先级排序（Priority Ranking）方法，通过让优化器对智能体框架组件进行排序来直接评估其优化能力，解决了传统间接评估中无法捕捉中间步骤错误的问题。基于Shor数据集（182个人工验证场景）的实验表明，排序性能与实际优化能力强相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架优化（Harness Optimization）研究仅通过目标智能体的最终性能增益来间接评估优化器，忽略了中间步骤的错误行为，导致无法区分优化器的更新是源于明智决策还是单纯试错。因此需要一种直接、低成本的优化器评估方法。

Method: 提出优先级排序（Priority Ranking）方法：让框架优化器对给定框架中的组件（如工具）按其对智能体性能提升/阻碍的潜力进行排序，从而在步骤级别量化优化器能力，无需昂贵的回滚或人工检查。同时构建了Shor数据集，包含182个跨领域、跨设计、跨时间阶段的人工验证优化场景。

Result: 优化器的排序性能与其在多步框架优化中实际提升智能体的能力之间存在相关性，验证了优先级排序可作为优化能力的可靠预测指标。

Conclusion: 优先级排序提供了一种简单、低成本的直接评估框架优化器的方法，能有效预测优化器在实际优化中的表现，为自动化智能体创建的研究提供了更可靠的评估范式。

Abstract: Harness optimization enables automated agent creation by having an optimizer agent iteratively update the harness of target agents. Despite its success, current studies evaluate optimizers solely by observing target agents' performance gains. This indirect end-improvement evaluation neglects optimizers' actions at intermediate steps, which are often erroneous and hinder agent performance. Therefore, it is unclear whether harness optimization is driven by optimizers' informed update actions or simply trial-and-error. This necessitates direct evaluation of harness optimizers. However, evaluating harness optimizers directly is non-trivial and costly due to the lack of oracle harnesses. To address this, we present a simple, low-cost design to directly evaluate them, namely priority ranking. By asking harness optimizers to rank components (e.g., tools) in a given harness by their potential to improve/hinder agent performance when updated, our design quantifies optimizer ability at the step level without expensive rollouts or manual examination. More importantly, optimizers' ranking performance correlates with their ability to improve agents in actual multi-step harness optimization, establishing priority ranking as a reliable predictor of optimization ability. Priority ranking is enabled by Shor, a collection of 182 human-verified optimization scenarios spanning across domains, designs, and time stages. Codes and data can be found at https://github.com/k59118/Harness_Optimizer_Evaluation.

</details>


### [43] [Search-E1: Self-Distillation Drives Self-Evolution in Search-Augmented Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22511)
*Zihan Liang,Yufei Ma,Ben Chen,Zhipeng Qian,Xuxin Zhang,Huangyu Dai,Lingtao Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Search-E1是一种自我演化方法，仅通过vanilla GRPO与离线自蒸馏（OFSD）交替进行，无需额外复杂模块即可提升搜索增强型语言模型在问答任务上的性能，在7个QA基准上以Qwen2.5-3B达到0.440平均EM，超越所有开源基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前将语言模型训练为搜索增强推理智能体的主流范式是后训练，但最近的工作通过添加复杂机制（外部监督、辅助模块、树搜索、手工奖励等）来提升性能，这些方法虽然有效但使训练流程膨胀且依赖特定资源/设计。本文旨在探究这些复杂机制是否真正必要。

Method: 提出Search-E1，一种自我演化方法，核心是交替使用vanilla GRPO（组相对策略优化）和离线自蒸馏（OFSD）。每个GRPO轮次后，策略在其自身训练问题上进行推理；然后通过token级前向KL散度目标，将策略在推理时的分布对齐到其在特权上下文（暴露更高效的兄弟轨迹）下的自身分布。

Result: 在7个QA基准测试上，Search-E1使用Qwen2.5-3B达到0.440的平均精确匹配（EM），超越了所有开源基线（同规模和更大规模）。

Conclusion: Search-E1证明，无需外部监督、辅助模块或复杂奖励设计，仅通过vanilla GRPO与离线自蒸馏的简单交替即可提供密集的逐步骤监督信号，让搜索增强型智能体通过自我演化实现显著性能提升。

Abstract: Post-training has become the dominant recipe for turning a language model into a competent search-augmented reasoning agent. A line of recent work pushes its performance further by adding elaborate machinery on top of this standard pipeline. These augmentations import external supervision from stronger external systems, attach auxiliary modules such as process reward models or retrospective critics, restructure the rollout itself with tree search or multi-stage curricula, or shape the reward with hand-crafted bonuses and penalties. Each addition delivers a measurable gain, but each also inflates the training pipeline and ties the recipe to resources or designs that may not always be available. We take a step back and ask whether any of this machinery is actually necessary, and propose Search-E1, a self-evolution method that lets a search-augmented agent improve through only vanilla GRPO interleaved with offline self-distillation (OFSD). After each GRPO round, the policy rolls out on its own training questions. A token-level forward KL objective then aligns the policy's inference-time distribution to its own distribution under a privileged context that exposes a more efficient sibling trajectory. Despite this simplicity, the procedure naturally provides dense per-step supervision. On seven QA benchmarks, Search-E1 reaches $0.440$ average EM with Qwen2.5-3B, surpassing all open-source baselines at both scales. Code and complete version will be made public soon.

</details>


### [44] [A Subjective Logic-based method for runtime confidence updates in safety arguments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22530)
*Benjamin Herd,Jessica Kelly,Clarissa Heinemann,João-Vitor Zacchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于主观逻辑的动态定量保障方法，通过整合设计时证据和运行时安全性能指标（SPI），实现保障案例的连续置信度更新，并在模拟的ML施工锥检测场景中验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态安全案例无法反映系统运行时行为变化，缺乏动态置信度更新机制，需一种能实时响应运行时证据的方法来持续量化保障可信度。

Method: 构建基于主观逻辑（SL）的保障案例框架，融合设计时证据与窗口化运行时SPI；采用非贝叶斯的规则更新置信度：无违规时逐步提升，发生违规时立即惩罚扣减，以优先保障安全响应速度。

Result: 在仿真施工区辅助功能的ML施工锥检测组件上，展示了置信度随运行时SPI证据的动态演化过程，验证了方法对违规行为的快速惩罚响应能力。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效实现运行时驱动的动态定量保障，在安全关键系统中具有优于经典贝叶斯更新的响应时效性，可作为静态安全案例的增强补充。

Abstract: We present a method for dynamic quantitative assurance that enhances static safety cases with continuous, runtime-driven confidence updates. The method quantifies and propagates confidence across the development lifecycle by integrating design-time evidence and windowed runtime Safety Performance Indicators (SPIs) within a single Subjective Logic (SL)-based assurance case. At runtime, SPI evidence is continuously evaluated, and targeted claims are updated using a rule that increases confidence in the absence of violations and imposes prompt penalties when violations occur. This design prioritizes safety-relevant responsiveness over exact classical Bayesian posterior updates. We demonstrate the method using a simulation-based construction zone assist function, focusing on an ML-based construction cone detection component, and show how confidence evolves as SPI evidence is observed in operation.

</details>


### [45] [TerminalWorld: Benchmarking Agents on Real-World Terminal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22535)
*Zhaoyang Chu,Jiarui Hu,Xingyu Jiang,Pengyu Zou,Han Li,Chao Peng,Peter O'Hearn,Earl T. Barr,Mark Harman,Federica Sarro,He Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TerminalWorld 是一个自动从80,870个终端记录中逆向工程生成1,530个真实终端任务的基准引擎，覆盖18个类别和1,280个独特命令。在200个精选任务上测试8个前沿模型和6个智能体，最高通过率仅62.5%，且与现有基准相关性弱（Pearson r=0.20）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终端任务基准通常由专家人工设计，难以真实反映开发者实际工作中的终端使用场景，缺乏真实性和可扩展性。

Method: 构建可扩展数据引擎，从80,870个"野外"终端录屏中自动逆向工程出高保真评估任务，生成1,530个验证任务，并从中精选200个经人工审查的代表性子集TerminalWorld-Verified。

Result: 在TerminalWorld-Verified上对8个前沿模型和6个智能体进行全面评测，最高通过率仅62.5%；与现有专家策展基准Terminal-Bench得分之间仅存在弱相关性（Pearson r=0.20）。

Conclusion: 当前系统在处理真实终端工作流时仍存在显著困难；TerminalWorld通过自动化的数据引擎实现了真实性与可扩展性，能够随开发者实践演进持续评估智能体能力。

Abstract: We introduce TerminalWorld, a scalable data engine that automatically reverse-engineers high-fidelity evaluation tasks from "in-the-wild" terminal recordings. Processing 80,870 terminal recordings, the engine yields a full benchmark of 1,530 validated tasks, spanning 18 real-world categories, ranging from short everyday operations to workflows exceeding 50 steps, and covering 1,280 unique commands. From these, we curate a Verified subset of 200 representative, manually reviewed tasks. Comprehensive benchmarking on TerminalWorld-Verified across eight frontier models and six agents reveals that current systems still struggle with authentic terminal workflows, achieving a maximum pass rate of only 62.5%. Moreover, TerminalWorld captures real-world terminal capabilities distinct from existing expert-curated benchmarks (e.g., Terminal-Bench), with only a weak correlation to their scores (Pearson r=0.20). The automated engine makes TerminalWorld authentic and scalable by construction, enabling it to evaluate agents in real-world terminal environments as developer practices evolve. Data and code are available at https://github.com/EuniAI/TerminalWorld.

</details>


### [46] [Think Thrice Before You Speak: Dual knowledge-enhanced Theory-of-Mind Reasoning for Persuasive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22602)
*Minghui Ma,Bin Guo,Runze Yang,Mengqi Chen,Yan Liu,Jingqi Liu,Yahan Pei,Xuehao Ma,Qiuyun Zhang,Zhiwen Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于心智理论的说服式对话（ToM-PD）任务，构建大规模标注数据集ToM-BPD，并设计知识增强的逐步推理框架TTBYS，实验表明Qwen3-8B+TTBYS在预测欲望、信念和说服策略上分别超越GPT-5达1.20%、22.80%和16.97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM依赖简单提示策略且缺乏充分的ToM知识，无法捕捉心理状态间的内在依赖关系，导致表示碎片化和推理不稳定。

Method: 基于BDI框架显式建模多轮对话中心理状态的序列依赖关系；构建大规模细粒度标注数据集ToM-BPD；提出TTBYS知识增强逐步推理框架，利用显式和隐式先验经验改进LLM对欲望、信念和说服策略的推理。

Result: Qwen3-8B+TTBYS在欲望预测上超越GPT-5达1.20%，信念预测上超越22.80%，说服策略预测上超越16.97%；案例研究表明该方法提升了推理的可解释性和一致性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模心理状态依赖关系与知识增强的逐步推理，可显著提升LLM在说服式对话中的ToM推理能力，增强可解释性与一致性。

Abstract: Persuasive dialogue requires reasoning about others' latent mental states, a capability known as Theory of Mind (ToM). However, due to reliance on simple prompting strategies and insufficient ToM knowledge, existing LLMs often fail to capture the intrinsic dependencies among mental states, leading to fragmented representations and unstable reasoning. To address these challenges, we introduce the ToM-based Persuasive Dialogue (ToM-PD) task, grounded in the Belief-Desire-Intention (BDI) framework, which explicitly models the sequential dependencies among mental states in multi-turn dialogues. To facilitate research on this task, we construct a large-scale annotated dataset, ToM-based Broad Persuasive Dialogues (ToM-BPD), capturing fine-grained mental states and corresponding persuasive strategies. We further propose Think Thrice Before You Speak (TTBYS), a knowledge-enhanced stepwise reasoning framework that leverages both explicit and implicit prior experiences to improve LLMs' inference of desires, beliefs, and persuasive strategies. Experimental results demonstrate that Qwen3-8B equipped with TTBYS outperforms GPT-5 by 1.20%, 22.80%, and 16.97% in predicting desires, beliefs, and persuasive strategies, respectively. Case studies further show that our approach enhances interpretability and consistency in reasoning.

</details>


### [47] [Spreadsheet-RL: Advancing Large Language Model Agents on Realistic Spreadsheet Tasks via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22642)
*Banghao Chi,Yining Xie,Mingyuan Wu,Jingcheng Yang,Jize Jiang,Zhaoheng Li,Shengyi Qian,Minjia Zhang,Klara Nahrstedt,Rui Hou,Xiangjun Fan,Hanchao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Spreadsheet-RL，一个面向电子表格智能体的强化学习微调框架，通过自动化数据收集流水线、领域专用基准数据集和多轮强化学习环境，在真实Excel场景中显著提升了AI智能体的电子表格操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子表格智能体大多依赖通用大语言模型的特化提示，虽能处理简单操作，但难以应对真实应用中复杂的多步骤工作流，亟需专门训练的智能体。

Method: 提出Spreadsheet-RL框架，包含：(1) 自动化流水线从在线论坛规模化收集成对的起始-目标电子表格；(2) 构建Domain-Spreadsheet基准数据集，涵盖金融、供应链管理等领域任务；(3) 设计Spreadsheet Gym环境，通过Python沙箱暴露Excel功能，配备丰富工具集和工具路由规则，支持多轮强化学习训练。

Result: 在SpreadsheetBench上，Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507的Pass@1从12.0%提升至23.4%；在Domain-Spreadsheet数据集上，Pass@1从8.4%提升至17.2%。

Conclusion: Spreadsheet-RL显著增强了AI智能体在通用和领域专用电子表格任务上的性能，展现出良好的泛化能力和实际应用潜力，为推进LLM与数据界面的交互提供了新方向。

Abstract: Spreadsheet systems (e.g., Microsoft Excel, Google Sheets) play a central role in modern data-centric workflows. As AI agents grow increasingly capable of automating complex tasks, such as controlling computers and generating presentations, building an AI-driven spreadsheet agent has emerged as a promising research direction. Most existing spreadsheet agents rely on specialized prompting over general-purpose LLMs; while this design has potentials on simple spreadsheet operations, it struggles to manage the complex, multi-step workflows typical of real-world applications.
  We introduce Spreadsheet-RL, a reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning framework designed to train specialized spreadsheet agents within a realistic Microsoft Excel environment. Spreadsheet-RL features an automated pipeline for scalable collection of paired start-goal spreadsheets from online forums, as well as domain-specific evaluation tasks in areas such as finance and supply chain management, which we compile into the new Domain-Spreadsheet benchmark dataset. It also includes a Spreadsheet Gym environment designed for multi-turn RL: Spreadsheet Gym exposes extensive Excel functionality through a Python sandbox, along with a refined harness that incorporates a comprehensive tool set and carefully designed tool-routing rules for spreadsheet tasks. Through comprehensive experiments, we show that Spreadsheet-RL substantially enhances AI agent's performance on both general and domain-specific spreadsheet tasks: it improves Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507's Pass@1 on SpreadsheetBench from 12.0% to 23.4%, and raises Pass@1 from 8.4% to 17.2% on our curated Domain-Spreadsheet dataset. These results highlight Spreadsheet-RL's strong potential for generalization and real-world adoption in spreadsheet automation, and broadly, its promise for advancing LLM-based interactions with data interfaces in everyday work.

</details>


### [48] [AtelierEval: Agentic Evaluation of Humans & LLMs as Text-to-Image Prompters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22645)
*Hanjun Luo,Zhimu Huang,Sylvia Chung,Yiran Wang,Yingbin Jin,Jialin Li,Jiang Li,Xinfeng Li,Hanan Salam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AtelierEval，首个统一量化提示工程能力的基准，涵盖360个专家设计任务，并引入AtelierJudge评估器，实现人与MLLM在提示生成上的可扩展评估。实验揭示模仿优于规划，倡导图像增强的提示方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I评估仅固定提示、评测模型输出，忽略了上游提示器（人类或MLLM）将用户意图转化为详细提示的能力，缺乏量化这一能力的统一基准。

Method: 构建AtelierEval基准：基于认知视角设计三类任务，通过现实挑战分类法实例化360个专家任务，提供人类和MLLM双接口；提出AtelierJudge：基于技能、记忆增强的智能体评估器，对提示-图像对进行主客观评分。

Result: AtelierJudge与人类专家的Spearman相关系数达0.79，接近人类水平。在4个T2I后端上对8个MLLM和48名人类用户进行实验，验证了基准的有效性，并发现模仿策略优于规划策略。

Conclusion: AtelierEval可作为鲁棒的诊断工具，揭示了当前提示器能力的不足，并指明图像增强的提示生成是未来发展方向。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) systems increasingly rely on upstream prompters, either humans or multimodal large language models (MLLMs), to translate user intent into detailed prompts. Yet current benchmarks fix the prompt and only evaluate T2I models, leaving the prompting proficiency of this upstream component entirely unmeasured. We introduce AtelierEval, the first unified benchmark that quantifies prompting proficiency across 360 expert-crafted tasks. Grounded in a cognitive view, it spans three task categories and instantiates tasks using a taxonomy of real-world challenges, with a dual interface for both humans and MLLMs. To enable scalable and reliable evaluation, we propose AtelierJudge, a skill-based, memory-augmented agentic evaluator. It produces subjective and objective scores for prompt-image pairs, achieving a Spearman correlation of 0.79 with human experts, approaching human performance. Extensive experiments benchmark 8 MLLMs against 48 human users across 4 T2I backends, validate AtelierEval as a robust diagnostic tool, and reveal the superiority of mimicry over planning, advocating for an image-augmented direction for future prompters. Our work is released to support future research.

</details>


### [49] [Claw AI Lab: An Autonomous Multi-Agent Research Team](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22662)
*Fan Wu,Cheng Chen,Zhenshan Tan,Taiyu Zhang,Xinzhen Xu,Yanyu Qian,Dingcheng Gao,Lanyun Zhu,Qi Zhu,Yi Tan,Deyi Ji,Guosheng Lin,Tianrun Chen,Deheng Ye,Fayao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Claw AI Lab 是一个实验室自主研究平台，通过可自定义角色、协作工作流、实时监控和回滚/恢复控制，将自动化研究从隐藏的提示到论文管道转变为可交互的 AI 实验室，并利用 Claw-Code Harness 实现代码库、数据集和检查点的可运行实验集成，在五个 AI 研究案例中优于基线系统 AutoResearchClaw。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化研究系统多为隐藏的 prompt-to-paper 管道（单智能体或固定串行工作流），缺乏可交互性、可控性和实验室般的用户体验，导致实验难以检查、迭代和忠实转化为最终论文，且容易出现部分运行和格式错误的结果报告。

Method: 1) 用户通过一个提示实例化完整研究团队，包含可自定义角色和协作工作流；2) 提供统一仪表板实现实时监控、工件检查和回滚/恢复控制；3) 支持探索、多智能体讨论和复现三种研究模式；4) 核心组件 Claw-Code Harness 连接本地代码库/数据集/检查点，将执行工件反馈回研究循环，提升执行集成度、实验完成度和结果完整性。

Result: 在五个 AI 研究案例的内部评估中，以 AutoResearchClaw 为基线，Claw AI Lab 在 idea 新颖性、实验完整性和论文呈现质量上被 AI 专家评委一致优先选择。

Conclusion: Claw AI Lab 是迈向新范式的早期步骤：将自主研究构建为可用、可交互且具有可靠性意识的科学基础设施。

Abstract: We present Claw AI Lab, a lab-native autonomous research platform that advances automated research from a hidden prompt-to-paper pipeline into an interactive AI laboratory. Rather than centering the system around a single agent or a fixed serial workflow, we allow users to instantiate a full research team from one prompt, with customizable roles, collaborative workflows, real-time monitoring, artifact inspection, and rollback/resume control through a unified dashboard. The platform also supports distinct research modes for exploration, multi-agent discussion, and reproduction, making autonomous research substantially more steerable and laboratory-like in practice. A key practical contribution of Claw AI Lab lies in its Claw-Code Harness, which connects local codebases, datasets, and checkpoints to runnable experiments and feeds execution artifacts back into the research loop. As a result, the harness improves not only execution integration, but also experimental completion and result integrity: experiments are easier to inspect, iterate on, and faithfully transfer into final papers, reducing common failure modes such as partial runs and malformed result reporting. In our internal evaluation on five AI research case studies, using AutoResearchClaw as the baseline, Claw AI Lab is consistently preferred by AI expert judges on idea novelty, experiment completeness, and paper presentation quality. We view Claw AI Lab as an early step toward a new paradigm: autonomous research as usable, interactive, and reliability-aware scientific infrastructure.

</details>


### [50] [WorkstreamBench: Evaluating LLM Agents on End-to-End Spreadsheet Tasks in Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22664)
*Thomson Yen,Julian Poeltl,Harshith Srinivas Gear,Yilin Meng,Joshua Fan,Adam Shen,Yili Liu,Ali Bauyrzhan,Siri Du,Haoyang Liu,Daniel Guetta,Hongseok Namkoong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对端到端电子表格任务的智能体评估基准，聚焦金融建模和场景分析等关键工作流。评估体系涵盖准确性、公式和格式三个维度。实验发现Claude系列表现领先，但即使最强智能体在复杂任务中也远未达到专业金融标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的电子表格基准测试仅关注问答或单公式编辑，无法衡量智能体从高级用户指令端到端生成完整电子表格的能力，而这一能力对于金融等领域的实际应用至关重要。

Method: 构建了一个包含三个维度的评估分类法：准确性(Accuracy)、公式(Formula)、格式(Format)，每个维度包含细粒度专业标准。设计了不同难度的端到端电子表格生成任务，对多个LLM智能体进行系统评估。

Result: Claude系列在基准测试中领先，输出最专业的电子表格。但随着任务难度增加（超过几个链式计算），所有智能体性能急剧下降，远未达到专业金融标准。

Conclusion: 当前智能体尚无法可靠地在真实工作流所需的复杂度水平上生成专业质量的电子表格，现有能力与实际企业需求之间存在显著差距。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly expected to carry out end-to-end workflows, producing complete artifacts from high-level user instructions. To meet enterprise needs, frontier AI labs have developed agents that can construct entire spreadsheets from scratch. This is especially relevant in finance, where core workflows such as financial modeling, forecasting, and scenario analysis are commonly conducted through spreadsheets. Yet, existing spreadsheet benchmarks do not measure this advanced capability, focusing instead on question-answering or single-formula edits. To address this gap, we provide one of the first evaluations of agents on end-to-end spreadsheet tasks, focusing on economically critical financial workflows such as modeling and scenario analysis. Since deliverables therein are routinely reviewed and revised by multiple stakeholders, judging their quality necessarily involves high-level criteria such as readability or ease of modification. To reflect the multidimensional nature of solution quality, we develop an evaluation taxonomy comprising three dimensions: Accuracy, Formula, and Format, each comprising fine-grained criteria that reflect professional standards. The Claude family leads the benchmark and produces the most professional-looking outputs in our qualitative review, but even the strongest agents frequently fall short of professional finance standards and degrade sharply as the difficulty increases beyond a few chained calculations. This suggests that current agents are not yet able to reliably produce professional-quality spreadsheets at the level of complexity real-world workflows demand.

</details>


### [51] [Is Capability a Liability? More Capable Language Models Make Worse Forecasts When It Matters Most](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22672)
*Nick Merrill,Jaeho Lee,Ezra Karger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM在预测具有超线性增长和体制转换尾部风险的时间序列（如金融和流行病学数据）时，出现逆缩放现象：能力更强的模型反而生成更差的分位数预测。该现象在FBSim基准、SIR流行病和现实数据集（COVID-19、麻疹、房价、恶性通胀）中均存在，源于上尾分布的过度外推。单阈值指标会掩盖此问题，建议使用连续无界准确性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM预测基准多采用单阈值指标（如准确率），可能掩盖模型在尾部风险预测上的失败。需要系统评估LLM在超线性增长和体制转换风险场景下的分布预测能力。

Method: 发布无污染模拟基准FBSim，设计合成SIR流行病和线性对照任务；使用分位数分解分析上尾/下尾差异；在Llama-3.1系列中控制规模和微调变量；对比单阈值与连续评分指标的效果。

Result: 逆缩放现象在多个领域一致出现；失败集中在上尾；模型规模和后训练独立贡献此效应；领域知识不能挽救校准；单阈值指标会反转能力-准确性关系符号。

Conclusion: LLM预测评估必须包含连续无界指标（如分位数评分），仅用单阈值二分类指标会导致对模型能力的误导性排序。逆缩放源于模型对超线性增长过程的激进外推偏差。

Abstract: We document inverse scaling in LLMs on forecasting problems whose underlying time series exhibit superlinear growth and tail risk of regime change, a structure common in finance and epidemiology. On these tasks, more capable models produce worse distributional forecasts. The pattern appears on ForecastBench-Sim (FBSim), a contamination-free, simulated-world benchmark we release, in forecasting synthetic SIR epidemics with a matched linear control, and replicates in real-world datasets on COVID-19, measles, housing markets, and hyperinflation. A per-quantile decomposition shows the failure concentrates at the upper tail, which more capable models shift upward to track aggressive extrapolations of growth, while the lower tail stays put. A within-family study of Llama-3.1 shows that both model scale and post-training independently contribute to this effect. Domain knowledge does not reliably rescue calibration. This inverse scaling does not appear on single-threshold metrics common in LLM forecasting benchmarks, reversing the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on identical outputs. Single-threshold scoring at conventional cutoffs misses the upper-tail cost; tail-inclusive scoring reverses the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on the same outputs. We recommend that LLM forecasting evaluations use continuous (and unbounded) measures of accuracy alongside bounded binary threshold metrics.

</details>


### [52] [Forecasting Scientific Progress with Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22681)
*Sean Wu,Pan Lu,Yupeng Chen,Jonathan Bragg,Yutaro Yamada,Peter Clark,David Clifton,Philip Torr,James Zou,Junchi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CUSP基准框架，用于评估AI系统在科学进展预测中的能力。通过对4,760个科学事件的多维度测试，发现当前前沿模型虽能识别合理的研究方向，但在预测科学突破是否实现及何时实现方面表现不佳，且存在领域异质性、过度自信和响应偏差等问题。研究表明，AI系统尚不能作为可靠的科学进展预测工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI是否能预测科学进步这一核心问题，尽管AI在科学发现中应用日益广泛，但其能否前瞻性地预见科学进展仍不清楚。作者希望系统性地量化当前AI模型在科学预测方面的能力边界与局限性。

Method: 构建了CUSP（Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress）基准，这是一个多学科、事件级别的评估框架，包含可行性评估、机制推理、生成式解决方案设计和时间预测四个维度。在4,760个科学事件上对前沿模型进行测试，并通过训练截止日期控制知识暴露条件。

Result: 模型在识别合理研究方向上有一定能力，但无法可靠预测科学进展是否实现以及何时实现；性能存在显著的领域异质性，AI领域的进展比生物、化学、物理更容易预测；模型对事件发生在训练截止日期前后不敏感，说明局限性不能仅归因于训练数据暴露；额外知识能提升性能但无法达到全信息设置的水平；模型表现出系统性过度自信和响应偏差。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统作为科学进展预测工具存在根本性不足。先验知识的获取并不能转化为可靠的预测能力，模型从事后信息中获益多于前瞻性预测。这表明AI在科学预测方面尚未达到可用水平。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in scientific discovery, yet whether it can anticipate scientific progress remains unclear. To study this question, we introduce a temporally grounded evaluation framework for forecasting scientific progress under controlled knowledge constraints. We present CUSP (Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress), a multi-disciplinary and event-level benchmark that evaluates scientific forecasting in AI systems through feasibility assessment, mechanistic reasoning, generative solution design, and temporal prediction. Across 4,760 scientific events, we observe systematic and domain-dependent limitations in current frontier models. While models can identify plausible research directions from competing candidates, they fail to reliably predict whether scientific advances will be realized and systematically misestimate when they will occur. Performance is highly heterogeneous across domains, with the timing of AI progress more predictable than advances in biology, chemistry, and physics. Performance is largely insensitive to whether events occur before or after the training cutoff, suggesting these limitations cannot be explained solely by knowledge exposure in training data. Under controlled information access, additional pre-cutoff knowledge improves performance but does not close the gap to full-information settings, which becomes more pronounced for high-citation advances. Models also exhibit systematic overconfidence and strong response biases, indicating unreliable uncertainty estimation. Taken together, current AI systems fall short as predictive tools for scientific progress. Access to prior knowledge does not translate into reliable forecasting, and performance benefits more from post-event information than from forward-looking prediction.

</details>


### [53] [AMEL: Accumulated Message Effects on LLM Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22714)
*Sid-ali Temkit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现大语言模型在作为自动评估器时，会因对话历史中已有的正/负极性而产生判断偏差（AMEL效应），这种偏差在模型不确定时更明显，负面历史影响比正面强1.62倍，且不随上下文长度增长而加剧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被广泛应用于自动评估任务（代码审查、内容审核、评分等），且通常在同一对话中处理多个待评估项。研究者关注历史对话的极性是否会污染模型对后续项目的判断，即是否存在积累性偏差。

Method: 通过75,898次API调用，对4家提供商（OpenAI、Anthropic、Google）的11个模型及4个开源模型进行实验。在隔离条件下与在正/负饱和历史条件下展示相同的测试项，对比模型判断的差异。进一步设计三项机制探针实验：分析token概率分布变化、分解负性不对称的语义与token级成分、检验偏差位置的影响。

Result: 1) 模型判断向对话历史的主流极性偏移（d=-0.17, p<10^-46）；2) 偏差集中在模型基线不确定的项上（高熵项d=-0.34 vs 确定项d=-0.15）；3) 偏差不随上下文长度增长（5轮与50轮效果相同）；4) 负面历史比正面历史产生1.62倍偏差（t=13.46, p<10^-39）；5) 模型规模增大可缓解但无法消除偏差（如Opus -0.17 vs Haiku -0.22）；6) token概率连续偏移而非阈值触发；7) 5轮偏差无论出现在50轮历史的哪个位置效果相同。

Conclusion: 对话历史极性会对LLM评估判断产生系统性偏差，建议评估流水线中每条目使用全新上下文；若必须批量处理，应平衡历史中的正负极性。

Abstract: Large language models are routinely used as automated evaluators: to review code, moderate content, or score outputs, often with many items passing through one conversation. We ask whether the polarity of prior conversation history biases subsequent judgments, an effect we call the accumulated message effect on LLM judgments (AMEL). Across 75,898 API calls to 11 models from 4 providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and four open-source models), we present identical test items in isolation or following histories saturated with predominantly positive or negative evaluations.
  Models shift toward the conversation's prevailing polarity (d = -0.17, p < 10^-46). The effect concentrates on items where the model is genuinely uncertain at baseline (d = -0.34 for high-entropy items, vs d = -0.15 when the baseline is deterministic). Bias does not grow with context length: 5 prior turns and 50 produce the same shift (Spearman |r| < 0.01; OLS slope p = 0.80). And there is a negativity asymmetry: paired per item, negative histories induce 1.62x more bias than positive (t = 13.46, p < 10^-39, n = 2,481). Scaling helps but does not solve it (Anthropic: Haiku -0.22 to Opus -0.17; OpenAI: Nano -0.34 to GPT-5.2 -0.17).
  Three follow-ups narrow the mechanism. The token probability distribution shifts continuously, not at a threshold. The negativity asymmetry has both token-level and semantic components, though attributing the balance is exploratory at our sample sizes. Position does not matter: five biased turns anywhere in a 50-turn history produce the same shift. The simplest fix for evaluation pipelines is a fresh context per item; when batching is unavoidable, balancing the history helps.

</details>


### [54] [Parametric Modular Answer Set Programs Made Declarative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22716)
*Jorge Fandinno,Yuliya Lierler,Torsten Schaub*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了参数化模块逻辑程序这一新形式化方法，用于实现一阶回答集编程中的模块化，能够捕获clingo程序中集体控制功能的语义，并建立了模块化ASP的理论基础及其与传统非模块化ASP的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一阶回答集编程（ASP）中的模块化概念，以支持子程序的参数化定义和内涵性声明，从而增强ASP程序的结构化能力和可重用性。

Method: 提出了一种名为参数化模块逻辑程序的新形式化方法，该方法允许定义带参数和内涵性声明的子程序，并通过理论分析展示其如何捕获clingo程序中集体控制功能的语义。

Result: 建立了模块化ASP的理论基础，展示了该形式化方法的有用性，并将其与传统非模块化ASP建立了联系。

Conclusion: 参数化模块逻辑程序为ASP提供了有效的模块化机制，能够支持子程序的参数化和结构化管理，同时保持了与传统ASP的兼容性。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore the concept of modularity in first-order answer set programming (ASP). We introduce a new formalism called parametric modular logic programs, which allows defining subprograms with parameters and intensionality statements. We demonstrate how this formalism can capture the semantics of clingo-programs with collective control, a feature that enables structuring and instantiating subprograms. We provide theoretical foundations for modular ASP, illustrate its usefulness, and connect to traditional non-modular ASP.

</details>


### [55] [Advancing Mathematics Research with AI-Driven Formal Proof Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22763)
*George Tsoukalas,Anton Kovsharov,Sergey Shirobokov,Anja Surina,Moritz Firsching,Gergely Bérczi,Francisco J. R. Ruiz,Arun Suggala,Adam Zsolt Wagner,Eric Wieser,Lei Yu,Aja Huang,Miklós Z. Horváth,Andrew Ferrauiolo,Henryk Michalewski,Codrut Grosu,Thomas Hubert,Matej Balog,Pushmeet Kohli,Swarat Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模评估了利用LLM生成Lean形式化证明来解决开放数学问题的能力。最强大的智能体以每问题数百美元的成本，自主解决了353个开放Erdős问题中的9个，证明了492个OEIS猜想中的44个，并正在组合数学、优化、图论、代数几何和量子光学研究中部署。基础智能体（交替LLM生成与Lean验证）复现了Erdős的成功，但在最难问题上成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在数学推理中表现出色，但不可靠性限制了其在数学研究中的应用。利用LLM生成Lean等语言的形式化证明是一种缓解方案，但该方法解决开放问题的能力尚未经过大规模评估。

Method: 使用最强大的智能体进行大规模形式化证明搜索，以及一个基础智能体（交替进行LLM生成和Lean验证），在353个开放Erdős问题和492个OEIS猜想上进行实验。

Result: 最强大智能体自主解决了9个开放Erdős问题（每问题成本数百美元），证明了44个OEIS猜想；基础智能体复现了Erdős成功但更难问题上成本更高。

Conclusion: AI辅助形式化证明搜索在解决开放数学问题方面具有强大能力，不同智能体设计对其效果和成本有显著影响。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly excel at mathematical reasoning, but their unreliability limits their utility in mathematics research. A mitigation is using LLMs to generate formal proofs in languages like Lean. We perform the first large-scale evaluation of this method's ability to solve open problems. Our most capable agent autonomously resolved 9 of 353 open Erdős problems at the per-problem cost of a few hundred dollars, proved 44/492 OEIS conjectures, and is being deployed in combinatorics, optimization, graph theory, algebraic geometry, and quantum optics research. A basic agent alternating LLM-based generation with Lean-based verification replicated the Erdős successes but proved costlier on the hardest problems. These findings demonstrate the power of AI-aided formal proof search and shed light on the agent designs that enable it.

</details>

### [56] [PhysX-Omni: Unified Simulation-Ready Physical 3D Generation for Rigid, Deformable, and Articulated Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21572)
*Ziang Cao,Yinghao Liu,Haitian Li,Runmao Yao,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Liang Pan,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysX-Omni，一个统一的仿真就绪物理3D生成框架，支持刚体、可变形体和铰接体等多种资产类型；同时构建了首个通用仿真就绪3D数据集PhysXVerse，并提出包含六项关键属性的PhysX-Bench评估基准。实验表明该方法在生成和理解方面均表现优异，并验证了其在场景生成和机器人策略学习中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D生成方法要么忽略物理属性，要么局限于单一资产类别（刚体、可变形体或铰接体），缺乏统一的仿真就绪3D生成框架。

Method: 1) 提出面向视觉语言模型的高效几何表示，直接编码高分辨率3D结构无需压缩；2) 构建PhysXVerse数据集，覆盖多样化的室内外类别；3) 设计PhysX-Bench基准，评估几何、绝对尺度、材质、可操作性、运动学和功能描述六个属性。

Result: 在常规指标和PhysX-Bench上，PhysX-Omni在生成和理解任务中均表现强劲；额外实验验证了其在仿真就绪场景生成和机器人策略学习中的潜力。

Conclusion: PhysX-Omni为仿真就绪物理3D生成提供了统一框架，有望推动具身AI和物理仿真等下游应用的广泛发展。

Abstract: Simulation-ready physical 3D assets have emerged as a promising direction owing to their broad applicability in downstream tasks. However, most existing 3D generation methods either neglect physical properties or are limited to a single asset category, e.g., rigid, deformable, or articulated objects. To address these limitations, we introduce PhysX-Omni, a unified framework for simulation-ready physical 3D generation across diverse asset types. Specifically, we develop a novel and efficient geometry representation tailored for Vision-Language Models, which directly encodes high-resolution 3D structures without compression, significantly improving generation performance. In addition, we construct the first general simulation-ready 3D dataset, PhysXVerse, covering diverse indoor and outdoor categories. Furthermore, to comprehensively and flexibly evaluate both generative and understanding capabilities in the wild, we propose PhysX-Bench, which encompasses six key attributes: geometry, absolute scale, material, affordance, kinematics, and function description. Extensive experiments with conventional metrics and PhysX-Bench show that PhysX-Omni performs strongly in both generation and understanding. Moreover, additional studies further validate the potential of PhysX-Omni for applications in simulation-ready scene generation and robotic policy learning. We believe PhysX-Omni can significantly advance a wide range of downstream applications, particularly in embodied AI and physics-based simulation.

</details>


### [57] [Lens: Rethinking Training Efficiency for Foundational Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21573)
*Dong Chen,Fangyun Wei,Ziyu Wan,Dongdong Chen,Jiawei Zhang,Jinjing Zhao,Sirui Zhang,Yang Yue,Zhiyang Liang,Baining Guo,Chong Luo,Jianmin Bao,Ji Li,Lei Shi,Qinhong Yang,Xiuyu Wu,Xuelu Feng,Yan Lu,Yanchen Dong,Yitong Wang,Yunuo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lens是一个3.8B参数的T2I模型，通过高信息密度训练数据、精心设计的架构、RL后训练、推理优化等策略，在多个基准上达到或超越6B+参数模型的表现，仅需约19.3%的训练算力，并在单卡H100上实现3.15秒生成1024²图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在以更小的模型规模和更低的训练计算成本，实现与大规模SOTA T2I模型竞争甚至超越的性能，提升文本到图像生成的训练效率和推理速度。

Method: (1) 训练数据层面：构建Lens-800M数据集，使用GPT-4.1生成平均109词的高密度描述；每个batch包含多分辨率、多样长宽比的图像以扩大视觉覆盖。(2) 架构层面：采用语义VAE提供更好的潜在表示，使用强语言编码器加速优化并实现从纯英文训练数据到多语言的泛化。(3) 后训练：RL with taxonomy-driven prompts (Lens-RL-8K) 和结构化奖励规则抑制伪影；reasoner模块通过无训练的系统提示搜索对齐用户需求；蒸馏加速实现4步推理。

Result: 在多个基准上达到或超越6B+参数SOTA模型，仅需约19.3%的训练算力；支持1:2到2:1任意长宽比及1440²分辨率；支持多语言提示；1024²图像生成仅需3.15秒（单卡H100），蒸馏版4步推理仅0.84秒。

Conclusion: 通过高信息密度数据、高效架构和系统优化，小模型可以在T2I任务中以远低于大模型的计算成本实现竞争性甚至更优的性能，验证了"数据质量与系统优化"路线是提升生成质量的有效途径。

Abstract: We introduce Lens, a 3.8B-parameter T2I model that achieves performance competitive with, and in several cases surpassing, state-of-the-art models with more than 6B parameters across various benchmarks, while requiring significantly less training compute. For example, Lens requires only about 19.3% of the training compute used by Z-Image. The training efficiency of Lens stems from two key strategies beyond its compact model size. First, we maximize data information density per training batch by (i) training on Lens-800M, a dataset of 800M densely captioned image-text pairs whose captions are generated by GPT-4.1 and contain approximately 109 words on average, providing richer semantic supervision than conventional short captions, and (ii) constructing each batch from images with multiple resolutions and diverse aspect ratios, thereby enlarging the effective visual coverage of each optimization step. Second, we improve convergence speed through careful architectural choices, including adopting a semantic VAE that provides better latent representations and employing a strong language encoder that accelerates optimization while enabling multilingual generalization from English-only training data. After pre-training, we apply RL with taxonomy-driven prompts (Lens-RL-8K) and structured reward rubrics to suppress artifacts and improve visual quality, a reasoner module with training-free system prompt search to better align user requests with the model, and distillation-based acceleration for 4-step inference. Through efficient training and systematic optimization, Lens generalizes to arbitrary aspect ratios from 1:2 to 2:1 and resolutions up to 1440^2, and supports prompts in several commonly used languages. Thanks to its compact size, Lens generates a 1024^2 image in 3.15 seconds on a single NVIDIA H100 GPU, while its distilled turbo version performs 4-step generation in 0.84 seconds.

</details>


### [58] [GenEvolve: Self-Evolving Image Generation Agents via Tool-Orchestrated Visual Experience Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21605)
*Sixiang Chen,Zhaohu Xing,Tian Ye,Xinyu Geng,Yunlong Lin,Jianyu Lai,Xuanhua He,Fuxiang Zhai,Jialin Gao,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenEvolve 是一个基于工具编排视觉经验蒸馏的自我进化框架，通过将每轮生成建模为工具编排轨迹，并利用视觉经验蒸馏提供密集的 token 级监督，使图像生成智能体能够自我进化并更有效地使用工具，在多项基准上达到 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式的图像生成已不再是简单的提示词到图像的问题，高质量生成需要智能体将模型的内部生成能力与外部资源相结合。随着请求变得更加多样化和具有挑战性，需要开发一个能够通过轨迹自我进化、更有效地使用工具的通用图像生成智能体。

Method: GenEvolve 将每次生成尝试建模为工具编排轨迹（tool-orchestrated trajectory），智能体在其中收集证据、选择参考、调用生成技能并组合成提示-参考程序。通过比较同一请求的多个轨迹，将最佳与最差差异抽象为结构化视觉经验，仅提供给特权教师分支。受在线策略自蒸馏启发，视觉经验蒸馏提供密集的 token 级监督，帮助学生智能体内化更好的搜索、知识激活、参考选择和提示构建能力。

Result: 构建了 GenEvolve-Data 和 GenEvolve-Bench。在公开基准和 GenEvolve-Bench 上的实验表明，该方法在现有图像生成框架中取得了实质性进展，达到了 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: GenEvolve 通过视觉经验蒸馏和工具编排轨迹，成功实现了图像生成智能体的自我进化，验证了从轨迹对比中提取结构化经验作为密集监督信号的有效性，为构建更强大的通用图像生成智能体提供了新范式。

Abstract: Open-ended image generation is no longer a simple prompt-to-image problem. High-quality generation often requires an agent to combine a model's internal generative ability with external resources. As requests become more diverse and demanding, we aim to develop a general image-generation agent that can self-evolve through trajectories and use tools more effectively across varied generation challenges. To this end, we propose GenEvolve, a self-evolving framework based on Tool-Orchestrated Visual Experience Distillation. In GenEvolve, each generation attempt is modeled as a tool-orchestrated trajectory, where the agent gathers evidence, selects references, invokes generation skills, and composes them into a prompt-reference program. Unlike existing agentic generation methods that mainly rely on image-level scalar rewards, GenEvolve compares multiple trajectories for the same request and abstracts best-worst differences into structured visual experience, provided only to a privileged teacher branch. Inspired by on-policy self-distillation, Visual Experience Distillation provides dense token-level supervision, helping the student internalize better search, knowledge activation, reference selection, and prompt construction. We further construct GenEvolve-Data and GenEvolve-Bench. Experiments on public benchmarks and GenEvolve-Bench show substantial gains over strong baselines, achieving state-of-the-art performance among current image-generation frameworks. Our website is as follows: https://ephemeral182.github.io/GenEvolve/

</details>


### [59] [UniVL: Unified Vision-Language Embedding for Spatially Grounded Contextual Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21611)
*Jiayun Wang,Yu Wang,Weijie Gan,Zhenting Wang,Wei Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniVL，一种空间约束的上下文图像生成框架，通过将文本指令直接渲染到空间掩码上形成统一视觉输入，取代了传统方法中独立的文本编码器。该方法采用OCR预训练骨干读取统一条件，生成融合视觉语义与空间位置的UniVL嵌入，以两阶段流程对齐VAE并条件化扩散模型。在构建的477K图像基准上，UniVL将FID从14降至11，PSNR从16提升至20，同时消除文本编码器，减少52%推理TFLOPs和44%运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控图像生成方法通常依赖独立的视觉编码器和文本编码器分别处理参考图像和全局文本提示，导致计算开销大，且难以将语义精确绑定到具体空间位置。本文旨在消除对独立文本编码器的依赖，实现空间约束的图像生成，并大幅降低推理计算量。

Method: 1) 提出空间约束上下文图像生成任务，将文本指令渲染到空间掩码上形成统一视觉输入；2) 采用OCR预训练的UniVL编码器读取统一条件，生成融合视觉语义与空间位置的嵌入序列fVIL；3) 两阶段流程：先对齐UniVL与VAE嵌入空间，再以UniVL嵌入条件化预训练扩散骨干；4) 构建UniVL-ImgGen基准数据集（477K张掩码标注图像）用于训练和评估。

Result: 在UniVL-ImgGen基准上，UniVL相比文本提示基线：FID从14降至11，PSNR从16提升至20；完全消除文本编码器，推理TFLOPs降低52%，运行时间减少44%。

Conclusion: UniVL通过统一视觉条件输入替代独立文本编码器，在提升图像质量的同时显著降低了计算开销，为高效、空间可控的图像生成提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: We introduce spatially grounded contextual image generation, a controllable image generation task that reframes the conditioning paradigm. Instead of supplying a reference image and a global text prompt through two separate encoders, one for vision and one for language, UniVL is trained to bind semantics to spatial locations directly from a single unified visual input, where the textual instruction is rendered onto the spatial mask. This removes the need for a standalone text encoder at inference time. The resulting model supports contextual image generation by following user-specified instructions about what should appear where, while substantially reducing computation.
  To address this task, we propose a framework in which the UniVL encoder, adapted from an optical-character-recognition-pretrained backbone, reads the unified condition optically and produces a UniVL embedding, fVIL, that fuses visual and semantic intent with spatial locations in a single token sequence. A two-stage pipeline first aligns UniVL with the VAE embedding space and then conditions a pretrained diffusion backbone entirely on UniVL embeddings, eliminating the standalone text encoder, such as T5. Although this reframing uses a deliberately minimal text interface, it yields strong empirical gains. On UniVL-ImgGen, a benchmark of 477K mask-annotated images that we construct for training and evaluation, UniVL improves image quality over text-prompted baselines, reducing FID from 14 to 11 and increasing PSNR from 16 to 20. It also eliminates the text encoder entirely, reducing inference TFLOPs by up to 52% and runtime by up to 44%. Additional ablation studies validate the contributions of the proposed components, paving the way for efficient, spatially grounded image generation with a unified conditioning paradigm.

</details>


### [60] [Flat-Pack Bench: Evaluating Spatio-Temporal Understanding in Large Vision-Language Models through Furniture Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21625)
*Aditya Chetan,Eric Cai,Peeyush Kushwaha,Bharath Raj Nagoor Kani,Utkarsh Mall,Qianqian Wang,Noah Snavely,Bharath Hariharan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Flat-Pack Bench基准，用于评估大型视觉语言模型在细粒度时空推理（如家具组装任务中的动作时序、状态定位、部件匹配与追踪）上的能力，实验表明现有SOTA LVLMs在这些任务上表现不佳，暴露了其在时间信息利用、物体追踪和空间交互理解方面的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解基准主要集中在粗粒度任务（动作分割、分类、描述、检索），且依赖易于语言识别的实体（家用物品、动物、人等），无法充分评估模型在复杂真实场景中所需的细粒度时空理解能力，如家具组装、烹饪等需要逐步精确理解的应用。

Method: 提出了Flat-Pack Bench基准，以家具组装任务为核心，通过多项选择题搭配视觉提示（突出相关部件）作为参考，评估LVLMs在动作时序排序、组装状态时序定位、部件匹配理解与部件追踪四个细粒度任务上的表现。

Result: 实验表明，当前最先进的LVLMs在细粒度时空推理任务上表现困难，尤其是在有效利用视频中的时间信息、物体追踪能力以及理解物理接触等空间交互方面存在显著局限。

Conclusion: 现有LVLMs在处理需要精细时空理解的视频任务上能力不足，Flat-Pack Bench为评估和推动这一方向的进展提供了有价值的基准。

Abstract: The emergence of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) has significantly advanced video understanding capabilities. However, existing benchmarks focus predominantly on coarse-grained tasks such as action segmentation, classification, captioning, and retrieval. Furthermore, these benchmarks often rely on entities that can be easily identified verbally, like household objects, animals, human subjects, etc., limiting their applicability to complex, in-the-wild video scenarios. But, many applications such as furniture assembly, cooking, etc., require step-by-step fine-grained spatio-temporal understanding of the video, which is not sufficiently evaluated in current benchmarks. To address this gap, we introduce Flat-Pack Bench, a novel benchmark centered on furniture assembly tasks. Our benchmark evaluates LVLMs on nuanced tasks, including temporal ordering of assembly actions, temporal localization of assembly state, understanding part mating, and tracking, using multiple-choice questions paired with visual prompts highlighting relevant parts as references for fine-grained questions. Our experiments reveal that state-of-the-art LVLMs struggle significantly with fine-grained spatio-temporal reasoning, highlighting their limitations in effectively leveraging temporal information from videos, limited tracking ability, and understanding of spatial interactions like physical contact.

</details>


### [61] [Look-Closer-Then-Diagnose: Confidence-Aware Ultrasound VQA via Active Zooming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21652)
*Yue Zhou,Erxuan Wu,Yikang Sun,Hongjoo Lee,Yuan Bi,Huixiong Xu,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模拟超声医生认知工作流的VLM框架，通过"缩放-诊断"范式和基于不确定性的奖励机制，在超声病变定位任务上提升了39.3%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在超声领域表现不佳，原因在于：1）缺乏交互式放大病变区域的能力；2）将标注视为无偏金标准，忽略其固有的主观性和歧义性。

Method: 1）提出结构化Zoom-then-Diagnose范式，模拟超声医生的交互式搜索过程，实现病灶聚焦推理；2）在GRPO框架中引入基于随机分组 rollout 的不确定性感知奖励，通过预测一致性估计模型置信度，鼓励模型对清晰案例强化准确预测，对歧义案例保持谨慎。

Result: 在肝脏、乳腺和甲状腺数据集上的实验表明，病变定位性能提升了39.3%。

Conclusion: 模型学会了主动"靠近观察再诊断"的能力，验证了模拟超声医生认知工作流对提升VLM在超声领域表现的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have significantly advanced medical visual question answering, yet their performance in ultrasound remains suboptimal. In clinical practice, sonographers explicitly focus on lesion regions to formulate reports, though diagnostic interpretations sometimes vary due to inherent subjectivity. However, existing VLMs are not explicitly structured to interactively zoom into lesions prior to diagnosis; moreover, they typically treat annotations as unbiased ground truths, failing to account for their inherent subjectivity and ambiguity. In this paper, we propose a framework specifically designed to consider the sonographer's cognitive workflow. We first introduce a structured Zoom-then-Diagnose paradigm, which replicates the interactive search process to enable lesion-focused reasoning. Furthermore, within the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) framework, we introduce an uncertainty-aware reward derived from stochastic group-wise rollouts to estimate prediction consistency as a proxy for model confidence. Together, these two components encourage the model to reinforce accurate predictions on clear cases while remaining cautious under ambiguity. Experiments across liver, breast, and thyroid datasets show that our framework improves lesion localization by 39.3\%, demonstrating that our model has learned the ability to actively look closer and diagnose.

</details>


### [62] [MRecover: A Conditional Generative Model for Recovering Motion-Corrupted MR images Using AI Generated Contrast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21669)
*Jinghang Li,Tales Santini,Courtney Clark,Bruno de Almeida,Cong Chu,Salem Alkhateeb,Andrea Sajewski,Jacob Berardinelli,Hecheng Jin,Tobias Campos,Jeremy J. Berardo,Joseph Mettenburg,Ariel Gildengers,Howard J. Aizenstein,Minjie Wu,Tamer S. Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MRecover，一种基于条件生成模型的方法，利用T1w图像合成高分辨率T2w TSE图像，用于海马亚区分割。在7T数据上训练（n=577），在3T数据上良好泛化（n=416, r=0.87-0.97），在ADNI3运动伪影数据中增加了31.8%的可分析样本（593 vs 450），并提升了效应量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海马亚区分割依赖于高分辨率T2w TSE MRI，该序列易受运动伪影影响，导致大量数据丢失，亟需一种能恢复或合成可用TSE图像的方法。

Method: 开发了一个条件生成模型MRecover，利用常规采集的T1w图像合成TSE图像，采用自回归切片条件化策略以保持体积一致性。模型在7T MRI数据（n=577）上训练。

Result: 在域内7T数据上达到高保真度（n=148, SSIM=0.84, FSIM=0.94）；在域外3T数据上，合成与真实采集的亚区体积高度相关（n=416, r=0.87-0.97）；在ADNI3数据中，经质量控制后可分析受试者从450人增至593人（提升31.8%）；合成图像因样本量增加带来了更大的效应量（全海马ε²=0.121-0.100 vs 0.086-0.062）。

Conclusion: MRecover能够有效从T1w图像合成高质量T2w TSE图像，显著减少因运动伪影导致的数据损失，并提升后续海马亚区萎缩分析的统计效力。

Abstract: Hippocampal subfield segmentation requires high-resolution T2w turbo spin echo (TSE) MRI, yet this sequence is susceptible to motion artifacts, leading to substantial data loss. We developed a conditional generative model (MRecover) that synthesizes routinely acquired T1w images to create TSE images with autoregressive slice conditioning for volumetric consistency. Trained on 7T MRI data (n=577), the model achieved high in-domain fidelity (n=148, SSIM=0.84, FSIM=0.94) and generalized well to out-of-domain 3T data: subfield volumes from synthesized and the as-acquired images closely matched: (n=416, r=0.87-0.97) and yielded 31.8% more analyzable subjects in the motion-affected ADNI3 dataset after quality control (593 vs 450). The synthesized images also achieved larger effect sizes due to increasing the sample size for diagnostic group differences in hippocampal subfield atrophy (whole hippocampus $ε^2$= 0.121-0.100 vs. 0.086-0.062, left-right hemispheres). Project page: https://jinghangli98.github.io/MRecover/

</details>


### [63] [AVI-HT: Adaptive Vision-IMU Fusion for 3D Hand Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21714)
*Ziyi Kou,Ankit Kumar,Mia Huang,Taylor Niehues,Vatsal Mehta,Ergys Ristani,Li Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AVI-HT，一种自适应视觉-IMU融合方法，通过联合建模第一人称视角图像与手套上的6自由度IMU信号来追踪3D手部姿态。在严重视觉遮挡的手物交互场景中，该方法相比基线将关键点误差降低16.1%，手腕对齐变体降低24.2%。其成功依赖于同步的多模态训练数据和跨传感器深度注意力机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D手部姿态追踪方法在视觉严重遮挡的手物交互场景中准确性和可用性不足，需要一种能够自适应融合视觉和IMU信号的鲁棒方法。

Method: (1) 构建DexGloveHOI数据集，包含10万+对同步视觉-IMU样本及3D标注姿态；(2) 提出跨传感器深度注意力机制，自适应调节视觉与各IMU传感器的信任权重；(3) 支持UmeTrack和MANO两种手部模型。

Result: 在DexGloveHOI数据集上，AVI-HT相比基线方法将平均关键点误差降低16.1%，手腕对齐变体降低24.2%。消融实验揭示了每根手指IMU传感器在不同活动类型中的贡献，以及模型对IMU噪声和视觉-IMU时间错位的敏感性。

Conclusion: AVI-HT通过自适应视觉-IMU融合显著提升了3D手部姿态追踪在遮挡场景下的性能，验证了跨传感器注意力机制和同步多模态数据训练的有效性。

Abstract: We present AVI-HT, an adaptive visual-IMU fusion approach for tracking 3D hand poses by jointly modeling the egocentric image with on-glove 6-DoF IMU signals. AVI-HT achieves significantly improved accuracy and availability, particularly in hand-object interaction (HOI) scenarios involving heavy visual occlusion. Two complementary ingredients underpin its success: (1) synchronized multi-modal training data pairing on-body vision-IMU sensor streams with ground-truth 3D hand poses from a motion-capture system, and (2) a cross-sensor deep attention mechanism that adaptively modulates the trust assigned to the vision and individual IMU sensors. To evaluate AVI-HT in real-world settings, we conduct extensive experiments on our DexGloveHOI dataset that consists of 100K+ pairwise vision-IMU samples with synchronized 3D annotated poses, in which users manipulate a variety of objects during daily tasks. We compare against multiple single- and multi-modal tracking approaches under two hand models (UmeTrack, MANO). The results show that AVI-HT reduces mean keypoint error by 16.1% and its wrist-aligned variant by 24.2% over the baselines. Ablation studies further reveal the per-finger contribution of IMU sensors across activity types, and the model's sensitivity to IMU noise and temporal misalignment in vision-IMU fusion.

</details>


### [64] [BEiTScore: Reference-free Image Captioning Evaluation with an Efficient Cross-Encoder Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21728)
*Gonçalo Gomes,Bruno Martins,Chrysoula Zerva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于轻量级交叉编码器的图像描述评估指标，从VQA模型初始化，结合对抗性LLM数据增强，在保持计算效率的同时实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像描述评估指标存在两难困境：基于LLM的评估指标计算成本高昂，而基于CLIP的编码器受限于token长度、缺乏细粒度敏感性和组合泛化能力（将描述视为"词袋"）。

Method: 1）从视觉问答模型检查点初始化的轻量级交叉编码器；2）精心组装的有监督训练数据混合，包含基于LLM的对抗性数据增强以增强对细粒度视觉-语言错误的敏感度；3）引入新的基准测试用于评估多样化场景下的详细描述。

Result: 所提出的指标在多个评估场景中达到SOTA性能，同时保持大规模基准测试、质量感知解码或奖励引导所需的计算效率。

Conclusion: 通过VQA模型初始化与对抗性数据增强，成功在评估质量与计算效率之间取得平衡，为图像描述评估提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Image captioning evaluation remains a significant challenge, as vision-language models evolve toward more challenging capabilities such as generating long-form and context-rich descriptions. State-of-the-art evaluation metrics involve extensive computational costs associated with the use of Large Language Models (LLMs) as judges, or instead suffer from the limitations of standard CLIP-based encoders, such as strict token limits, lack of fine-grained sensitivity, or lack of compositional generalization by treating captions as ``bags-of-words.'' We propose a new learned metric that tackles the aforementioned challenges, based on a lightweight cross-encoder that is initialized from a visual question-answering model checkpoint, balancing a strong weight initialization with computational efficiency. Our training scheme uses a carefully assembled data mixture for supervised learning, featuring adversarial LLM-based data augmentations to enhance model sensitivity to fine-grained visual-linguistic errors. We also introduce a new benchmark designed to assess detailed captioning evaluation across diverse scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed metric achieves state-of-the-art performance while maintaining the efficiency required for large-scale benchmarking, quality-aware decoding, or reward guidance.

</details>


### [65] [Improving 3D Labeling in Self-Driving by Inferring Vehicle Information using Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21747)
*Steven Chen,Shivesh Khaitan,Nemanja Djuric*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用视觉语言模型（VLM）进行零样本车辆信息推理，以改进自动驾驶中3D车辆标注的质量与效率，实验表明该方法能减少人工标注时间并提升标注质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶场景中3D车辆标注依赖大量人工且容易出错（尤其是遮挡情况），现有方法效率低、质量不稳定，亟需一种能自动推断车辆信息并生成精确3D边界框的方法来提升标注流程。

Method: 利用视觉语言模型（VLM）从图像裁剪区域中零样本推理车辆的品牌、型号和代际，同时输出准确的3D边界框尺寸以辅助人工标注；通过迭代提示工程优化推理效果，并比较不同VLM的表现。

Result: 与强基线相比，该方法不仅准确率高，还能有效缓解特定失效模式（如严重遮挡时VLM比LiDAR辅助的人工标注提供更优的3D尺寸）；在公开和私有数据集上的实验证明结论具有可泛化性。

Conclusion: 将VLM集成到标注流程中可以减少人工标注时间并提高标注质量。

Abstract: We present an approach to improve 3D vehicle labeling in self-driving applications through zero-shot inference of vehicle information, leveraging Vehicle Make and Model Recognition (VMMR) methods. The proposed approach utilizes a Vision Language Model (VLM) to both infer a vehicle's make, model, and generation from image crops, and output accurate 3D bounding box dimensions to seed manual labeling. We evaluate the impact of iterative prompt engineering and the choice of different VLMs on both vehicle bounding box inference and make/model/generation recognition. When compared to strong baselines, the proposed approach not only shows high accuracy, but also excels in mitigating specific failure modes where VLMs provide better dimensions than initial lidar-aided human annotated labels (e.g., in cases of significant vehicle occlusion). Experiments on both public and proprietary data strongly suggest that our conclusions are generalizable across different labelers and datasets. The results demonstrate that integrating VLMs into the labeling process can reduce manual labeling time while increasing label quality.

</details>


### [66] [BodyReLux: Temporally Consistent Full-Body Video Relighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21766)
*Li Ma,Mingming He,Xueming Yu,David M. George,Ahmet Levent Taşel,Paul Debevec,Julien Philip*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BodyReLux是一个基于视频扩散的主体特定框架，用于对全身人体表演进行时间一致的重打光。该模型在由传统静态OLAT捕捉和新型动态性能捕捉构成的混合数据集上训练，利用文本到视频模型的生成先验，并通过光源token化表示和掩码注意力实现精确的动态光照控制，最终生成逼真且时间一致的重打光视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体表演的重打光是后期制作和内容创作中的基本任务，现有方法在动态场景下的时间一致性和光照控制精度方面存在不足。

Method: 1) 构建混合数据集：结合传统静态OLAT捕捉和新型动态性能捕捉（快速交替两个平滑变化的光照序列，利用闪烁融合阈值避免频闪）；2) 基于预训练文本到视频模型进行微调，利用生成先验；3) 提出光源token化的光照条件方法，每个光源表示为一个token；4) 采用掩码注意力机制支持动态光照控制；5) 设计数据增强流程。

Result: 实现了逼真、鲁棒且时间一致的视频重打光效果，能够对特定主体的人体表演进行动态光照控制。

Conclusion: BodyReLux通过混合数据集构建、光源token化条件方法和掩码注意力机制，有效解决了动态人体表演视频重打光的时间一致性和光照控制精度问题。

Abstract: Being able to relight human performance is a fundamental task for post production and content creation. We present BodyReLux, a subject-specific video diffusion-based framework for relighting full-body human performances in a temporally consistent way. Our model is trained on a hybrid dataset of pixel-aligned video relighting pairs, covering a diverse combination of lighting conditions, performances and viewpoints. To acquire such dataset, we combine traditional static One-Light-at-a-Time (OLAT) capture and a novel dynamic performance capture in which two smoothly varying lighting sequences are rapidly interleaved. Because the lighting operates above the human flicker-fusion threshold, the interleaving does not appear to strobe. We train our video relighting model from a pretrained text-to-video model to fully leverage the generative priors for producing high quality videos. To achieve accurate lighting control, we introduce a new lighting conditioning method that represents each light source as a token. We further condition on sequences of lighting using masked attention to support dynamic lighting control. Together with a carefully designed data augmentation pipeline, we achieve photorealistic, robust, and temporally consistent video relighting of subject-specific human performances.

</details>


### [67] [SceneGraphGrounder: Zero-Shot 3D Visual Grounding via Structured Scene Graph Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21788)
*Xuefei Sun,Xujia Zhang,Brendan Crowe,Doncey Albin,Christoffer Heckman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneGraphGrounder提出了一种基于结构化图匹配的零样本3D视觉定位框架，通过构建3D场景图并实现查询图与场景图的约束对齐，实现多视图一致和可解释的空间推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的零样本3D视觉定位方法依赖视图相关推理或隐式表示，缺乏空间一致性和可解释性，难以处理组合性查询。

Method: 提出视觉标记提示策略从2D视图推断物体间关系，构建持久的3D场景图编码空间和语义关系；将查询构建为查询图，通过约束对齐实现与场景图的匹配。

Result: 在ScanRefer基准上取得与零样本方法相比有竞争力的性能，仅使用RGB-D输入；在真实移动机器人上验证了长时域物理环境中的鲁棒空间推理能力。

Conclusion: 基于图匹配的显式3D场景图表示能够有效提升零样本3D视觉定位的空间一致性和可解释性，并具备真实世界部署潜力。

Abstract: Zero-shot 3D visual grounding requires localizing objects in unstructured environments from free-form natural language. Recent vision-language model (VLM) approaches achieve promising results but rely on view-dependent reasoning or implicit representations, limiting spatial consistency and interpretability for compositional queries. We propose SceneGraphGrounder, a framework that reformulates 3D grounding as structured graph matching over a reconstructed 3D scene graph. To enable this formulation, we introduce a visual marker prompting strategy that enables a VLM to infer object-object relationships from 2D views, which are subsequently lifted into a persistent 3D scene graph encoding both spatial and semantic relations. Given a query, we construct a query graph and perform constrained alignment with the scene graph, ensuring multi-view consistency and interpretable reasoning. Experiments on the ScanRefer benchmark demonstrate that our method achieves competitive performance among zero-shot approaches, using only RGB-D inputs. We further validate our framework through real-world deployment on a mobile robot, demonstrating robust spatial reasoning in long-horizon physical environments. We will make our code publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [68] [MM-Conv: A Multimodal Dataset and Benchmark for Context-Aware Grounding in 3D Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21796)
*Anna Deichler,Jim O'Regan,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Lubos Marcinek,Anna Klezovich,Iolanda Leite,Jonas Beskow*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于动态3D环境的参考通信基准（含6.7小时VR交互数据）和一个两阶段接地流水线（先消解会话歧义再视觉定位），实验表明该解耦方法比端到端方法在会话接地任务上更有效，平均性能提升11-22个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在静态图像任务上表现优异，但在动态、多轮对话中难以消解歧义表达，缺乏面向会话参考通信的基准和有效方法。

Method: 构建了包含6.7小时第一人称VR交互数据（同步语音、动作、视线、3D场景几何）的基准，含4,200+人工校验的参考表达（完整/部分/代词三类）；提出两阶段接地流水线：先通过上下文重写消解会话歧义，再进行视觉定位。

Result: 上下文重写方法平均提升接地性能11-22个百分点；使用GroundingDINO在代词类表达上达56.7%，近乎最佳端到端基线的两倍。

Conclusion: 将语言推理与视觉感知解耦比端到端方法在会话接地任务上更有效。

Abstract: Grounding language in the physical world requires AI systems to interpret references that emerge dynamically during conversation. While current vision-language models (VLMs) excel at static image tasks, they struggle to resolve ambiguous expressions in spontaneous, multi-turn dialogue. We address this gap by introducing (1) a benchmark for referential communication in dynamic 3D environments, built from 6.7 hours of egocentric VR interaction with synchronized speech, motion, gaze, and 3D scene geometry, and (2) a two-stage grounding pipeline that explicitly resolves conversational ambiguity before visual localization. The benchmark includes over 4,200 manually verified referring expressions spanning full, partitive, and pronominal types. Our contextual rewriting approach improves grounding performance by 11-22 percentage points on average, with a pure detector (GroundingDINO) reaching 56.7% on pronominals after rewriting, nearly double the best end-to-end baseline. Results demonstrate that decoupling linguistic reasoning from visual perception is more effective than end-to-end approaches for conversational grounding.

</details>


### [69] [Seizure-Semiology-Suite (S3): A Clinically Multimodal Dataset, Benchmark, and Models for Seizure Semiology Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21852)
*Lina Zhang,Tonmoy Monsoor,Peizheng Li,Jiarui Cui,Xinyi Peng,Chong Han,Prateik Sinha,Siyuan Dai,Jessica Nichole Pasqua,Colin M McCrimmon,Weiting Liu,Hailey Marie Miranda,Bing Hu,Xiangting Wu,Tengyou Xu,Chunhan Li,Jiaye Tian,Jiarui Tang,Detao Ma,Lingye Kong,Junnan Lyu,Jungang Li,Yan Zan,Junhua Huang,Rajarshi Mazumder,Vwani Roychowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Seizure-Semiology-Suite，一个包含438个癫痫发作视频、超过35,000个密集标签的数据集和基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在癫痫发作症状学理解方面的能力。通过七任务分层基准测试和报告质量指数（Seizure-RQI），系统揭示了MLLMs在侧别推理、时间定位、症状排序和临床报告准确性方面的系统性弱点。癫痫特定微调和两阶段神经符号框架显著提升了性能，其中癫痫与非癫痫分类F1得分达到0.96。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在通用视频理解方面表现出色，但其对无意识、时空演变的病理运动行为（如癫痫发作症状学）的解读能力尚未得到检验，缺乏相关基准数据集和评估体系。

Method: 构建了包含438个癫痫视频、35,000+密集标签、覆盖20个ILAE定义症状学特征的数据集；提出七任务分层基准（从低级视觉感知到时间序列、叙事报告生成和癫痫诊断）；引入Seizure-RQI报告质量指数；对11个开源MLLMs进行基线评估；采用癫痫特定微调和两阶段神经符号框架进行改进。

Result: 11个开源MLLMs在侧别推理、时间定位、症状排序和临床报告准确性方面存在系统性弱点；癫痫特定微调显著提升各任务性能；两阶段神经符号框架在癫痫与非癫痫分类上达到F1=0.96。

Conclusion: Seizure-Semiology-Suite为安全关键的医学视频理解中评估多模态模型建立了严格基准，推动临床可靠、领域自适应的多模态智能发展。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in general video understanding, their capacity to interpret involuntary, and spatio-temporally evolving pathologic motor behaviors such as seizure semiology remains largely untested. To address this gap, we introduce Seizure-Semiology-Suite, a clinically grounded dataset and benchmark for fine-grained, structured seizure semiology understanding. The dataset includes 438 seizure videos annotated with over 35,000 dense labels covering 20 ILAE-defined semiological features. Building on this dataset, we propose a seven-task hierarchical benchmark that systematically evaluates MLLMs from low-level visual perception to temporal sequencing, narrative report generation, and seizure diagnosis. To enable clinically meaningful evaluation of generated reports, we further introduce the Report Quality Index for Seizure Semiology (Seizure-RQI). Extensive baselines across 11 open-weight MLLMs reveal systematic weaknesses in laterality reasoning, temporal localization, symptom sequencing, and clinically faithful reporting. We show that seizure-specific fine-tuning substantially improves performance across tasks, and that a two-stage neuro-symbolic framework achieves an F1 score of 0.96 on epileptic versus non-epileptic seizure classification. Seizure-Semiology-Suite establishes a rigorous benchmark for evaluating multimodal models in safety-critical medical video understanding and guides the development of clinically reliable, domain-adaptive multimodal intelligence.

</details>


### [70] [Two-Stage Multimodal Framework for Emotion Mimicry Intensity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21869)
*Dinithi Dissanayake,Shaveen Silva,Ovindu Atukorala,Prasanth Sasikumar,Suranga Nanayakkara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于Hume-ABAW10情感模仿强度(EMI)挑战赛的分阶段多模态框架，融合文本、声学和视觉表示（可选运动分支），在验证集上平均皮尔逊相关系数达0.4722，测试集上以0.57的成绩获得第三名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对野外多模态视频片段中六种连续情感强度维度（钦佩、娱乐、决心、共情痛苦、兴奋、喜悦）的预测问题，现有方法在多模态融合和情感强度连续预测方面仍存在挑战，亟需一个实用且可复现的基线方案。

Method: 提出分阶段多模态框架：先独立训练各模态（文本、音频、视觉、可选运动）编码器，再通过轻量级回归器进行融合，采用模态Dropout和受控编码器自适应策略。

Result: 在扩大4:1划分的验证集上，文本-音频-视觉-运动四模态融合取得最佳性能，平均皮尔逊相关系数0.4722；测试集上以0.57获得挑战赛第三名。

Conclusion: 提出的多模态融合框架为EMI预测提供了一个实用且可复现的基线，运动分支虽增益有限但具有研究价值。

Abstract: We present our submission to the Hume-ABAW10 Emotional Mimicry Intensity (EMI) Challenge, which aims to predict six continuous emotion intensity dimensions: Admiration, Amusement, Determination, Empathic Pain, Excitement, and Joy, from in-the-wild multimodal video clips. We propose a staged multimodal framework that combines textual, acoustic, and visual representations, with an optional motion branch. Our approach first trains modality-specific encoders independently and then fuses their learned representations through a lightweight regressor with modality dropout and controlled encoder adaptation. Across our submitted systems, the best validation performance is obtained by the text--audio--vision--motion fusion model under the expanded 4:1 split, achieving an average Pearson correlation of 0.4722. Although the motion branch yields only very slight gains, its behavior can be interesting to study. Our team was placed third in the EMI challenge, achieving an average Pearson correlation of 0.57 for the test set. Overall, we provide a practical and reproducible baseline for EMI prediction.

</details>


### [71] [Universal CT Representations from Anatomy to Disease Phenotype through Agglomerative Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21906)
*Yuheng Li,Yuan Gao,Haoyu Dong,Yuxiang Lai,Shansong Wang,Mojtaba Safari,James E. Baciak,Xiaofeng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlexiCT是一个基于266,227个CT体素和56个公开数据集训练的CT基础模型家族，采用三阶段聚合持续预训练（2D轴向预训练、3D解剖预训练、报告引导语义对齐），在分割、分类、配准、视觉语言理解和临床检索五个下游任务中达到或超越以往任务特定方法，代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前CT-based AI模型碎片化严重，不同任务（分割、分类、配准、报告分析）需分别训练专用模型，缺乏统一的CT基础模型来捕获通用的CT表示。

Method: 使用三阶段聚合持续预训练：(1) 2D轴向预训练；(2) 3D解剖预训练；(3) 报告引导语义对齐。支持切片级、体素级和视觉-语言分析。

Result: 在五个下游任务家族（分割、分类、配准、视觉语言理解、临床检索）的多个基准测试中，达到或超越以往任务特定方法；嵌入空间沿肿瘤分期梯度组织，表明能捕获疾病表型相关影像特征。

Conclusion: FlexiCT证明了CT基础模型可以超越任务特定的碎片化范式，统一捕获CT影像中的通用表示，并能反映疾病表型特征，是CT表示学习领域的大规模公共资源。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) is a central to three-dimensional medical imaging, yet CT-based artificial intelligence remains fragmented across task-specific models for segmentation, classification, registration, and report analysis. Here we present FlexiCT, a family of CT foundation models trained by agglomerative continual pretraining on 266,227 CT volumes from 56 publicly available datasets, forming a large-scale public resource for CT representation learning. FlexiCT uses agglomerative pretraining across three stages: two-dimensional axial pretraining, three-dimensional anatomical pretraining and report-guided semantic alignment.
