Result: 实验表明CEDAR在重建-稀疏权衡上具有竞争力，同时产生的解释更具可解释性且与人类感知更一致。在CLIP-like架构中，单个坐标可用文本概念解释；在BLIP等生成模型中，可解码为自然语言描述。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言表示中的表观纠缠可以通过合适的基变换来解缠，无需过度完备的维度扩展。CEDAR证明了通过适当的坐标变换即可揭示嵌入的组合语义结构。

Abstract: Vision-language models learn powerful multimodal embeddings, yet their internal semantics remain opaque. While sparse autoencoders (SAEs) can extract interpretable features, they rely on expanding the representation dimension, which compromises the original geometry and introduces redundancy. We introduce CEDAR (Conceptual Embedding Disentanglement via Adaptive Rotation), a post-hoc method that reveals the compositional structure of pretrained embeddings without increasing dimensionality. By learning an invertible transformation with a top-$k$ sparsity bottleneck, CEDAR concentrates semantic information into axis-aligned disentangled coordinates. In CLIP-like architecture, individual coordinates can be interpreted with textual concepts, while for generative models such as BLIP, they can be decoded into natural language descriptions. Experiments demonstrate that CEDAR achieves a competitive reconstruction-sparsity trade-off while producing explanations that are more interpretable and better aligned with human perception. Our results suggest that the apparent entanglement in vision-language representations can be resolved through a suitable change of basis, eliminating the need for overcomplete expansions.

</details>


### [145] [Improving Viewpoint-Invariance and Temporal Consistency for Action Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22695)
*Yannick Porto,Renato Martins,Thomas Chalumeau,Cedric Demonceaux*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段动作检测方法，通过训练时增强虚拟视角提取运动特征，并利用基于选择性状态空间序列建模的视角不变多尺度时序编码器，有效提升了视角变化不变性和全局时序一致性，在PKU-MMD和BABEL数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于外观的视频检测方法在训练时视角多样性不足，而基于运动的方法难以建模连续运动窗口间的细粒度时序关系，导致在视角变化和时序一致性方面表现不佳。

Method: 第一阶段：从增强的虚拟视角中提取运动特征（仅在训练时使用）；第二阶段：引入基于选择性状态空间序列建模的视角不变多尺度时序编码器，跨视角和时间尺度聚合信息。

Result: 在PKU-MMD和BABEL基准测试的所有数据划分上，该方法显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该方法通过虚拟视角增强和状态空间时序建模，有效解决了动作检测中的视角变化和时序一致性问题，实现了优越性能。

Abstract: Viewpoint change invariance and action temporal consistency are critical aspects for the effective deployment of human action detection of untrimmed videos. Existing appearance-based video detection methods often struggle with limited viewpoint diversity during training, while motion-based detection approaches frequently fail to model fine-grained temporal relationships across consecutive motion windows. This paper introduces a novel two-stage action detection approach designed to improve both view-invariance and global temporal coherence properties. In the first stage, we extract motion features from augmented virtual viewpoints, solely used at training. Then, the second stage introduces a new view-invariant, multi-scale temporal encoder based on selective state-space sequence modelling to aggregate information across viewpoints and time scales. Experiments on PKU-MMD and BABEL benchmarks demonstrate that this approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in all considered splits. Code and trained models are available at: https://icb-vision-ai.github.io/HydraView-TAD

</details>


### [146] [WorldKV: Efficient World Memory with World Retrieval and Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22718)
*Jung Yi,Minjae Kim,Paul Hyunbin Cho,Wooseok Jang,Sangdoo Yun,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldKV是一个无需训练的视频扩散模型框架，通过World Retrieval（选择性检索已驱逐的KV-cache块）和World Compression（基于关键帧相似性的冗余token剪枝），在保持实时推理速度的同时，实现了与全KV-cache注意力相当的世界一致性，吞吐量提升约2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型虽能实现实时、动作条件的世界生成，但在维持持久世界一致性（重新访问之前视角时内容一致）方面存在困难：全KV-cache注意力能保持一致性但内存和计算成本随展开长度线性增长，破坏实时性；滑动窗口推理恢复吞吐量却丢失长期一致性。

Method: WorldKV包含两个组件：1) World Retrieval：将被驱逐的KV-cache块存储在GPU/CPU内存中，通过相机/动作对应关系选择性检索场景相关块，将其插回原生注意力窗口而无需重新编码；2) World Compression：通过关键帧与锚帧的键-键相似性剪枝每个块中的冗余token，将每块存储减半，在固定预算下容纳2倍以上的历史信息。

Result: 在Matrix-Game-2.0和LingBot-World-Fast上，WorldKV在全KV内存保真度上匹配或超越，吞吐量约为全KV注意力的2倍；且与记忆训练基线相比具有竞争力，无需任何微调。

Conclusion: WorldKV作为一种无需训练的训练-free框架，有效解决了自回归视频扩散模型中一致性与实时性之间的矛盾，通过检索和压缩机制在保持高吞吐量的同时维持了长期世界一致性。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models have enabled real-time, action-conditioned world generation. However, sustaining a persistent world, where revisiting a previously seen viewpoint yields consistent content, remains an open problem. Full KV-cache attention preserves this consistency but breaks real-time constraints: memory footprint and attention cost grow linearly with rollout length. Sliding window inference restores throughput but discards long-term consistency. We propose WorldKV, a training-free framework with two components: World Retrieval and World Compression. World Retrieval stores evicted KV-cache chunks in GPU/CPU memory and selectively retrieves scene-relevant chunks via camera/ action correspondence, inserting them back into the native attention window without re-encoding. World Compression prunes redundant tokens within each chunk via key-key similarity to an anchor frame, halving per-chunk storage to fit 2x more history under a fixed budget. On Matrix-Game-2.0 and LingBot- World-Fast, WorldKV matches or exceeds full-KV memory fidelity at roughly 2x the throughput, and is competitive with memory-trained baselines without any fine-tuning. Project Page: https://cvlab-kaist.github.io/WorldKV/

</details>


### [147] [Spectral Tail Auxiliary Learning for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22751)
*Xingyi Li,Jiahui Zhang,Yiheng Li,Yun Cao,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现生成图像在超高频尾部存在异常抬升（spectral tail uplift），并将其归因于生成模型中的非线性谐波累积。基于此提出STAL方法，通过频域辅助监督训练空间检测器，无需额外推理开销，在9个数据集上实现强泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成图像检测方法利用频域线索（如伪影、高频差异），但对频域中具体且重复出现的频谱规律性理解不足，亟需系统分析和有效利用。

Method: 系统分析真实与生成图像的一维径向对数功率谱，发现生成图像在超高频尾部偏离幂律衰减出现异常抬升；提出STAL（Spectral Tail Auxiliary Learning），通过尾感知频率教师将频谱尾线索迁移至空间检测器，推理时丢弃频域模块。

Result: 在9个公开数据集上，STAL在跨生成器、数据分布和真实场景下均表现出强泛化性与稳定性，且无推理开销。

Conclusion: 生成图像的频谱尾抬升可作为跨生成架构的结构性检测线索，STAL框架有效利用该线索实现高效且泛化的AI生成图像检测。

Abstract: As generative image models evolve rapidly, the perceptual gap between generated and real images continues to narrow, making AI-generated image detection increasingly challenging. Many existing methods exploit frequency-domain cues for detection, typically described as frequency-domain artifacts or high-frequency discrepancies. However, the specific and recurring spectral regularities remain insufficiently understood and characterized. In this paper, we systematically analyze the one-dimensional radial log-power spectra of real and generated images. We find that generated images do not necessarily exhibit higher or lower energy across the entire spectrum or high-band range. Instead, their spectra deviate from the power-law decay and show an anomalous uplift in the ultra-high-frequency tail. We term this phenomenon spectral tail uplift. We further attribute this phenomenon to nonlinear harmonic accumulation in trained generative models, suggesting that it can serve as a structural cue across generative architectures. Based on this observation, we propose Spectral Tail Auxiliary Learning (STAL), a frequency-domain auxiliary supervision framework for generalizable AI-generated image detection. STAL transfers spectral-tail cues from a tail-aware frequency teacher to a spatial detector during training, while all frequency-domain modules are discarded at inference time. Consequently, STAL introduces no inference overhead. Extensive experiments on 9 public datasets show that STAL achieves strong generalization and stability across generators, data distributions, and real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [148] [DecQ: Detail-Condensing Queries for Enhanced Reconstruction and Generation in Representation Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22777)
*Tianhang Wang,Yitong Chen,Wei Song,Zuxuan Wu,Min Li,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DecQ通过轻量级细节浓缩查询（detail-condensing queries）从冻结的视觉基础模型中间特征中提取细粒度信息，在不破坏预训练语义空间的前提下，有效缓解了表征自编码器中重建与生成的权衡问题，在仅增加3.9%计算量的情况下将PSNR从19.13 dB提升至22.76 dB，并实现3.3倍更快的生成收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表征自编码器（RAE）冻结视觉基础模型（VFM）作为分词器编码器，虽能提供鲁棒的高层表征以促进快速收敛和高质量生成，但冻结VFM固化了空间重建能力，限制了细粒度生成和图像编辑；而引入重建导向的微调信号又会破坏预训练语义空间并降低生成保真度。为克服这一权衡困境，需要一种既能保持冻结语义空间又能提升重建质量的方案。

Method: 提出DecQ框架，核心是引入轻量级细节浓缩查询（detail-condensing queries），通过浓缩器模块从VFM的浅层和深层中间特征中提取细粒度信息，将这些查询注入解码器以支持重建，并在生成建模阶段与patch tokens联合生成。通过聚合多层级信息，DecQ有效缓解了重建与生成之间的权衡。

Result: (1) 仅增加8个查询和3.9%额外计算量，将基于冻结DINOv2的RAE的PSNR从19.13 dB提升至22.76 dB；(2) 生成建模方面，DecQ比RAE实现3.3倍更快的收敛速度，无引导FID达1.41，有引导FID达1.05。

Conclusion: DecQ以极小的计算开销同时提升了重建质量和生成性能，有效解决了RAE中冻结VFM带来的重建-生成权衡问题，为基于冻结视觉基础模型的潜在扩散模型提供了一种简单而有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Representation Autoencoders (RAEs) leverage frozen vision foundation models (VFMs) as tokenizer encoders, providing robust high-level representations that facilitate fast convergence and high-quality generation in latent diffusion models. However, freezing the VFM inherently constrains its spatial reconstruction capacity, limiting fine-grained generation and image editing; in contrast, incorporating reconstruction-oriented signals via fine-tuning disrupts the pretrained semantic space and degrades generative fidelity. To address this trade-off, we propose DecQ, a simple yet effective framework for RAEs. Specifically, DecQ introduces lightweight detail-condensing queries that extract fine-grained information from intermediate VFM features through condenser modules. These queries are incorporated into the decoder to support reconstruction and are jointly generated with patch tokens during generative modeling. By aggregating information from both shallow and deep layers, DecQ effectively mitigates the reconstruction--generation trade-off, improving both reconstruction quality and generative performance. Our experiments demonstrate that: (1) with only 8 additional queries and 3.9% extra computation, DecQ improves reconstruction over the frozen DINOv2-based RAE, increasing PSNR from 19.13 dB to 22.76 dB; and (2) for generative modeling, DecQ achieves 3.3$\times$ faster convergence than RAE, attaining an FID of 1.41 without guidance and 1.05 with guidance.

</details>


### [149] [Sensor2Sensor: Cross-Embodiment Sensor Conversion for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22809)
*Jiahao Wang,Bo Sun,Yijing Bai,Vincent Casser,Songyou Peng,Zehao Zhu,Meng-Li Shih,Xander Masotto,Shih-Yang Su,Kanaad V Parvate,Tiancheng Ge,Linn Bieske,Dragomir Anguelov,Mingxing Tan,Chiyu Max Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Sensor2Sensor框架，利用生成式模型将非结构化的行车记录仪单目视频转换为高保真的多模态自动驾驶传感器数据（多视角相机图像+激光雷达点云），解决了自动驾驶系统训练数据缺乏多样性和规模的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统需要大规模、多样化的数据集进行鲁棒训练与验证，但专有车队数据在规模、传感器配置多样性以及地理和长尾行为覆盖方面存在局限；而海量的行车记录仪视频数据虽然丰富多样，却与需要结构化多模态输入的自动驾驶系统不兼容。

Method: 首先利用4D高斯泼溅（4DGS）对真实AV日志进行重建和新视角渲染，将其转换为行车记录仪风格的视频，从而构建配对训练数据；然后采用扩散架构实现从单目视频到多模态传感器数据的生成转换。

Result: 通过定量评估验证了生成传感器数据的保真度和真实性，并成功将互联网和行车记录仪中的挑战性视频转换为逼真的多模态数据格式。

Conclusion: Sensor2Sensor有效弥合了真实世界非结构化视频与自动驾驶系统所需结构化数据之间的鸿沟，为自动驾驶开发解锁了海量外部数据源。

Abstract: Robust training and validation of Autonomous Driving Systems (ADS) require massive, diverse datasets. Proprietary data collected by Autonomous Vehicle (AV) fleets, while high-fidelity, are limited in scale, diversity of sensor configurations, as well as geographic and long-tail-behavioral coverage. In contrast, in-the-wild data from sources like dashcams offers immense scale and diversity, capturing critical long-tail scenarios and novel environments. However, this unstructured, in-the-wild video data is incompatible with ADS expecting structured, multi-modal sensor inputs for validation and training. To bridge this data gap, we propose Sensor2Sensor, a novel generative modeling paradigm that translates in-the-wild monocular dashcam videos into a high-fidelity, multi-modal sensor suite (AV logs) comprising multi-view camera images and LiDAR point clouds. A core challenge is the lack of paired training data. We address this by converting real AV logs into dashcam-style videos via 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) reconstruction and novel-view rendering. Sensor2Sensor then utilizes a diffusion architecture to perform the generative conversion. We perform comprehensive quantitative evaluations on the fidelity and realism of the generated sensor data. We demonstrate Sensor2Sensor's practical utility by converting challenging in-the-wild internet and dashcam footage into realistic, multi-modal data formats, further unlocking vast external data sources for AV development.

</details>


### [150] [MotiMotion: Motion-Controlled Video Generation with Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22818)
*Lee Hsin-Ying,Hanwen Jiang,Yiqun Mei,Jing Shi,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Zhixin Shu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotiMotion通过将运动控制重构为“推理-生成”问题，利用无训练的视觉语言推理器完善主轨迹并推测合理的次生运动，结合置信度感知的控制方案，生成了更自然、更符合常识的图像到视频内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动控制型图像到视频生成模型严格遵循用户提供的稀疏、不精确且因果不完整的轨迹，导致输出不自然或不可信，尤其是缺失了次生因果后果。

Method: 1) 提出MotiMotion框架，将运动控制重构为推理-生成问题；2) 使用无训练的视觉语言推理器完善主轨迹坐标并推测合理的次生运动；3) 设计置信度感知的控制方案，高置信度严格跟随，低置信度利用生成先验修正伪影。

Result: 在自建MotiBench基准上，基于VLM评估和人工研究均表明MotiMotion生成的视频具有更合理的物体行为和交互，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: MotiMotion通过引入因果推理和置信度感知控制，有效提升了运动驱动图像到视频生成的自然性与合理性。

Abstract: Current motion-controlled image-to-video generation models rigidly follow user-provided trajectories that are often sparse, imprecise, and causally incomplete. Such reliance often yields unnatural or implausible outcomes, especially by missing secondary causal consequences. To address this, we introduce MotiMotion, a novel framework that reformulates motion control as a reasoning-then-generation problem. To encourage causally grounded and commonsense-consistent interactions, we leverage a training-free vision-language reasoner to refine image-space coordinates of primary trajectories and to hallucinate plausible secondary motions. To further improve motion naturalness, we propose a confidence-aware control scheme that modulates guidance strength, enabling the model to closely follow high-confidence plans while correcting artifacts under low-confidence inputs with its internal generative priors. To support systematic evaluation, we curate a new image-to-video benchmark, MotiBench, consisting of interaction-centric scenes where new events are triggered by motion. Both VLM-based evaluation and a human study on MotiBench demonstrate that MotiMotion produces videos with more plausible object behaviors and interaction, and is preferred over existing approaches.

</details>


### [151] [Cambrian-P: Pose-Grounded Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22819)
*Jihan Yang,Zifan Zhao,Xichen Pan,Shusheng Yang,Junyi Zhang,Bingyi Kang,Hu Xu,Saining Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Cambrian-P，一种通过引入每帧可学习相机标记和位姿回归头来增强视频多模态大语言模型的方法。该方法在空间推理基准上提升4.5-6.5%，在8个额外基准上泛化良好，并实现了流式位姿估计SOTA。使用伪标注位姿训练还能提升通用视频QA，证明位姿信号超越空间推理的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频多模态大语言模型将视频帧视为孤立的2D快照，忽视了相机位姿（位置和朝向）这一关键的空间坐标信号，导致模型缺乏对人类所感知的持续场景的理解能力。

Method: 在视频MLLM中为每帧添加可学习的相机标记（camera tokens）和位姿回归头（pose regression head），并设计精心采样的训练方案；支持使用伪标注位姿进行训练。

Result: 在VSI-Bench等空间推理基准上提升4.5-6.5%，在8个额外空间和通用视频QA基准上表现良好，在ScanNet上实现流式位姿估计SOTA，且伪标注位姿训练还能提升通用视频QA性能。

Conclusion: 相机位姿是视频模型理解物理世界的基础信号，不仅有助于空间推理，对通用视频理解也有显著价值。

Abstract: Camera pose matters. The position and orientation of each viewpoint define a shared spatial coordinate frame that relates observations across video frames. Yet this signal is largely absent from multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) for video understanding, which process frames as isolated 2D snapshots, instead of the persistent scene humans perceive. We revisit pose as a lightweight supervisory signal and introduce Cambrian-P, a video MLLM augmented with per-frame learnable camera tokens and a pose regression head. With a carefully designed sampling scheme, the model achieves substantial gains of 4.5-6.5% on spatial reasoning benchmarks such as VSI-Bench, generalizes across eight additional spatial and general video QA benchmarks, and, as a byproduct, achieves state of the art streaming pose estimation on ScanNet. Surprisingly, training on pseudo-annotated poses from in-the-wild video further improves general video QA benchmarks, showing pose helps beyond spatial reasoning. Together, these results position camera pose as a fundamental signal for video models that reason about the physical world.

</details>


### [152] [Which Way Did It Move? Diagnosing and Overcoming Directional Motion Blindness in Video-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22823)
*Jongseo Lee,Hyuntak Lee,Sunghun Kim,Sooa Kim,Jihoon Chung,Jinwoo Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现Video-LLMs存在"方向运动盲症"：在判断简单物体运动方向（左/右/上/下）时表现接近随机水平。通过追踪信息流定位到"方向绑定鸿沟"——运动方向信息在视觉编码器、投影器和LLM隐状态中线性可访问，但无法正确绑定到言语答案。为此提出MoDirect数据集和DeltaDirect方法（基于相邻帧特征差预测归一化2D运动向量），在合成基准上准确率从25.9%提升至85.4%，在真实基准上提升21.9个百分点，且不损害标准视频理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Video-LLMs在时序视频理解上取得快速进展，但最基本的感知基元——图像平面内的运动方向判断却表现极差，这一基础能力的缺失亟需诊断和解决。

Method: 1) 通过追踪信息流定位问题：运动方向信息在视觉编码器、投影器和LLM隐状态中线性可访问，但无法正确绑定到言语答案（方向绑定鸿沟）。2) 提出MoDirect数据集（合成+真实）用于方向运动指令微调与评估。3) 提出DeltaDirect：一种诊断驱动的投影器级目标，从相邻帧特征差预测归一化2D运动向量。

Result: 在MoDirect-SynBench上，指令微调结合DeltaDirect将运动方向准确率从25.9%提升至85.4%；在MoDirect-RealBench上，无需真实微调数据即提升21.9个百分点，同时保持标准视频理解性能。

Conclusion: Video-LLMs存在方向运动盲症，根源是方向绑定鸿沟。DeltaDirect通过投影器级运动向量预测有效缓解此问题，具有良好的泛化性和可解释性。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) have made rapid progress on temporal video understanding, yet many fail at a basic perceptual primitive: signed image-plane motion direction. On simple videos of a single object moving left, right, up, or down, most Video-LLMs perform near chance, with above-chance cases largely attributable to prediction biases rather than genuine direction understanding. We call this failure directional motion blindness. We localize the failure by tracing motion direction information through the Video-LLM pipeline. Motion direction remains linearly accessible from the vision encoder, projector, and LLM hidden states, but the readout fails to bind this signal to the correct verbal answer option, revealing a direction binding gap. Although synthetic motion direction instruction tuning reduces this gap on the source domain, motion direction concept vector analysis shows that visual complexity weakens the signal magnitude and limits out-of-domain generalization. We introduce MoDirect, a dataset family for motion direction instruction tuning and evaluation, and DeltaDirect, a diagnosis-driven, projector-level objective that predicts normalized 2-D motion vectors from adjacent-frame feature deltas. On MoDirect-SynBench, instruction tuning with DeltaDirect improves motion direction accuracy from 25.9% to 85.4%. On MoDirect-RealBench, DeltaDirect improves real-world motion direction accuracy by 21.9 points over the vanilla baseline without real-world tuning data, while preserving standard video-understanding performance. Code: https://github.com/KHU-VLL/DeltaDirect

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Chain Reactions: How Nonce Collisions in ECDSA Compromise Polygon MEV Searchers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21498)
*Yash Madhwal,Andrey Seoev,Raffaele Della Pietra,Anastasiia Smirnova,Yury Yanovich*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Polygon MEV生态系统中由于非ce重用导致ECDSA私钥可被完全恢复的严重漏洞，分析了密封竞价拍卖中低延迟压力如何导致可预测的非ce模式，并提供了线性代数攻击的形式化描述和链上证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链交易依赖ECDSA签名的安全性，而非ce生成是关键。Polygon MEV生态系统中的搜索者为了在密封竞价拍卖中实现亚秒级响应时间，采用了可预测的非ce模式，这导致签名间存在线性关系，使被动攻击者可通过初等代数恢复私钥。

Method: 通过链上数据分析，识别搜索者使用的可预测非ce模式；建立紧凑的线性方程组形式化描述攻击方法，涵盖跨钱包非ce碰撞的危险情况；在Polygon链上提供具体的可利用模式证据。

Result: 发现了Polygon上可被利用的非ce模式，证明了协议诱导的延迟压力如何导致生产级区块链系统中的灾难性密码学失败，单个实现错误可同时危害多个账户。

Conclusion: 协议层面的延迟压力会迫使开发者采用不安全的密码学实践，导致严重的系统性安全风险。需要在协议设计和实现层面重视非ce生成的随机性和安全性。

Abstract: ECDSA signatures form the bedrock of blockchain transaction authentication, yet their security critically depends on proper nonce generation. We uncover a critical vulnerability in the Polygon MEV ecosystem: systematic nonce reuse that enables complete private key recovery. Analyzing on-chain data reveals that searchers, driven by the need for sub-second response times in sealed-bid auctions, employ predictable nonce patterns. These patterns create linear relationships between signatures, allowing passive attackers to recover private keys using elementary algebra. We provide a compact linear-system formulation for such attacks, including the dangerous case of cross-wallet nonce collisions, and present concrete evidence of exploitable patterns on Polygon. Our findings demonstrate how protocol-induced latency pressures can lead to catastrophic cryptographic failures in production blockchain systems, where a single implementation error compromises multiple accounts simultaneously.

</details>


### [154] [Frequency-Domain Regularized Adversarial Alignment for Transferable Attacks against Closed-Source MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21541)
*Leitao Yuan,Qinghua Mao,Daizong Liu,Kun Wang,Wenjie Wang,Yan Teng,Jing Shao,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FRA-Attack，通过统一频域正则化视角解决多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的对抗迁移攻击问题。高频DCT目标函数用于特征对齐，捕获模型间共享的视觉焦点；模型无关的低通频域梯度正则化（FGR）去除替代模型特有高频伪影，保留可迁移低频方向。在7个供应商的15款旗舰MLLM上达到SOTA，尤其对GPT-5.4、Claude-Opus-4.6和Gemini-3-flash表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs仍易受基于迁移的定向攻击，关键在于有效捕获不同模型间共享的内在视觉焦点，使扰动对齐于可迁移的语义线索而非替代模型特有行为。现有方法受限于空间域特征冗余和替代模型特有梯度信号，阻碍了跨模型迁移性。

Method: 提出了FRA-Attack，包含两个核心组件：1）高频DCT目标函数——在patch特征上应用高通DCT，抑制冗余全局结构，聚焦于携带MLLMs内在视觉焦点的高频带；2）频域梯度正则化（FGR）——一种模型无关的低通正则化器，仅利用几何频率坐标调节替代梯度，不涉及任何替代模型统计量，从而去除替代特有高频伪影并保留可迁移低频方向。两者构成统一的频域迁移性处理框架。

Result: 在7个供应商的15款旗舰MLLM上，FRA-Attack实现了优越的跨模型迁移性，在GPT-5.4、Claude-Opus-4.6和Gemini-3-flash上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 从频域正则化角度统一处理特征对齐和梯度优化，能够有效提升对抗样本在不同MLLM间的迁移性，验证了频域视角在对抗迁移攻击中的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) remain vulnerable to transfer-based targeted attacks, where perturbations optimized on open-source surrogate encoders can generalize to closed-source MLLMs. A key challenge for improving adversarial transferability is to effectively capture the intrinsic visual focus shared across different models, such that perturbations align with transferable semantic cues rather than surrogate-specific behaviors. However, existing methods suffer from spatial-domain feature redundancy and surrogate-specific gradient signals, thereby hindering cross-model transferability. In this paper, we propose FRA-Attack, which addresses both challenges from a unified frequency-domain regularization perspective. For feature alignment, a high-pass DCT objective on patch features suppresses redundant global structures and concentrates the loss on the high-frequency band that carries the MLLMs' intrinsic visual focus. For gradient optimization, we introduce Frequency-domain Gradient Regularization (FGR), a \textit{model-agnostic} low-pass regularizer that modulates the surrogate gradient using only the geometric frequency coordinate, \textit{i.e.}, no surrogate-derived statistic is involved, so that FGR is model-agnostic by construction, removing surrogate-specific high-frequency artifacts while preserving transferable low-frequency directions. Together, the two components form a unified frequency-domain treatment of transferability. Extensive experiments on $15$ flagship MLLMs across $7$ vendors show that FRA-Attack achieves superior cross-model transferability, particularly with state-of-the-art performance on GPT-5.4, Claude-Opus-4.6 and Gemini-3-flash.

</details>


### [155] [ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY: A Cross-Build Binary Dataset with Temporal Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21615)
*Chang Liu,Noah Fleischmann,Nicolò Altamura,Edward Raff,James Holt,Kristopher Micinski*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY，一个包含73,610个二进制文件的统一框架，整合了跨编译器多样性、跨版本历史与CVE标签，支持对二进制变体的多维度查询与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制语料库仅覆盖一两个二进制变体维度（如跨编译器构建无时间轴、或单构建版本的CVE标签），缺少同时包含跨构建多样性、跨版本历史与CVE标签的可查询结构。

Method: 构建包含73,610个二进制文件的语料库，覆盖248个开源项目，使用GCC/Clang/MSVC在多个优化级别下为Linux和Windows编译，包含多年历史构建；每个二进制文件的编译上下文、源代码、易受攻击函数和包版本均作为一等元数据存储在数据库中。

Result: 通过三项分析验证：1) 三阶段LLM基准测试（识别、策略引导检测、跨构建迁移）；2) MalConv嵌入、jTrans函数嵌入与TLSH模糊哈希在同一包版本聚类中的对比；3) 贝叶斯回归分解二进制相似性的时间距离、文件变更与提交贡献。

Conclusion: ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY将二进制变体的多个维度整合为统一框架，为二进制漏洞分析、版本演化研究及跨构建迁移学习提供支持。

Abstract: Existing binary corpora typically capture only one or two axes of binary variation: they either provide cross-compiler builds without a temporal axis, or CVE labels for single-build binaries. None combine cross-build diversity, cross-version history, and CVE labels into a queryable structure. We present ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY, which consolidates these dimensions into a unified framework where every binary's compilation context, source code, vulnerable functions, and package version are stored as first-class metadata.
  ASSEMBLAGE-DEEPHISTORY comprises 73,610 binaries spanning 248 open-source projects, compiled across GCC, Clang, and MSVC at multiple optimization levels on Linux and Windows, with multi-year historical builds. Each binary is indexed in a database that links it to its source code, functions, debug info, variant builds, historical versions, and vulnerable functions. Three analyses demonstrate this structure's value: (1) a three-stage LLM benchmark (recognition, strategy-guided detection, and cross-build transfer) to test whether LLMs reason about binary vulnerabilities or pattern-match on build-specific artifacts; (2) a comparison of MalConv embeddings, jTrans function embeddings, and TLSH fuzzy hashes quantifying how same-package versions cluster in each space; and (3) a Bayesian regression decomposing binary similarity into contributions from temporal distance, file changes, and commits.

</details>


### [156] [Adversarial Reframing: A Framework for Targeted Generation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21674)
*Shahnewaz Karim Sakib,Swati Kar,Anindya Bijoy Das*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: THREAT是一个基于推理的多LLM协作框架，通过将提示发现形式化为非凸优化问题，高效生成越狱提示，以更低的计算成本实现更高的攻击成功率，且生成的提示仅有不到1%被标记为有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在实际部署中仍易受到越狱攻击（即基于提示的攻击绕过安全过滤器），现有方法存在计算成本高或攻击效果有限的问题，亟需更高效且有效的漏洞发现方法。

Method: 提出THREAT框架：将提示发现建模为非凸优化问题，采用推理驱动的迭代搜索循环，协调多个LLM协同工作，以降低运行时间并提高攻击有效性。

Result: 在多种数据集和模型架构上，THREAT相比现有方法实现了更高的攻击成功率和更低的计算成本；生成的越狱提示在被标记为有害的比例上低于1%，而对应的未修改提示约有50%被拒绝。

Conclusion: THREAT揭示了对齐LLM中先前未检测到的安全漏洞，可作为主动增强基础模型安全性的实用工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely deployed in diverse real-world settings, yet remain vulnerable to jailbreaking, where prompt-based attacks bypass safety filters. We present THREAT (Targeted Harmful generation via Reframing and Exploitation of Adversarial Tactics), a reasoning-driven framework that coordinates multiple LLMs in an iterative search loop to find textual jailbreak prompts. We formulate prompt discovery as a nonconvex optimization problem and provide an efficient solution that lowers runtime and improves attack effectiveness. Across diverse datasets and model architectures, THREAT delivers higher attack success rates with lower computational cost than prior methods. The crafted prompts were flagged as harmful in fewer than 1% of cases, compared with about 50% refusals for the corresponding unmodified prompts. These findings reveal previously undetected vulnerabilities in aligned LLMs and position THREAT as a practical tool for proactively strengthening the safety of foundation models.

</details>


### [157] [FuzzingBrain V2: A Multi-Agent LLM System for Automated Vulnerability Discovery and Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21779)
*Ze Sheng,Zhicheng Chen,Qingxiao Xu,Kewen Zhu,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FuzzingBrain V2是一个基于多智能体和OSS-Fuzz的自动化漏洞检测系统，通过可疑点抽象、逻辑驱动分层分析和MCP上下文工程等方法，在AIxCC 2025决赛数据集上达到90%检出率，并在实际部署中发现29个零日漏洞（2个已分配CVE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM漏洞检测存在三个关键问题：高误报率且缺乏可复现验证、函数级/行级粒度的定位精度与上下文平衡不佳、对跨函数依赖和复杂触发条件的漏洞推理能力不足。

Method: (1) 基于Google OSS-Fuzz的全自动分析，确保漏洞可模糊测试复现；(2) 提出Suspicious Point控制流抽象实现最优粒度的精准定位；(3) 逻辑驱动分层函数分析+双层模糊测试增强资源约束下的覆盖率；(4) 基于MCP的静态/动态分析工具与上下文工程增强复杂漏洞推理。

Result: AIxCC 2025决赛C/C++数据集上检测率90%（40个中检出36个）；实际部署中发现29个零日漏洞（12个开源项目），均被维护者确认修复，2个已分配CVE编号。

Conclusion: FuzzingBrain V2通过结合模糊测试可复现性验证与多智能体LLM分析，有效解决了当前自动化漏洞检测中的高误报、粒度选择和复杂推理等核心挑战。

Abstract: Software vulnerabilities pose critical security threats, with nearly 50,000 CVEs reported in 2025. While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for automated vulnerability detection, three key challenges remain. First, LLM-generated vulnerability reports suffer from high false positive rates and lack
  reproducible verification. Second, existing LLM-based approaches use suboptimal granularities for vulnerability localization: function-level analysis overlooks bugs when context becomes extensive, while line-level analysis lacks sufficient context. Third, existing approaches have difficulty reasoning about
  vulnerabilities with complex cross-function dependencies and triggering conditions.
  We present FuzzingBrain V2, a multi-agent system that addresses these gaps through four key contributions: (1) fully automated vulnerability analysis built on Google's OSS-Fuzz, ensuring all reported vulnerabilities are fuzzer-reproducible; (2) Suspicious Point, a novel control-flow-based abstraction for precise
  vulnerability localization at the optimal granularity; (3) logic-driven hierarchical function analysis with dual-layer fuzzing enhancing function coverage under resource constraints; (4) MCP-based static and dynamic analysis tools with context engineering enhancing complex vulnerability reasoning.
  On the AIxCC 2025 Final Competition C/C++ dataset, FuzzingBrain V2 achieved 90% detection rate (36 of 40 vulnerabilities). In real-world deployment, FuzzingBrain V2 discovered 29 zero-day vulnerabilities across 12 open-source projects, all confirmed and fixed by maintainers, with 2 assigned CVE IDs.

</details>


### [158] [Polars inside Intel SGX2 Enclaves: An Empirical Study of Confidential Analytical Query Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21797)
*Wei Wang,Burns Smith,Kenny Leftin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Intel SGX2可信执行环境中运行Arrow原生DataFrame引擎Polars的性能表现，发现端到端开销约为1.5倍，但表加载开销从2.27倍上升到4.07倍，而查询计算开销从1.52倍下降到1.43倍；惰性API比急切API快约2.25倍，且大内存下急切API会OOM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TEE机密分析研究多聚焦于SQL数据库引擎或早期SGX1代，缺乏对新一代SGX2与Arrow原生引擎（如Polars）结合的全面性能评估。

Method: 使用Gramine在Intel SGX2 enclave中运行Polars DataFrame引擎，基于TPC-H SF30基准测试和Azure Blob Storage，采用四种数据集宽度配置（约22-73GB），分别评测标准TPC-H功率分数和仅查询变体，并比较惰性与急切API的执行差异。

Result: 端到端开销稳定在1.49-1.56倍，但表加载开销从2.27倍升至4.07倍，查询开销从1.52倍降至1.43倍；中位数查询延迟1.45倍，最大2.57倍；惰性API比急切API快2.25-2.27倍，急切API在41GB以上出现OOM。

Conclusion: SGX2能够支持Arrow原生分析处理，安全开销与DuckDB-SGX2研究相当，但加载路径放大和API级优化是端到端性能的关键决定因素。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) have renewed interest in confidential analytics, but most prior evaluations focus on SQL database engines or earlier SGX generations. This paper studies an Arrow-native DataFrame engine, Polars, running inside Intel SGX2 enclaves via Gramine on TPC-H SF30 with Azure Blob Storage. We report both the standard TPC-H power score and a query-only variant that removes table-loading time in order to separate compute overhead from data-ingestion overhead. Across four dataset-width configurations (approximately 22-73 GB), end-to-end overhead remains nearly constant at 1.49-1.56$\times$, but this composite metric obscures two distinct behaviors: query-only overhead declines from 1.51-1.52$\times$ to 1.43-1.44$\times$, whereas table-loading overhead rises from 2.27$\times$ to 4.07$\times$. We further show that overhead is not uniform across queries: for the len130 configuration, the median per-query SGX slowdown is 1.45$\times$ with a maximum of 2.57$\times$, and a small set of queries exhibits pronounced run-to-run spikes consistent with stateful EPC pressure. Finally, we compare Polars' lazy and eager APIs under the same TEE setting. Lazy execution is 2.25-2.27$\times$ faster overall, while eager execution fails with out-of-memory errors at 41 GB and above. Relative to the recent DuckDB-SGX2 study, our results suggest that SGX2 can support Arrow-native analytical processing with a similar order of security overhead, but that load-path amplification and API-level optimization are first-order determinants of end-to-end performance.

</details>


### [159] [Graph Structure of Chebyshev Permutation Polynomials over Binary and Ternary Adic Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21819)
*Xiaoxiong Lu,Yuling Dai,Chengqing Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了环 $\mathbb{Z}_{2^{k_1}3^{k_2}}$ 上Chebyshev置换多项式的函数图结构，给出了路径长度和圈结构的显式刻画，证明了图具有强正则性，包括固定长度的圈数量恒定以及随 $k_1, k_2$ 增长的可预测分支模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有限域上非线性映射的函数图结构对于分析其动力学复杂性及在密码学和伪随机生成中的潜在应用至关重要。

Method: 基于Chebyshev多项式模2的幂和3的幂的新性质，将环中每个元素视为顶点，多项式定义的映射关系视为有向边，构建函数图并分析其路径长度与圈结构。

Result: 尽管二元和三元分量引入了复杂性，但函数图表现出强正则性：固定长度的圈数量恒定，且随着 $k_1$ 和 $k_2$ 增大，分支模式是可预测的。

Conclusion: 结果扩展了先前在素数幂环上的研究，为数字非线性映射的复杂性涌现提供了见解，并支持其密码应用的安全性分析。

Abstract: Understanding the functional graph of a nonlinear map over a finite domain is crucial for analyzing its dynamical complexity and potential applications in cryptography and pseudorandom generation. In this paper, we investigate the graph structure of Chebyshev permutation polynomials over the ring $\mathbb{Z}_{2^{k_1}3^{k_2}}$, where $k_1$ and $k_2$ are positive integers and $0\in\{k_1, k_2\}$. Each element of the ring is regarded as a vertex, and the mapping relation defined by the polynomial corresponds to a directed edge. Building on new properties of Chebyshev polynomials modulo powers of $2$ and $3$, we provide an explicit characterization of path lengths and cycle structures in the functional graph. We show that, despite the complexities introduced by the binary and ternary components, the graph exhibits strong regularities, including a constant number of cycles of a given length and predictable branching patterns as $k_1$ and $k_2$ increase. Our results extend previous studies over prime-power rings, offering insights into the emergence of complexity in digital nonlinear maps and supporting the security analysis of their cryptographic applications.

</details>


### [160] [A Large Language Model Approach to Generating Bypass Rules for Malware Evasion in Analysis Sandbox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21821)
*Zhiyong Sui,Lamine Noureddine,Mst Eshita Khatun,Sideeq Bello,Justin Woodring,Aisha Ali-Gombe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ABLE系统，利用大语言模型自动生成YARA规则以绕过沙箱逃逸检测，在334个真实恶意软件样本上达到79%的绕过成功率，相比现有分析平台多识别47%的恶意软件家族分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的沙箱逃逸对抗方法依赖人工逆向分析每个逃逸机制并手动编写绕过规则，难以应对快速演变的逃逸技术，缺乏可扩展性。

Method: ABLE系统利用LLM分析因潜在逃逸行为而终止的恶意软件执行轨迹，采用多种推理策略生成目标绕过YARA规则，并引入自动清洗管道和基于反馈的迭代优化机制来修复语法错误并提升规则有效性。

Result: 在334个真实恶意软件样本上，ABLE实现79%的绕过成功率，其中迭代优化贡献了29.5%的成功案例；相比现有分析平台，ABLE多识别47%的恶意软件家族分类并暴露了之前隐藏的行为。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的自动化规则生成方法能有效扩展沙箱逃逸对抗能力，迭代优化机制显著提升了绕过规则的质量，为恶意软件分析提供了更全面的行为可见性。

Abstract: Sandbox evasion remains a critical challenge for automated malware analysis, as modern malware employs environment checks to detect analysis platforms and suppress malicious behavior. Existing approaches rely on manually crafted bypass rules that require deep reverse engineering of each evasion mechanism -an approach that cannot scale against rapidly evolving evasion techniques. In this paper, we leverage large language models (LLMs) to automatically generate YARA rules that bypass evasion checks in sandbox environments. We propose ABLE, which analyzes execution traces from malware terminated due to potentially evasive behavior and employs multiple reasoning strategies to generate targeted bypass rules. To address syntactic errors and improve the efficacy of the bypass rules in the LLM outputs, we introduce an auto-sanitization pipeline and feedback-driven iterative refinement. We evaluate ABLE on 334 real-world malware samples across four open-weight LLMs. ABLE achieves a 79% bypass success rate, with iterative refinement contributing 29.5% of successful cases. Compared to existing analysis platforms, ABLE identifies 47% more malware family classifications and exposes previously hidden behaviors.

</details>


### [161] [Quality-Assured Fuzz Harness Generation via the Four Principles Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21824)
*Ze Sheng,Dmitrijs Trizna,Luigino Camastra,Zhicheng Chen,Qingxiao Xu,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: QuartetFuzz 是一个基于四原则框架（逻辑正确性、API协议合规、安全边界尊重、入口点充分性）的自主测试桩生成系统，通过LLM智能体在模糊测试前进行生成-检查-修复循环，在23个开源项目（C/C++、Java、JavaScript）中提交42个漏洞报告（29个已确认/修复，含3个CVE），误报率仅4.8%，并在586个生产测试桩中识别出53个违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化测试桩生成工具缺乏对正确性的系统保证，逻辑错误、API误用和生命周期违规在源码层面未被检测；LLM驱动的规模化生成使质量失控问题更加严峻。

Method: 提出四原则框架（P1:逻辑正确性、P2:API协议合规、P3:安全边界尊重、P4:入口点充分性），包含数学规范与可实现检查；基于LLM智能体在模糊测试前执行生成-检查-修复循环，确保测试桩满足P1-P4。

Result: 在23个开源项目中提交42个漏洞报告，29个已确认/修复（含3个CVE），仅2个被拒绝（4.8%误报率）；内置P1/P2检查自动拦截58次测试桩导致的崩溃；在586个生产测试桩中发现53个违规（45个已确认，35个已修复）。

Conclusion: 四原则框架首次在源码层面定义了测试桩正确性，通过自主LLM智能体显著提升了测试桩质量，证明了系统化质量保证对自动测试桩生成的重要性。

Abstract: Fuzz testing is the dominant technique for finding memory-safety vulnerabilities in C/C++ software, yet its effectiveness hinges on the quality of fuzz harnesses -- the programs that bridge fuzzers and library APIs. A growing body of tools now automate harness generation, but none systematically ensures the correctness of produced harnesses: logic errors, API misuse, and lifecycle violations go undetected at the source level. As LLM-driven generation scales harness creation, uncontrolled quality turns scale into a liability.
  We present QuartetFuzz, an autonomous harness-generation system that systematically improves correctness throughout the generation process. At its core is the Four Principles framework -- Logic Correctness (P1), API Protocol Compliance (P2), Security Boundary Respect (P3), and Entry Point Adequacy (P4) -- the first source-level definition of harness correctness with mathematical specifications and implementable checks. We operationalize these principles in an autonomous LLM agent that produces harnesses satisfying P1-P4 through a generate-check-fix loop before any fuzzing begins.
  Deployed on 23 open-source projects spanning C/C++, Java, and JavaScript, the system submits 42 bug reports, of which 29 are fixed or confirmed upstream (including 3 CVEs) and only 2 are rejected (4.8% FP rate). During generation, the built-in P1/P2 checks automatically intercepted 58 harness-induced crashes that would otherwise have been false positives. Applied as a quality auditor to 586 existing production harnesses across 70 projects, the system identifies 53 violations (45 confirmed, 35 fixed). We release a dataset of 100 labeled harnesses for reproducible evaluation. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/OwenSanzas/QuartetFuzz

</details>


### [162] [SPIDER: Two Server Functionality for the Cost of Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21857)
*Ofir Dvir,Kali Hale,Javin Zipkin,Divyakant Agrawal,Dahlia Malkhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 baseSPIDER 和 SPIDER 两种隐私信息检索（PIR）方案。baseSPIDER 在单服务器有状态客户端设置下，匹配渐近最优通信复杂度并改进常数因子，设计更简洁。SPIDER 是首个无需服务器合作、基于默认数据库接口的单服务器 PIR 构造，通过简单转换消除部署障碍，并可将该转换广泛用于其他 PIR 方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单服务器 PIR 方案在通信复杂度常数因子、设计简洁性以及部署可行性（依赖专用 API、辅助服务器状态或协议特定交互）方面存在不足，需要更高效、更易于实际部署的解决方案。

Method: baseSPIDER 采用单服务器有状态客户端预处理+存储提示的方法；SPIDER 通过将 baseSPIDER 转换为默认服务器设置，无需服务器合作或专用 API，仅需常规索引访问即可实现隐私检索。该转换方法还可推广至其他三种近期 PIR 方案。

Result: baseSPIDER 在通信复杂度常数因子上优于现有方案，尤其适合大条目数据库；SPIDER 是首个在默认服务器接口下实现隐私的单服务器 PIR，消除了部署障碍，但客户端计算开销较高。

Conclusion: baseSPIDER 和 SPIDER 分别在不同维度推进了 PIR 技术：前者优化效率与简洁性，后者通过默认服务器设计大幅降低实际部署门槛，且转换方法具有通用性。

Abstract: We introduce baseSPIDER and SPIDER, private information retrieval (PIR) schemes that embody two technical advancements. The baseSPIDER protocol operates with a single server and a stateful client that performs pre-processing and stores hints for future queries. In this setting, baseSPIDER introduces a new approach that matches the asymptotically optimal communication complexity of state-of-the-art schemes while improving constant factors--an advantage that is particularly significant for databases with large entries. In addition, baseSPIDER offers a conceptually simpler design relative to prior protocols. SPIDER operates over a default database interface and requires no cooperation from the server at any stage. To our knowledge, SPIDER is the first single-server PIR construction of this design, achieving privacy without specialized APIs, auxiliary server state, or protocol-specific interaction beyond conventional indexed access. SPIDER is built via a simple transformation of baseSPIDER to the default server setting, eliminating deployment barriers and enabling immediate applicability to existing systems. This transformation can be applied more broadly to three recent PIR solutions, adapting them for use in the default-server paradigm and yielding solutions of independent interest. SPIDER compares to the resulting modified solutions by exhibiting a simpler design while incurring higher client computational work.

</details>


### [163] [CCLab: Adversarial Testing of Learning- and Non-Learning-Based Congestion Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21915)
*Zhi Chen,Shehab Sarar Ahmed,Chenkai Wang,Brighten Godfrey,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CCLab对抗测试框架，系统评估学习型与传统拥塞控制算法在对抗环境下的鲁棒性，发现学习型CC整体更鲁棒，且对抗轨迹可用于训练更鲁棒的CC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拥塞控制算法在对抗条件下的鲁棒性尚未被充分理解，现有研究缺乏对学习型与传统CC在信号损坏或环境挑战下的系统性比较。

Method: 提出CCLab对抗测试框架，包含基于强化学习的对抗智能体，与拥塞控制策略形成闭环，在特征层面和环境层面生成有界扰动，并通过显式约束保证真实性。

Result: 两类CC在对抗测试下均出现性能下降，但学习型CC整体比传统人工设计算法更鲁棒；对抗轨迹可用于训练更鲁棒的CC，在挑战性和正常条件下均优于现有学习型CC。

Conclusion: CCLab框架能有效评估CC鲁棒性，对抗训练是提升CC鲁棒性的可行方向，学习型CC在对抗环境下具有相对优势。

Abstract: Congestion controllers (CCs) are critical to network performance, and yet their robustness under adverse conditions remains insufficiently understood. While recent learning-based CCs have demonstrated strong performance in controlled environments, it is unclear how they compare to traditional CCs when controllers' input signals are corrupted or when environmental conditions become systematically challenging. In this paper, we introduce CCLab, an adversarial testing framework for systematically evaluating the robustness of both learning-based and non-learning-based CCs. CCLab includes a reinforcement learning (RL)-based adversarial agent that operates in a closed loop with the congestion control policy, generating bounded perturbations either on input signals (feature-level) or on external network conditions (environment-level), while preserving realism through explicit constraints. Using this framework, we compare learning-based CCs with non-learning-based CCs under both feature-level and environment-level adversarial conditions. While both types of CCs suffer from performance degradation under adversarial testing, we find that learning-based CCs, in general, are more robust than traditional human-designed algorithms. Finally, we show that our adversarial traces can be used to train more robust CCs that outperform existing learning-based CCs under both challenging and normal conditions.

</details>


### [164] [Blind Spots in the Guard: How Domain-Camouflaged Injection Attacks Evade Detection in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22001)
*Aaditya Pai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现并形式化了LLM智能体注入检测中的一个系统性盲点：当注入载荷模仿目标文档的领域词汇和权威结构时（称为“领域伪装注入”），标准检测器的检测率大幅下降（Llama 3.1 8B从93.8%降至9.7%，Gemini 2.0 Flash从100%降至55.6%）。多智能体辩论架构会放大静态注入攻击，而专用安全分类器Llama Guard 3完全无法检测伪装载荷。作者认为这一漏洞对于弱模型而言是架构性的而非偶然性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注入检测器基于静态、模板化的载荷进行校准，这些载荷明确宣称为覆盖指令。然而，当注入载荷被生成以模仿目标文档的领域词汇和权威结构时（领域伪装注入），标准检测器无法识别，存在系统性盲点。

Method: 形式化定义了伪装检测差距（CDG），即在静态注入和伪装注入之间检测率的差异。在跨3个领域、45个任务上系统评估了Llama 3.1 8B、Gemini 2.0 Flash以及Llama Guard 3的检测表现，并测试了多智能体辩论架构下的攻击放大效应。

Result: Llama 3.1 8B检测率从93.8%降至9.7%，Gemini 2.0 Flash从100%降至55.6%；Llama Guard 3完全无法检测伪装载荷（0%）。CDG在统计上显著（p<0.001）。多智能体辩论可将小模型的静态注入攻击放大9.9倍。针对性检测增强仅提供部分修复（Llama提升10.2%，Gemini提升78.7%）。

Conclusion: 领域伪装注入对现有检测器构成了严重威胁，弱模型上的漏洞是架构性的而非偶然性的，需要更根本的防御方案。

Abstract: Injection detectors deployed to protect LLM agents are calibrated on static, template-based payloads that announce themselves as override directives. We identify a systematic blind spot: when payloads are generated to mimic the domain vocabulary and authority structures of the target document, what we call domain camouflaged injection, standard detectors fail to flag them, with detection rates dropping from 93.8% to 9.7% on Llama 3.1 8B and from 100% to 55.6% on Gemini 2.0 Flash. We formalize this as the Camouflage Detection Gap (CDG), the difference in injection detection rate between static and camouflaged payloads. Across 45 tasks spanning three domains and two model families, CDG is large and statistically significant (chi^2 = 38.03, p < 0.001 for Llama; chi^2 = 17.05, p < 0.001 for Gemini), with zero reverse discordant pairs in either case. We additionally evaluate Llama Guard 3, a production safety classifier, which detects zero camouflage payloads (IDRcamouflage = 0.000), confirming that the blind spot extends beyond few-shot detectors to dedicated safety classifiers. We further show that multi-agent debate architectures amplify static injection attacks by up to 9.9x on smaller models, while stronger models show collective resistance. Targeted detector augmentation provides only partial remediation (10.2% improvement on Llama, 78.7% on Gemini), suggesting the vulnerability is architectural rather than incidental for weaker models. Our framework, task bank, and payload generator are released publicly.

</details>


### [165] [Parser-Free Querying of Security Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22027)
*Evan Luo,Julien Piet,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Sieve 是一个利用大语言模型从自然语言安全查询自动生成可执行查询代码的系统，通过轻量级日志格式上下文实现，在133个安全查询评估中相比人工脚本降低了3倍以上的错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全分析师需要查询系统日志检测威胁和调查事件，但每种日志源使用不同的半结构化格式，日志易产生但难使用。传统方案（逐源构建解析器）强大但需要持续工程投入；直接使用grep等工具查询原始日志虽然避免了解析成本，但要求分析师了解每种日志的消息变体，且无法表达安全调查所需的多行时间序列查询。

Method: Sieve 系统通过利用大语言模型，结合自动提取的轻量级日志格式上下文，从自然语言安全问题生成可执行的查询代码。每次查询仅需一次LLM调用，随后进行确定性执行。

Result: 在5种日志类型上的133个安全查询评估中，Sieve在复杂的时间序列和跨事件查询上相比人工分析师脚本实现了3倍以上的错误率降低，在主动调查中最关键的多行关联任务上提升最为显著。

Conclusion: LLM生成的代码可以弥合结构化日志查询的表达能力与直接处理原始文件的即时性之间的差距。

Abstract: Security analysts routinely query system logs to detect threats and investigate incidents, but each log source uses its own semi-structured format: logs are cheap to produce, but expensive to use. The standard approach, building per-source parsers to normalize logs into structured schemas, is powerful but requires continuous engineering effort for each new format. Querying raw logs directly with tools like grep avoids this cost, but requires analysts to know each source's message variants and cannot express the multi-line temporal queries that security investigations demand. We present Sieve, a system that generates executable query code from natural-language security questions by grounding a large language model with lightweight, automatically extracted log-format context, requiring only one LLM call per query followed by deterministic execution. Evaluating 133 security queries across 5 log types, we find that Sieve achieves over a 3x reduction in error rate on complex temporal and cross-event queries compared to manual analyst scripting, with the largest gains on the multi-line correlation tasks most critical to active investigations. Our results and benchmark provide evidence that LLM-generated code can bridge the gap between the expressiveness of structured log querying and the immediacy of working directly with raw files.

</details>


### [166] [Safeguarding Text-to-Image Generative Models Against Unauthorized Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22060)
*Yilan Gao,Sida Huang,Hongyuan Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WaveGuard是一种单次生成、基于生成器的保护框架，通过在发布图像中注入频率感知的不可感知扰动，在保持视觉质量的同时，有效防止用户通过API查询窃取生成模型的知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭权重生成服务以查询API形式部署，用户无法访问模型参数，但攻击者可通过反复查询收集大量合成图像来训练替代模型，实现未经授权的知识蒸馏和能力复制。现有防御需要在不牺牲视觉保真度的前提下，提供对扰动幅度的显式控制并高效扩展至大规模输出。

Method: WaveGuard采用频率感知的扰动生成器，在用户指定的扰动预算下，向合成图像注入结构化、不可感知的扰动。该方法为单次前向传播，通过频域操作实现扰动注入，在保持感知质量的同时降低受保护图像作为训练数据的效用。

Result: 在WikiArt合成输出蒸馏设置下的实验表明，WaveGuard实现了优效的效能-保真度-效率权衡，具备显式的不可感知性控制，并在保护效率上取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: WaveGuard是一种实用且高效的防御方案，能够在保持图像视觉质量的前提下，有效防御针对闭权重生成服务的模型窃取攻击，且具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Closed-weight generative services are increasingly deployed through query-based APIs, where users can obtain generated outputs while model parameters remain inaccessible. However, such deployment does not prevent model stealing: an attacker can repeatedly query the service, collect large volumes of released synthetic images, and use them as training data for a private substitute model. This query-output-driven process enables unauthorized knowledge distillation and capability replication without direct access to the original weights. To mitigate this threat, a practical defense should preserve the visual fidelity of released images, provide explicit control over perturbation magnitude, and scale efficiently to large-volume output release. We present WaveGuard, a single-pass, generator-based protection framework that safeguards released synthetic images under a user-specified perturbation budget. WaveGuard employs a frequency-aware perturbation generator to inject structured, imperceptible perturbations that maintain perceptual utility for benign viewers while reducing the usefulness of protected images as training data for unauthorized student models. Extensive experiments under WikiArt-related synthetic-output distillation settings show that WaveGuard achieves a favorable efficacy--fidelity--efficiency trade-off, with explicit imperceptibility control and substantial gains in protection efficiency.

</details>


### [167] [QT-PUF: Quantum Tunneling Leakage Based PUF for Implantable IoMT Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22113)
*Yueqi Ma,Vivek Mohan,Chip-Hong Chang,Emmanuel M. Drakakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出QT-PUF，一种基于量子隧穿栅极泄漏电流的物理不可克隆函数，专为超低功耗医疗物联网（IoMT）设备设计。该PUF利用标准CMOS工艺中工艺偏差引起的栅极泄漏电流差异，通过伪电阻I-V前端差分读出电路将皮安级泄漏差异转换为数字响应，无需外部激励或稳定电路。在65nm CMOS工艺下仿真显示，熵值达0.9999998，片间汉明距离为0.5001，平均功耗96.04 nW/bit（19.21 fJ/bit），在1.0–1.3V和10–70°C范围内平均BER低于0.000163，能满足植入式设备的工作条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoMT设备面临物理篡改和伪造威胁，现有PUF（如基于存储器、环形振荡器或仲裁器的方案）因功耗高、需要额外激励电路或稳定性差，不适合超低功耗IoMT应用。因此需要一种极低功耗、高可靠性且无需外部激励的PUF方案来保障设备信任与完整性。

Method: 利用标准CMOS器件中工艺偏差导致的量子隧穿栅极泄漏电流作为熵源；设计伪电阻I-V前端差分读出电路，将皮安级泄漏电流差异转换为稳定的数字响应；采用纯静态偏置工作方式，无需外部激励或稳定化电路。

Result: 65nm CMOS工艺仿真：熵值0.9999998，片间汉明距离0.5001，平均功耗96.04 nW/bit（19.21 fJ/bit），工作电压0.9–1.3V、温度0–100°C，在1.0–1.3V和10–70°C范围内平均BER低于0.000163。

Conclusion: QT-PUF通过利用量子隧穿栅极泄漏作为熵源，实现了超低功耗（nW级）和高可靠性，特别适合植入式医疗设备等对功耗和稳定性要求严苛的IoMT应用场景。

Abstract: The Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) marks a shift toward decentralized healthcare, enabling continuous monitoring and personalized care through connected wearable and implantable devices. However, ensuring the trust and integrity of these devices themselves remains a major challenge, as physical compromise or counterfeiting can directly endanger patient safety, privacy, and data integrity. This work presents QT-PUF, a gate-tunneling-leakage-based physical unclonable function (PUF) that leverages quantum-mechanical gate leakage resulting from process-induced variations in standard CMOS devices. A differential readout circuit with a pseudo-resistor I-to-V frontend is proposed to convert the picoampere-level leakage variations into digital responses. Unlike existing PUFs such as those based on memory, ring oscillators, or arbiters, which are less suitable for ultralow-power IoMT devices (due to additional circuitry, power overhead, or poor stability), QT-PUF requires no external excitation or stabilization and operates under static bias. Simulation-based measurements for a $\mathbf{65}$~nm CMOS process demonstrate an entropy of $\mathbf{0.9999998}$, an FHD of $\mathbf{0.5001}$, and an average power (energy) consumption of $\mathbf{96.04}$~nW/bit ($\mathbf{19.21}$~fJ/bit, respectively) at $\mathbf{1.2\,V}$ and $\mathbf{35\,^{\circ}C}$ for the proposed PUF. It operates reliably across $\mathbf{0.9}\text{--}\mathbf{1.3}$~V and $\mathbf{0}\text{--}\mathbf{100\,^{\circ}C}$ with an average BER below $\mathbf{0.000163}$ across $\mathbf{1.0}\text{--}\mathbf{1.3}$~V and $\mathbf{10}\text{--}\mathbf{70\,^{\circ}C}$ within the operating conditions of typical implantable devices.

</details>


### [168] [Adversarial Trust Poisoning in Vehicular Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22122)
*Yutong Liu,Chenyi Wang,Ming F. Li,Qingzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TrustFlip攻击，利用协同感知系统中的一致性检测防御机制本身，通过部署物理对抗性物体使良性车辆间产生不一致观测，从而误导防御系统对目标车辆降权或排除，导致协作感知性能下降。作者进一步提出TrustReflect轻量级防御机制作为初步缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同感知防御系统依赖跨车辆一致性检测和信任评估来抵御数据伪造与操纵攻击，但这些防御机制本身存在被反向利用的攻击面。研究者旨在揭示此类间接攻击对协同感知系统的威胁。

Method: TrustFlip攻击：部署物理的、真实存在的对抗性物体，使良性车辆之间产生不一致的观测结果；利用防御系统的一致性检测机制，将这些不一致性错误归因于目标车辆，导致其信任评分下降并被排除出协作。TrustReflect防御：轻量级自反思机制，将争议区域标记为不确定并将其排除在信任评估之外。

Result: 在多种协同感知架构和防御机制下，TrustFlip可在87.7%的场景中移除目标良性车辆，并使平均精度（AP）下降高达13%。TrustReflect可将攻击成功率降低35-100%。

Conclusion: 揭示了基于一致性的协同感知防御存在根本性安全漏洞，攻击者可利用物理世界对抗性物体间接操控信任评估系统。TrustReflect作为初步缓解方案证明了自反思机制的有效性，但更鲁棒的防御仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Collaborative perception (CP) enables connected and autonomous vehicles to share sensor data and jointly reason about their environment. To defend against adversaries that fabricate or manipulate shared data, existing systems employ cross-vehicle inconsistency detection and trust estimation, penalizing vehicles whose observations conflict with the majority. In this work, we show that these defenses themselves introduce a new attack surface. We present TrustFlip, a novel attack that weaponizes consistency-based defenses to poison the trust assigned to benign vehicles. Instead of injecting false data into the collaboration pipeline, it deploys physical adversarial objects that are genuine but induce inconsistent observations among benign vehicles. The resulting inconsistencies are misattributed by the defense to the targeted vehicle, causing its trust score to degrade and eventually leading to its downweighting or exclusion from collaboration. Consequently, the system loses reliable sensing contributors, degrading perception capability and potentially inducing safety-critical failures. We evaluate TrustFlip across multiple collaborative perception architectures and defense mechanisms. Our results show that state-of-the-art defenses can be significantly affected: the attack removes the targeted benign vehicle from collaboration in up to 87.7% of scenarios and drops Average Precision (AP) by up to 13%. As an initial mitigation, we introduce TrustReflect, a lightweight self-reflection mechanism that marks disputed regions as uncertain and excludes them from trust evaluation, reducing the attack success rate by 35-100%.

</details>


### [169] [PACT: Reducing Alert Fatigue in Low-Prevalence SOC Streams with Triggered Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22324)
*Samuel Ndichu,Tao Ban,Seiichi Ozawa,Takeshi Takahashi,Daisuke Inoue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PACT是一种帕累托感知的触发式主动学习框架，通过自适应窗口评分偏移触发器和混合获取规则，在低患病率的SOC警报流中，相比冻结基线和定期更新方法，显著降低了良性归一化假阳性负担（分别降低43%和21%），同时使用更少的分析师查询（减少3.8-5.2倍），但纯FP最小化会以召回率大幅下降为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOC面临持续的告警疲劳问题：在低患病率的数据流中，即使很低的假阳性率也会产生大量调查负担，而宏观F1分数掩盖了分析师的真实负担。现有方法未能有效解决这一权衡问题。

Method: PACT包含三个组件：1) 冻结的XGBoost-Focal筛选器作为基础分类器；2) 自适应窗口评分偏移触发器，用于检测模型需要更新的时机；3) 混合获取规则，结合阈值相对不确定性和高评分采样，实现主动学习的数据选取。

Result: 在AIT-ADS和BOTSv1基准上，PACT达到自适应方法中最低的良性归一化假阳性负担，相比冻结基线负担降低43%和21%，相比定期均匀随机更新减少3.8倍和5.2倍的分析师查询。消融实验表明获取策略贡献超过触发时机，但自由运行触发下阳性窗口召回率下降约10个百分点。

Conclusion: PACT有效平衡了假阳性负担和召回率，证明了主动学习在低患病率安全告警流中的价值。纯FP最小化策略虽然负担更低，但会导致召回率不可接受的下降（BOTSv1下降55个百分点），说明负担与召回率之间存在根本性权衡。

Abstract: Security operations centers face persistent alert fatigue: in low-prevalence streams, even low false-positive rates generate substantial investigation load, while aggregate F1 scores obscure analyst burden. We introduce PACT, a Pareto-aware controller for triggered active learning, which wraps an already-deployed frozen XGBoost-Focal screener with an adaptive windowing score-shift trigger and a hybrid acquisition rule combining threshold-relative uncertainty with high-score sampling. On two public low-prevalence benchmarks, AIT-ADS (AIT Alert Data Set), and BOTSv1 (Boss of the SOC version 1), PACT attains the lowest benign-normalized false-positive (FP) burden among the adaptive methods tested. It reduces burden by 43% and 21%, respectively, relative to a frozen baseline, while using 3.8x and 5.2x fewer analyst queries than periodic uniform-random updating. A matched-trigger ablation controls trigger timing and shows that acquisition contributes beyond timing alone, at the cost of approximately ten percentage points of positive-window recall under free-running triggers. A frozen threshold-only baseline pushes FP lower still but collapses BOTSv1 recall by 55 percentage points. Under the evaluated workload assumptions, pure FP minimization trades unacceptable recall for that lower burden.

</details>


### [170] [Building Europe's Quantum Shield: The Strategic view for a Continent-Wide Quantum Key Ditribution (QKD) Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22332)
*Leandros Maglaras,Ilias Papastamatiou,Alexios Aivaliotis,Evangelos Markatos,Konstantinos Karantzalos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了量子计算的快速发展给现有安全系统带来的风险，以及欧盟通过EuroQCI和SEEWQCI项目构建结合光纤、卫星通信和增强安全规则的数字防护体系，重点保护电网、医院等关键基础设施和欧洲公民数据安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算的快速发展既能带来科学突破，也能破解现有安全系统，对数字系统构成新威胁，因此需要构建抗量子安全基础设施。

Method: 通过欧盟的欧洲量子通信基础设施（EuroQCI）部署，以及SEEWQCI项目结合光纤电缆、卫星通信和增强安全规则，构建多层次数字防护体系。

Result: 形成了结合地面光纤、卫星通信和强化安全规则的综合性量子安全通信基础设施方案，专注于保护电网、医院等关键服务。

Conclusion: 欧盟通过EuroQCI和SEEWQCI项目构建的数字防护体系对保护欧洲公民数据安全和关键基础设施具有重要意义。

Abstract: The fast growth of quantum computing can lead to amazing scientific breakthroughs while on the same time can be used to break today's security systems, raising new risks for existing digital systems. Facing this challenge, the European's Union's deployement of the European Communication Infrastructure (EuroQCI) is crucial. The SEEWQCI project combines fiber cables, satellite communications and enhanced security rules to build a strong digital shield. Its focus is to protect vital services like power grids and hospitals keeping Europeans' data safe.

</details>


### [171] [A First Measurement Study on Authentication Security in Real-World Remote MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22333)
*Huijun Zhou,Xiaohan Zhang,Haozhe Zhang,Haoyang Zhang,Mi Zhang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对远程MCP服务器的认证安全进行了大规模测量研究，发现40.55%的服务器未实施认证，且所有测试的OAuth-enabled服务器均存在至少一个安全缺陷（共325个），其中动态客户端注册缺陷影响96.6%的服务器，可导致敏感信息泄露和账户接管。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP作为LLM与外部服务连接的通用接口日益重要，远程部署中客户端与服务器之间的认证边界安全关键但未被充分研究。

Method: 识别了7,973个活跃远程MCP服务器，提出包含3个MCP特有类别和传统OAuth错误配置共4类9种具体缺陷类型的分类体系，并实现结合被动流量检测与主动动态探测的半自动化检测框架。

Result: 40.55%的服务器无认证暴露工具；对119个可测试的OAuth-enabled服务器检测出325个缺陷，每个服务器至少有一个缺陷，动态客户端注册缺陷影响96.6%；已获得9个CVE ID。

Conclusion: MCP生态中存在普遍的认证弱点，亟需加强基于OAuth的远程部署安全。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is emerging as a common interface connecting large language models (LLMs) with external services. Remote deployments are becoming increasingly important as agents connect to user-linked online services, such as social, productivity, and financial services. In such deployments, the authentication boundary between MCP clients and remote servers becomes security-critical, yet remains underexplored.
  We present the first measurement study of authentication security in real-world remote MCP servers. We identify 7,973 live remote MCP servers, finding that 40.55% expose tools without authentication. Among authenticated servers, OAuth is the dominant authorization mechanism for reaching remote services, and OAuth deployments in the MCP ecosystem commonly exhibit three characteristics: open client environments, dynamic client registration, and delegated authorization. These characteristics distinguish MCP deployments from traditional OAuth and introduce new attack surfaces. Guided by this observation, we derive a taxonomy of authentication flaws comprising three MCP-specific categories and conventional OAuth misconfigurations, for a total of four categories and nine concrete flaw types. To evaluate these flaws at scale, we implement a semi-automated detection framework that combines passive traffic inspection with active dynamic probing. Applying it to 119 testable real-world OAuth-enabled MCP servers, we find that each server exhibits at least one flaw, with a total of 325 flaws identified, among which dynamic client registration flaws affect 96.6% of tested servers. Many of these flaws can lead to sensitive information leakage and account takeover. Through responsible disclosure, we obtained 9 CVE IDs. Our findings expose pervasive authentication weaknesses in the MCP ecosystem and underscore the urgent need for hardened OAuth-based remote deployments.

</details>


### [172] [TimeGuard: Channel-wise Pool Training for Backdoor Defense in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22365)
*Quang Duc Nguyen,Siyuan Liang,Yiming Li,Fushuo Huo,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了13种针对时间序列预测的后门防御方法，发现数据纠缠导致通道级信号稀释、任务公式化偏移导致训练损失退化两大根本问题，并提出TimeGuard防御方法，通过通道级池训练、时间感知初始化池和距离正则化损失选择，在保持干净性能的同时显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测在多个领域至关重要，但易受后门攻击；目前针对TSF的后门防御研究不足，面临数据纠缠和任务公式化偏移两大挑战。

Method: 提出TimeGuard方法：(1)采用通道级池训练（channel-wise pool training）作为核心范式；(2)利用时间感知标准初始化高置信度池以缓解信号稀释；(3)引入距离正则化损失选择，在训练过程中逐步扩展可靠池并缓解损失退化。

Result: 在多个数据集、预测架构和TSF后门攻击上，TimeGuard显著提升鲁棒性，MAE_P相比领先基线提升1.96倍，同时干净性能MAE_C保持在5%以内。

Conclusion: TimeGuard有效解决了TSF后门防御中数据纠缠和任务公式化偏移导致的根本问题，为时间序列预测领域提供了实用的训练时后门防御方案。

Abstract: Time Series Forecasting (TSF) plays a critical role across many domains, yet it is vulnerable to backdoor attacks. However, backdoor defenses tailored to TSF remain underexplored, due to data entanglement and task-formulation shift challenges. To fill this gap, we conduct a systematic evaluation of thirteen representative backdoor defenses across the TSF life cycle and analyze their failure modes. Our results reveal two fundamental issues: (1) data entanglement induces channel-level signal dilution, rendering sample-filtering and trigger-synthesis defenses ineffective at localizing backdoors; and (2) task-formulation shift leads to training-loss degeneration, causing poisoned and clean windows to become indistinguishable at training stages. Based on these findings, we propose a training-time backdoor defense for TSF, termed TimeGuard. Our method adopts channel-wise pool training as the core paradigm and initializes a high-confidence pool using time-aware criteria to mitigate signal dilution. Moreover, we introduce distance-regularized loss selection to progressively expand the reliable pool during training and ease loss degeneration. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, forecasting architectures, and TSF backdoor attacks demonstrate that TimeGuard substantially improves robustness, boosting $\mathrm{MAE}_\mathrm{P}$ by $1.96\times$ over the leading baseline, while preserving clean performance within 5% $\mathrm{MAE}_\mathrm{C}$.

</details>


### [173] [EnCAgg: Enhanced Clustering Aggregation for Robust Federated Learning against Dynamic Model Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22506)
*Tianyun Zhang,Zhen Yang,Haozhao Wang,Ru Zhang,Yongfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种利用少量已知良性客户端作为参考的鲁棒聚合方法，通过密度-based低维梯度聚类、增强聚类低维梯度生成器和低维梯度重聚类三步，在动态投毒场景下有效识别和过滤恶意梯度，同时保留尽可能多的良性梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临模型投毒攻击威胁，现有防御方法依赖固定阈值或固定聚类数，难以适应动态投毒策略，且常因客户端数据异质性导致良性梯度丢失。

Method: 1）密度-based低维梯度聚类：将梯度投影到最分散的两个维度上，利用密度聚类识别恶意梯度，保留聚类内良性梯度和潜在良性离群点；2）增强聚类低维梯度生成器：学习生成与良性聚类边界对齐的伪梯度，作为桥梁连接稀疏良性梯度离群点；3）低维梯度重聚类：将生成的伪梯度与真实梯度一起聚类，恢复被误分类为噪声的良性梯度。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和MIND数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在动态投毒场景下表现出优越的保真度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够有效应对未知且变化的恶意客户端数量，在抵御投毒攻击的同时最大化保留良性梯度参与聚合，提升了联邦学习的安全性和实用性。

Abstract: Federated learning faces increasing threats from model poisoning attacks, which harms its application to improve privacy. Existing defense methods typically rely on fixed thresholds or perform clustering with a fixed number of clusters to distinguish malicious gradients from benign ones. However, these methods are difficult to adapt to dynamic poisoning strategies of malicious clients, and often result in the loss of benign gradients due to the heterogeneity of clients' local datasets. To address these problems, we propose a novel robust aggregation method that leverages a small number of known benign clients as references, enabling accurate identification and filtering of malicious gradients while retaining as many benign gradients as possible, even when the number of malicious clients is unknown and variable. First, we introduce a density-based low-dimensional gradient clustering method, which projects gradients onto the two most divergent dimensions and applies density-based clustering to identify malicious gradients while retaining clustered benign gradients and potentially benign outliers. Second, we design an enhancing clustering low-dimensional gradient generator model, which learns to generate pseudo-gradients aligned with the boundary of the benign cluster. These pseudo-gradients act as bridges to connect sparse benign gradient outliers. Third, we introduce low-dimensional gradient re-clustering that clusters the generated pseudo-gradients together with real gradients to recover benign gradients misclassified as noise points, enabling more benign gradients to participate in aggregation. Extensive experiments on the MNIST, CIFAR-10, and MIND datasets demonstrate that our method exhibits superior fidelity and robustness under dynamic poisoning scenarios.

</details>


### [174] [Building an Open Source Operational Technology Pentesting Platform: Lessons from LINICS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22590)
*Awais Rashid,Joseph Gardiner,Louise Evans*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LINICS——首个针对运营技术（OT）安全测试的开源平台，填补了类似Kali Linux在OT领域缺失的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IT安全领域已有Kali Linux等成熟开源平台，但OT（运营技术）领域缺乏类似的开源安全测试平台，阻碍了工业控制系统安全研究。

Method: 作者架构、构建并发布了LINICS开源平台，用于OT渗透测试和安全分析，并分享了过程中的实践经验。

Result: 成功构建了LINICS开源平台，为OT渗透测试和安全分析提供了专用工具集。

Conclusion: LINICS填补了OT安全领域缺乏开源测试平台的空白，为工业控制系统安全研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Information Technology (IT) security professionals have ready access to open-source platforms such as Kali Linux. But no such platform exists for Operational Technology (OT) that underpins Industrial Control Systems. We discuss experiences of architecting, building and releasing LINICS, an open-source platform for OT pentesting and security analysis.

</details>


### [175] [TriSweep: A Four-Drone Swarm Framework for Electromagnetic Side-Channel Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22709)
*Eric Yocam,Varghese Vaidyan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TriSweep是一个四无人机编队仿真框架，用于对嵌入式微控制器进行远距离(0.25-1.5 m)电磁侧信道分析。通过三架专用采集无人机(Anchor全频谱、Mask Probe掩码寄存器泄露、Cipher Probe掩码SubBytes输出泄露)和一架汇聚无人机实现相干合并(+4.8 dB SNR增益)与二阶掩码消除，在ANSSI ASCAD数据集上模拟密钥排名达到18±1.7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电磁侧信道分析假设探测探头静止且近距离贴近目标，这种威胁模型低估了空中对手的能力。本文旨在证明无人机编队可以在远距离实现有效的电磁侧信道攻击，突破传统威胁模型的局限。

Method: 采用四无人机编队架构：(1) Anchor无人机采集全频谱EM信号；(2) Mask Probe无人机采集掩码寄存器加载泄露；(3) Cipher Probe无人机采集掩码后SubBytes输出泄露；(4) Accumulator汇聚无人机对三路信号进行相干合并(+4.8 dB SNR增益)和二阶掩码消除(两路空间分离泄露流的中心化乘积)。使用基于profiling迹的互相关对齐补偿无人机悬停振动，双通道CNN用于去同步数据集分析。

Result: 在ANSSI ASCAD ATmega8515掩码AES-128数据集上，0.25 m距离模拟密钥排名为18±1.7(五种子)。100样本抖动变体上，profiling迹互相关对齐将单无人机排名从89降至21。双通道CNN在去同步数据集上损失收敛至0.454(随机基线5.545)，排名显著改善。尚未制造物理硬件。

Conclusion: TriSweep框架证明了无人机编队可用于远距离自主电磁侧信道分析，通过空间分离采集和相干处理可有效对抗掩码防护和信号去同步等防御措施。原型构建为下一步工作计划。

Abstract: Electromagnetic (EM) side-channel analysis traditionally assumes a stationary, close-proximity probe - a threat model that underestimates aerial adversaries. TriSweep is a simulation framework that designs and evaluates a four-drone swarm architecture for autonomous standoff EM-SCA of embedded microcontrollers at 0.25-1.5 m. Three spatially specialized collector drones - Anchor (full-spectrum), Mask Probe (mask-register loading leakage), and Cipher Probe (masked SubBytes output leakage) - feed a stationary Accumulator drone that performs coherent combining (+4.8 dB SNR gain) and second-order mask cancellation via a centered product of the two spatially separated leakage streams. Evaluated against three real ANSSI ASCAD datasets (ATmega8515 masked AES-128 and 50/100-sample desynchronized variants), the framework achieves a simulated key rank of 18 +/- 1.7 (five-seed) at 0.25 m on the primary masked dataset. Profiling-trace cross-correlation alignment reduces single-drone rank from 89 to 21 on the 100-sample-jitter variant, demonstrating compensation for drone hover vibration. A two-channel CNN in the Accumulator converges to a loss of 0.454 (vs. random baseline 5.545) and improves rank on desynchronized datasets. No physical hardware has been fabricated; prototype construction is the planned next step.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Finite-Particle Convergence Rates for Conservative and Non-Conservative Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22795)
*Krishnakumar Balasubramanian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出并分析了一种用于一步生成建模的保守漂移方法。该方法将原始位移驱动的漂移速度替换为核密度估计梯度速度，证明了该保守方法在R^d上的连续时间有限粒子收敛界，并与非保守方法进行了对比分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一般位移驱动漂移场存在的非保守性问题，即原始漂移速度不是梯度场，可能导致生成建模中的不理想行为。

Method: 将原始位移驱动速度替换为核密度估计梯度速度（核平滑数据得分与核平滑模型得分之差），该速度为梯度场；分别分析了保守方法（KDE梯度速度）和非保守方法（Laplace核位移速度）的理论性质。

Result: 建立了连续时间有限粒子收敛界：联合熵恒等式给出了经验Stein漂移、KDE平滑Fisher散度和中心速度平方的界；主要有限粒子修正项是倒数KDE自相互作用项；在均匀正则条件下残差速度为N^{-1/(d+4)}率，一般增长条件下为N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}率；非保守方法存在不可避免的残差项。

Conclusion: 连续时间残差速度界通过显式漂移大小η可转化为一步生成保证；保守方法在理论上优于非保守方法。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a conservative drifting method for one-step generative modeling. The method replaces the original displacement-based drifting velocity by a kernel density estimator (KDE)-gradient velocity, namely the difference of the kernel-smoothed data score and the kernel-smoothed model score. This velocity is a gradient field, addressing the non-conservatism issue identified for general displacement-based drifting fields. We prove continuous-time finite-particle convergence bounds for the conservative method on $\R^d$: a joint-entropy identity yields bounds for the empirical Stein drift, the smoothed Fisher discrepancy of the KDE, and the squared center velocity. The main finite-particle correction is a reciprocal-KDE self-interaction term, and we give deterministic and high-probability local-occupancy conditions under which this term is controlled. We keep the quadrature constants explicit and track their possible bandwidth dependence: the root residual-velocity rate $N^{-1/(d+4)}$ holds under an additional $h$-uniform quadrature regularity condition, while a more general growth condition yields the optimized root rate $N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}$, where $0\le β<2$. We also analyze the non-conservative drifting method with Laplace kernel, corresponding to the original displacement-based velocity proposed in~\cite{deng2026drifting}. For this method, a sharp companion kernel decomposes the velocity into a positive scalar preconditioning of a sharp-score mismatch plus a Laplace scale-mismatch residual, producing an analogous finite-particle rate with an unavoidable residual term. Finally, we explain how the continuous-time residual-velocity bounds translate into one-step generation guarantees through the explicit drift size $η$.

</details>


### [177] [Local Covariate Selection for Average Causal Effect Estimation without Pretreatment and Causal Sufficiency Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21548)
*Zeyu Liu,Zheng Li,Feng Xie,Yan Zeng,Hao Zhang,Kun Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需预处理假设和因果充分性假设的局部学习方法，用于因果效应估计中的协变量选择，通过刻画局部边界并开发局部识别程序，实现了高效且准确的因果效应估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果效应估计方法依赖全局因果结构学习或强假设（如因果充分性、预处理假设），这些条件在实际中往往不成立，且全局学习在高维场景下计算成本过高。

Method: 首先刻画一个局部边界，该边界包含至少一个有效调整集（当存在时）；然后在该边界内开发局部识别程序以高效搜索有效调整集。

Result: 在多个合成数据集和两个真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法实现了准确的因果效应估计，同时显著提升了计算效率。

Conclusion: 该方法在避免了预处理假设和因果充分性假设的同时，是完备且正确的，为高维场景下的因果效应估计提供了一种实用的局部学习方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of selecting covariates for unbiased estimation of the total causal effect.Existing approaches typically rely on global causal structure learning over all variables, or on strong assumptions such as causal sufficiency - where observed variables share no latent confounders - or the pretreatment assumption, which limits covariates to those unaffected by the treatment or outcome. These requirements are often unrealistic in practice, and global learning becomes computationally prohibitive in high-dimensional settings.To address these challenges, we propose a novel local learning method for covariate selection in nonparametric causal effect estimation that avoids both the pretreatment and causal sufficiency assumptions. We first characterize a local boundary that contains at least one valid adjustment set whenever one exists for identifying the causal effect, and then develop local identification procedures to efficiently search within this boundary.We prove that the proposed method is sound and complete. Experiments on multiple synthetic datasets and two real-world datasets show that our approach achieves accurate causal effect estimation while substantially improving computational efficiency.

</details>


### [178] [Support-aware offline policy selection for advertising marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21736)
*Prashant Shekhar,Caroline Howard*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种支持感知的离线决策框架，用于广告竞价中的保留价策略选择。该框架不输出单一的点估计优胜者，而是将日志证据转化为保守决策对象（包括认证策略、统计支配替代方案和待验证候选方案），并给出了统一的有限目录保证。实验表明，该框架能将19个策略的目录缩减为2个策略的验证候选名单，同时在44个广告主、交易平台和区域细分中证明非伤害性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的重放表（replay tables）和离线策略评估方法虽然能估计或排序策略价值，但无法直接回答"现有证据是否足以证明验证的合理性"这一操作性问题，并且存在弱阈值支持、多重比较效应、子群伤害和竞价者响应不确定性等风险。

Method: 提出支持感知的离线决策框架，将日志证据转化为三种输出：（1）认证策略（certified policies）、（2）统计支配的替代方案（statistically dominated alternatives）、（3）待验证候选方案（unresolved candidates）。核心理论结果是在同时不确定性控制和保守支持门控（conservative support gates）下，框架保留最佳通过门控策略的同时仅消除有认证遗憾的策略。支持结果包括：支持局部化重放泛化、信息论阈值分辨率极限、异质竞价者响应对局部化重放排序的影响。

Result: 在iPinYou实时竞价日志上，领先保留规则在第二季实现47.66%的重放提升、40.71%的同时下界提升，第三季实现43.87%的冻结超时重放提升。框架将19个策略目录缩减为2个策略的验证候选名单，并在44个细分中证明非伤害性。

Conclusion: 离线保留价策略评估应产生认证验证决策，而非仅仅点估计排名。

Abstract: Logged advertising auctions make offline reserve-price evaluation attractive but risky. Replay tables can identify policies with large apparent yield gains, yet they can also hide weak threshold support, multiple-comparison effects, subgroup harm, and bidder-response uncertainty. Existing replay and off-policy evaluation methods estimate or rank policy values, but they do not directly answer the operational question of whether the available evidence is strong enough to justify validation. This paper develops a support-aware offline decision framework for reserve-policy selection. Rather than outputting a single point-estimate winner, the framework converts logged evidence into a conservative decision object consisting of certified policies, statistically dominated alternatives, and unresolved candidates requiring further validation. The main theoretical result gives a unified finite-catalog guarantee showing that, under simultaneous uncertainty control and conservative support gates, the framework preserves the best gate-passing policy while eliminating only policies with certified regret. Supporting results characterize support-localized replay generalization, establish information-theoretic threshold-resolution limits, and quantify when heterogeneous bidder response can overturn localized replay rankings. Experiments on iPinYou real-time-bidding logs show that the leading reserve rule achieves a 47.66% replay lift in season two, a 40.71% simultaneous lower-bound lift, and a 43.87% frozen out-of-time replay lift in season three. The framework reduces a 19-policy catalog to a two-policy validation shortlist while certifying non-harm across 44 advertiser, exchange, and region segments. The results support the central claim that offline reserve-policy evaluation should produce certified validation decisions rather than point-estimate rankings alone.

</details>


### [179] [From Betting to Empirical Bernstein LIL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22124)
*Francesco Orabona*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文是2017-2018年的一份技术报告，利用在线投注策略的财富保证来推导迭代对数定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过在线投注策略的财富保证这一概率论工具，重新推导迭代对数定律，提供一种新的证明视角。

Method: 采用在线投注策略（在线赌博策略）的财富保证作为核心工具，从博弈论角度推导迭代对数定律。

Result: 成功利用在线投注策略的财富保证重新证明了迭代对数定律。

Conclusion: 在线投注策略的财富保证可以作为推导迭代对数定律的有效工具，为经典概率论结果提供了一种基于博弈论的证明方法。

Abstract: This is a verbatim copy of a technical report I wrote in 2017-2018 to obtain the law of the iterated logarithm using the guarantee on the wealth of an online betting strategy.

</details>


### [180] [Do Not Trust The Auctioneer: Learning to Bid in Feedback-Manipulated Auctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22438)
*Luigi Foscari,Matilde Tullii,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究重复一价拍卖中虚假投标（Shilling）仅影响学习者的观测反馈而不影响分配结果的问题。假设已知虚假投标分布，提出一个结合鲁棒区间消除分支（实现动态定价速率$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$）与乐观分支（利用去偏失标侧信息实现一价拍卖速率$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$）的算法，并给出匹配下界。结果表明仅反馈层面的虚假投标也会显著改变重复竞价问题的统计难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究重复一价拍卖中，仅影响反馈而不影响分配的虚假投标（Shilling）如何改变学习者的观测信息，进而影响其学习策略和遗憾界，揭示反馈操纵对统计难度的影响。

Method: 提出结合鲁棒区间消除分支（忽略虚假报告，达$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$速率）与乐观分支（对失标侧报告去偏，利用可靠的后缀信息，达$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$速率）的算法，通过验证与竞赛过程在不知先验尺度或反馈几何条件下自适应选择更新策略。

Result: 算法在已知虚假投标分布下实现自适应遗憾界；在单活跃区域情形给出匹配下界（对数因子内）。

Conclusion: 仅影响反馈的虚假投标会显著改变重复竞价问题的统计难度。

Abstract: Shilling is the use of artificial bids to make competition appear stronger and push prices upward. We study repeated first-price auctions in which shilling affects feedback but not allocation: the learner wins or loses against the real competing bid, but after a loss observes the maximum of the real bid and an independent shill bid. Thus the manipulation changes what the learner observes and hence how it learns to bid, without changing the outcome of the current auction. We analyze regret with respect to the best bid benchmark, assuming that the shill-bid distribution is known. Even then, shilling can mask the real bid, while useful side information appears only through intermittent low-shill events. Our algorithm combines a robust interval-elimination branch, which ignores the shilled report and achieves the dynamic-pricing rate $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{2/3})$, with an optimistic branch that debiases losing-side reports and exploits the resulting suffix information when it is reliable and achieves the first-price auctions rate $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$. A validation and racing procedure lets the algorithm use these optimistic updates without knowing the right scale or feedback geometry in advance. We complement the upper bounds with a matching lower bound, up to logarithmic factors, in the single-active-region case. Overall, the results show that even feedback-only shilling can sharply alter the statistical difficulty of repeated bidding.

</details>
