### [181] [A Martingale Kernel Independence Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22549)
*Felix Laumann,Zhaolu Liu,Mauricio Barahona*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对HSIC及其联合独立性扩展dHSIC的置换校准时耗时长的问题，提出了两种学生化统计量mHSIC和mdHSIC，其零分布为标准正态，无需置换检验，计算速度提升25-60倍，且保持相同的检验功效和I类错误控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HSIC和dHSIC是退化V-统计量，其零分布为数据依赖的加权χ²分布，迫使使用置换校准，每次检验成本需乘以置换次数（通常两个数量级），计算负担沉重。

Method: 1) mHSIC：自归一化的下三角求和，使用Hadamard积对两个经验中心化Gram矩阵进行处理，在独立性和有界四阶矩核假设下收敛到标准正态，无需样本拆分，二次计算复杂度。2) mdHSIC：采用半样本拆分，一半估计中心化，另一半运行下三角自归一化鞅，将条件均值残差压缩为d指数级小量，对每个固定的联合检验变量数渐近标准正态，每检验成本随d线性增长。

Result: 在合成数据上（每变量输入维度1-500，联合检验变量数2-10），两种统计量在经验I类错误率和检验功效上与置换校准基线匹配，运行速度快25-60倍。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过学生化构造消除了对置换检验的依赖，大幅提升了（联合）独立性检验的计算效率，同时保持了统计有效性。

Abstract: The Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion (HSIC) and its joint-independence extension $d\mathrm{HSIC}$ are degenerate $V$-statistics whose data-dependent weighted-$χ^2$ null limits force a permutation calibration that multiplies the per-test cost by the number of permutations, in practice two orders of magnitude. Adapting the recent martingale MMD construction for two-sample testing to the (joint) independence problem, we introduce two studentised statistics whose null distributions are standard normal regardless of the data law, so that a single normal-quantile lookup replaces the permutation step entirely. The first, $m\mathrm{HSIC}$, is a self-normalised lower-triangular sum of the Hadamard product of two empirically centred Gram matrices. Under independence and bounded-fourth-moment kernels it converges to a standard normal. It is consistent against every fixed alternative, and runs at quadratic cost in the sample size without any sample split, matching the biased HSIC $V$-statistic. Our second statistic, $md\mathrm{HSIC}$, achieves finite-sample consistency with a single half-sample split: the centring is estimated on one half and the lower-triangular self-normalised martingale is run on the other, shrinking the conditional-mean residual to a quantity that is exponentially small in $d$, so the statistic is asymptotically standard normal at every fixed number of jointly tested variables, with a per-test cost that grows only linearly in $d$. On synthetic data with per-variable input dimension from $1$ to $500$ and between $2$ and $10$ jointly tested variables, both statistics match the empirical type-I error rate and test power of permutation-calibrated baselines while running $25$ to $60\times$ faster.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Robust Statistical Estimators with Bounded Empirical Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21860)
*Valentio Iverson,Gautam Kamath,Argyris Mouzakis,Adam Smith*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"经验敏感性"的统计估计量稳健性新度量，并针对高斯均值估计问题研究了其界限，证明了达到最优误差的估计量其经验敏感度的下界为Ω(η + √(ηd/n))，且该界在log因子下是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统计估计量的稳健性度量缺乏在有限样本下对数据扰动敏感性的精细刻画，作者希望引入一种新的、以高概率成立的稳健性度量——经验敏感性，以更好地理解估计量在数据被少量污染时的行为。

Method: 定义经验敏感性为：以高概率，将数据集中最多ηn个点修改后，估计量的变化不超过某值。针对高斯均值估计问题，通过Efron-Stein论证等手段证明了下界Ω(η + √(ηd/n))，并利用鲁棒经验均值估计的最新结果证明该下界紧。

Result: 对于高斯均值估计，任何达到最优ℓ₂误差O(√(d/n))的估计量，其经验敏感度至少为Ω(η + √(ηd/n))，其中两项分别来自均值和方差上的障碍。该下界在log因子意义下是紧的。

Conclusion: 经验敏感性是衡量估计量对数据扰动敏感度的有效新工具，在高斯均值估计中给出了精确的界限，为鲁棒统计推断提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We introduce a new measure of robustness for statistical estimators, which we call \emph{empirical sensitivity}. An estimator $\hat θ$ has bounded empirical sensitivity if, with high probability over a dataset $X = (X_1, \dots, X_n) \sim \mathcal{D}^{\otimes n}$, for any dataset $Y$ obtained by modifying at most $ηn$ points in $X$, we have that $\hat θ(Y)$ is close to $\hat θ(X)$.
  We study bounds on this quantity for the prototypical problem of Gaussian mean estimation. We prove new lower bounds, showing that for any estimator $\hat μ$ which achieves an optimal $\ell_2$-error bound of $O\left(\sqrt{d/n}\right)$, the empirical sensitivity is at least $Ω\left(η+ \sqrt{ηd/n}\right)$. The two terms arise due to obstructions on the mean and variance (via an Efron-Stein argument) of such an estimator. We show that this bound is tight up to logarithmic factors, by employing recent results for robust empirical mean estimation.

</details>


### [183] [A Circular Chatterjee's Correlation Coefficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22062)
*Sourav Majumdar*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对圆形数据提出了Chatterjee秩相关的圆形版本，通过在圆形秩空间中平均响应切割点，消除了传统方法中任意切割点选择的问题。该系数保持了方向性和原始解释，在非原子圆形边际下独立时为零、完全函数依赖时为1，并证明了相合性和零假设下的分布无关性。模拟显示该方法在检测多圈缠绕关系（如响应绕圈2或4次）时特别有效，而标准圆形相关系数几乎失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chatterjee的秩相关在实数数据上表现良好，但应用于圆形数据时需要任意切割圆环展开为线性区间，不同切割点会导致不同的有限样本结果，而底层圆形关系并未改变。因此需要一种内在依赖于圆形序数、不依赖任意切割选择的圆形版系数。

Method: 在总体层面，对响应在圆形秩空间中进行切割平均化；在有限样本层面，对样本切割间隙进行平均，最终简化为仅基于循环秩的简单统计量。该系数保留了Chatterjee原始系数的方向性和解释性。

Result: 在非原子圆形边际下，该系数在独立时严格为零，在圆形响应是圆形预测子的可测函数时严格为一。证明了相合性，并推导了独立零假设下与分布无关的极限行为。模拟显示，对于多圈缠绕关系（响应绕圈2或4次），所提系数显著优于标准圆形相关系数。

Conclusion: 提出的圆形Chatterjee系数克服了传统方法对切割点选择的依赖，本质上适用于圆形数据的循环序数结构，尤其在检测复杂多圈函数关系方面具有显著优势，是对圆形相关性分析的重要补充。

Abstract: Chatterjee's rank correlation is a directed measure of association designed to detect whether one variable can be predicted as a function of another. While the original coefficient is naturally defined for real-valued data, circular data poses additional difficulty. Applying the usual construction requires cutting each circle at an arbitrary point and treating it as a line. Different choices of cut points can lead to different finite-sample values, even though the underlying circular relationship is unchanged. This paper proposes a circular version of Chatterjee's coefficient that removes this arbitrary choice. The population construction averages over response cuts in circular rank space, and the finite-sample construction averages over sample cut gaps and reduces to a simple statistic based only on cyclic ranks. The resulting coefficient is intrinsic to the circular ordering of the data, remains directed, and retains the key interpretation of Chatterjee's original coefficient. Under non-atomic circular marginals, it is zero exactly under independence and one exactly when the circular response is a measurable function of the circular predictor. We prove consistency and derive its distribution-free null behavior under independence. Simulations show that the proposed coefficient is especially useful for detecting multi-winding circular relationships, such as cases where the response goes around the circle twice or four times as the predictor goes around once, where standard circular correlations can be nearly blind.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [The Attribution Impossibility: No Feature Ranking Is Faithful, Stable, and Complete Under Collinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21492)
*Drake Caraker,Bryan Arnold,David Rhoads*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了当特征存在共线性时，没有特征排序方法能同时满足忠实性、稳定性和完备性，并提出DASH（Diversified Aggregation of SHAP）作为帕累托最优的集成方法，同时通过Lean 4定理证明器进行了305条定理的形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示特征归因排序在共线性条件下的固有限制，证明忠实性、稳定性和完备性三者不可兼得，并解决由此导致的归因不稳定问题。

Method: 通过理论证明（不可能性定理、设计空间刻画）、量化分析（四种模型类的归因比率发散行为）、提出DASH集成方法（达到Cramer-Rao方差下界）、以及Lean 4形式化验证（305条定理、16条公理、0个sorry）。

Result: 共线性对归因排序的影响被量化：梯度提升中归因比率按1/(1-ρ²)发散，Lasso无穷大，随机森林收敛；77个数据集中68%存在归因不稳定性；DASH在无偏聚合中达到帕累托最优。

Conclusion: 共线性导致特征排序不可靠，SHAP的公平性审计在此条件下不可信；条件SHAP无法规避该不可能性；DASH提供了稳定且最优的解决方案；这是可解释AI领域首个形式化验证的不可能性定理。

Abstract: No feature ranking can be simultaneously faithful, stable, and complete when features are collinear. For collinear pairs, ranking reduces to a coin flip. We prove this impossibility, quantify it for four model classes, resolve it via ensemble averaging (DASH), and machine-verify it with 305 Lean 4 theorems. We characterize the complete attribution design space: exactly two families of methods exist -- faithful-complete methods (unstable, with rankings that flip up to 50% of the time) and ensemble methods like DASH (stable, reporting ties for symmetric features) -- and no method lies outside this dichotomy. The impossibility is quantitative: the attribution ratio diverges as 1/(1-rho^2) for gradient boosting, is infinite for Lasso, and converges for random forests. DASH (Diversified Aggregation of SHAP) is provably Pareto-optimal among unbiased aggregations, achieving the Cramer-Rao variance bound with a tight ensemble size formula. In a survey of 77 public datasets, 68% exhibit attribution instability. Switching to conditional SHAP does not escape the impossibility when features have equal causal effects. The framework includes practical diagnostics -- a Z-test workflow and single-model screening tool -- and has direct consequences for fairness auditing: SHAP-based proxy discrimination audits are provably unreliable under collinearity. The design space theorem, diagnostics, and impossibility are mechanically verified in Lean 4 (305 theorems from 16 axioms, 0 sorry) -- to our knowledge, the first formally verified impossibility in explainable AI.

</details>


### [185] [Expectation Consistency Loss: Rethink Confidence Calibration under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21552)
*Jinzong Dong,Zhaohui Jiang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在协变量偏移下实现置信度校准的新方法。首先推导了协变量偏移下置信度校准的充要条件——期望一致性条件，揭示了协变量偏移不一定会导致未校准的置信度。随后基于该条件提出了期望一致性损失（ECL），一种无监督域自适应损失函数，兼容多种校准类型。理论上证明了ECL与期望校准误差（ECE）具有相同的样本复杂度，并提供了可小批量训练的机制。在模拟和真实数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度校准方法假设训练和测试数据独立同分布，限制了其在协变量偏移下的有效性。已有的协变量偏移校准方法存在类级校准困难、密度比估计不稳定等问题。

Method: 1) 推导了协变量偏移下置信度校准的充要条件——期望一致性条件；2) 基于该条件提出期望一致性损失（ECL），一种无监督域自适应损失；3) 理论上证明ECL与ECE具有相同样本复杂度，并设计了可小批量训练的优化方案。

Result: 在模拟和真实协变量偏移数据集上验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 协变量偏移不一定导致置信度未校准，期望一致性条件提供了比全局分布对齐更弱的校准条件；ECL损失兼容多种校准类型且具有理论保障。

Abstract: Confidence calibration for classification models is vital in safety-critical decision-making scenarios and has received extensive attention. General confidence calibration methods assume training and test data are independent and identically distributed, limiting their effectiveness under covariate shifts. Previous calibration methods under covariate shift struggle with class-wise or canonical calibrations and often rely on unstable importance weighting when density ratios are large or unbounded. Given the above limitations, this paper rethinks confidence calibration under covariate shifts. First, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for confidence calibration under covariate shifts, named Expectation consistency condition, which reveals covariate shifts do not necessarily lead to uncalibrated confidence and provides a weaker condition for confidence calibration than global covariate distribution alignment. Then, utilizing Expectation consistency condition, this paper proposes an unsupervised domain adaptation loss to calibrate confidence of the target domain, named Expectation consistency loss (ECL), which is compatible with canonical calibration, class-wise calibration, and top-label calibration. Third, we prove that computing ECL loss has the same sample complexity as Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and provide a theoretically grounded mini-batch trainable scheme for ECL loss. Finally, we validate the effectiveness of our method on both simulated and real-world covariate shift datasets.

</details>


### [186] [On the Sample Complexity of Discounted Reinforcement Learning with Optimized Certainty Equivalents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21763)
*Oliver Mortensen,Mohammad Sadegh Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限折扣MDP中基于优化确定性等价（OCE）风险度量的风险敏感强化学习，利用生成模型，给出了递归OCE下最优值函数和策略学习的PAC样本复杂度，刻画了可学习性条件并建立了紧的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险敏感强化学习在实际应用中至关重要，但针对OCE这类通用风险度量族的学习理论尚不完善。本文旨在系统性地刻画递归OCE下值函数与策略学习的样本复杂度，明确哪些风险度量是PAC可学习的，并建立紧的上下界。

Method: 采用基于生成模型的model-based方法进行分析。先对效用函数u进行精确刻画，区分dom(u)=R和dom(u)≠R两种情况；再推导PAC样本复杂度上界；最后建立值学习和策略学习的下界。

Result: 1) 当dom(u)≠R时，对应问题不可PAC学习；2) 给出了PAC可学习的充要条件；3) 下界在状态-动作空间大小SA上紧；4) 对CVaR_τ，得到了1/τ²的正确依赖，较此前最优结果提升了一个1/τ因子，但在1/(1-γ)依赖上非最优。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了递归OCE下风险敏感强化学习的可学习性边界，为相关风险度量提供了紧的样本复杂度理论，同时指出了有效折扣因子依赖上的改进空间。

Abstract: We study risk-sensitive reinforcement learning in finite discounted MDPs, where a generative model of the MDP is assumed to be available. We consider a family or risk measures called the optimized certainty equivalent (OCE), which includes important risk measures such as entropic risk, CVaR, and mean-variance. Our focus is on the sample complexities of learning the optimal state-action value function (value learning) and an optimal policy (policy learning) under recursive OCE. We provide an exact characterization of utility functions $u$ for which the corresponding OCE defines an objective that is PAC-learnable. We analyze a simple model-based approach and derive PAC sample complexity bounds. We establish that whenever $u$ does not have full domain $\text{dom}(u)\neq \mathbb{R}$, the corresponding problem is not PAC-learnable. Finally, we establish corresponding lower bounds for both value and policy learning, demonstrating tightness in the size $SA$ of state-action space, and for a more restricted class of utilities, we derive lower bounds that makes the dependence on the effective horizon $\frac{1}{1-γ}$ explicit. Specifically, for $\text{CVaR}_τ$ we show that the correct dependence on $τ$ is $\frac{1}{τ^2}$, thus improving by a factor of $\frac{1}τ$ over state-of-the-art although our bound has a suboptimal dependence on $\frac{1}{1-γ}$.

</details>


### [187] [MMD-Balls as Credal Sets: A PAC-Bayesian Framework for Epistemic Uncertainty in Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21783)
*Ahanaf Hasan Ariq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种PAC-Bayesian框架，通过最大均值差异（MMD）量化源域与目标域之间的分布偏移，并利用Walley精确概率理论将MMD球解释为信度集，从而为测试时自适应方法提供形式化的泛化保证和不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时自适应方法缺乏将分布偏移大小与预测可靠性联系起来的正式理论保证，无法回答“何时应该进行自适应”这一关键决策问题。

Method: (i) 在RKHS-Lipschitz损失假设下，推导出含MMD偏移惩罚项的PAC-Bayesian界；(ii) 通过MMD浓度不等式给出有限样本版本；(iii) 在信度集上建立均匀最坏情况风险界，并进行下-上风险分解；(iv) 推导测地线保持界，解释核引导自适应如何保护局部特征几何结构。

Result: 建立了MMD参数化的泛化边界；将信度集解释用于分离认知不确定性与偶然不确定性；提供了何时进行自适应的原则性决策准则。

Conclusion: 该框架为测试时自适应提供了形式化的理论基础，能够判断自适应是否必要，并量化预测的不确定性。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) methods improve model performance under distribution shift but lack formal guarantees connecting shift magnitude to prediction reliability. We develop a PAC-Bayesian framework yielding generalization bounds explicitly parameterized by the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) between source and target distributions. Our principal contribution is interpreting MMD-balls around the source distribution as credal sets in Walley's imprecise probability theory, yielding natural epistemic uncertainty quantification. We establish: (i) a PAC-Bayesian bound with an MMD-dependent shift penalty under an RKHS-Lipschitz loss assumption; (ii) a finite-sample version via MMD concentration; (iii) a uniform worst-case risk bound over all distributions in the credal set, with a lower-upper risk decomposition; and (iv) geodesic preservation bounds explaining why kernel-guided adaptation protects local feature geometry. The credal set interpretation separates epistemic from aleatoric uncertainty and provides a principled decision criterion for when adaptation is warranted.

</details>


### [188] [Aerodynamic force reconstruction using physics-informed Gaussian processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22111)
*Gledson Rodrigo Tondo,Igor Kavrakov,Guido Morgenthal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种概率物理信息机器学习方法，用于从含噪声的结构动力响应测量中重构气动荷载，并以Great Belt East Bridge为例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气动荷载建模常依赖对真实物理力的简化假设，导致模型精度受限；且在有噪声或不完整数据条件下，模型验证极具挑战性。

Method: 采用概率物理信息机器学习方法，避免过拟合和正则化，支持异构和多保真度数据训练，从含噪声结构动力响应中重构气动荷载。

Result: 在Great Belt East Bridge的线性非定常假设下，预测荷载与真实荷载高度吻合，在均方根误差、幅值、相位角和峰值等指标上表现良好。

Conclusion: 该方法在模型验证、未来荷载估计和结构损伤预测等方面具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of aerodynamic loads is essential for understanding and predicting the responses of complex structural systems. However, these models often rely on simplifications of the true physical forces, introducing assumptions that can limit their accuracy. Validating such models becomes particularly challenging in the presence of noisy or incomplete data. To address this, we introduce a probabilistic physics-informed machine learning approach designed to reconstruct the underlying aerodynamic loads from noisy measurements of structural dynamic responses. The model avoids overfitting, eliminates the need for regularization schemes, and allows for the use of heterogeneous and multi-fidelity data during the training process. The efficacy of the approach is demonstrated through the reconstruction of aerodynamic loads on the Great Belt East Bridge, simulated under a linear unsteady assumption. Results show a strong agreement between true and predicted loads, particularly related to root mean squared errors, magnitude, phase angle and peak values of the signals. The method for load reconstructing holds broad applicability, such as modeling validation, future load estimation, and structural damage prognosis.

</details>


### [189] [From Sequential Nodes to GPU Batches: Parallel Branch and Bound for Optimal $k$-Sparse GLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22188)
*Jiachang Liu,Andrea Lodi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种CPU-GPU协同框架，通过批量处理分支定界节点和轻量级自定义内核，实现带基数约束广义线性模型最优解认证的一到两个数量级加速，并可扩展至Rashomon全集收集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU加速在连续优化中成功，但在离散变量、组合结构及非线性目标的优化问题（如基数约束广义线性模型的最优解认证）中面临挑战，主要困难包括分支定界中异构节点的顺序处理以及CPU-GPU间频繁数据传输。

Method: 提出一个简单、通用、模块化的CPU-GPU框架，在GPU上批量处理多个BnB节点；基于少量GPU高效例程，利用填充（padding）和轻量级自定义内核处理不规则节点数据结构。

Result: 在困难实例上实现一到两个数量级的加速，且最优性间隙为零。

Conclusion: 该框架可扩展至收集整个Rashomon集合，支持下游统计分析如变量重要性分析和基于用户指定次要指标（如分类AUC）的模型选择。

Abstract: GPUs have significantly accelerated first-order methods for large-scale optimization, especially in continuous optimization. However, this success has not transferred cleanly to problems with discrete variables, combinatorial structure, and nonlinear objectives, such as certifying optimal solutions for cardinality-constrained generalized linear models. Major challenges include the sequential processing of heterogeneous nodes in branch and bound (BnB) and frequent data movement between the CPU and GPU. We propose a simple, generic, and modular CPU--GPU framework that processes multiple BnB nodes in batches on GPUs. The framework is built around a small set of GPU-efficient routines and uses padding together with lightweight custom kernels to handle irregular node data structures. Experiments show one to two orders of magnitude speedups and zero optimality gap on challenging instances. The framework can also be extended to collect the entire Rashomon set, enabling downstream statistical analysis such as variable-importance analysis and model selection under secondary user-specific measures (e.g., AUC in classification).

</details>


### [190] [Generative Modeling by Value-Driven Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22507)
*Pablo Moreno-Muñoz,Adrian Müller,Gergely Neu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散时间随机控制公式的生成建模新框架，通过线性规划对偶形式求解最优价值函数，进而得到价值驱动传输（VDT）策略，实现快速、鲁棒的直线传输路径，并在多个实验中展现了良好的性能和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流、扩散或Schrödinger桥的方法在生成路径上存在仿真效率低、路径弯曲等问题，需要一种能生成直线传输路径、仿真快速鲁棒且易于扩展的生成建模框架。

Method: 将测度传输问题建模为离散时间随机控制问题，利用控制理论中的经典结果将其转化为线性规划，通过对偶变量（最优价值函数）直接编码最优控制策略；开发了一种免仿真的原始-对偶算法来近似求解最优价值函数及相应的VDT策略。

Result: VDT策略能够生成直线传输路径，仿真快速且鲁棒；支持条件生成、无分类器引导、无配对数据翻译等扩展功能；在多个实验任务中表现优异，具有良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: VDT框架在生成路径质量和计算效率上优于流、扩散和Schrödinger桥等方法，是一种有前景的生成建模新范式。

Abstract: We propose a new framework for generative modeling based on a discrete-time stochastic control formulation of measure transport. Adapting classic results from control theory, we formulate our problem as a linear program whose dual variables correspond to the \emph{optimal value function} of the control problem, which directly encodes the optimal control policy. Exploiting this LP formulation, we develop an efficient simulation-free primal-dual algorithm for computing approximately optimal value functions and the associated \emph{value-driven transport} (VDT) policies which approximate the true optimal policy. We show that well-trained VDT policies enjoy numerous favorable properties in comparison with other state-of-the-art methods based on flows, diffusions, or Schrödinger bridges: they lead to straight transport paths which can be simulated quickly and robustly, and can be enhanced in all the same ways as diffusion and flow-based models (e.g., conditional generation, classifier-free guidance, unpaired data-to-data translation are all easy to incorporate). We evaluate our methodology in a range of experiments, with results that indicate strong performance and good potential for scalability.

</details>


### [191] [Multiple Neural Operators Achieve Near-Optimal Rates for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22724)
*Adrien Weihs,Hayden Schaeffer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多任务算子学习（MNO架构）的近似与统计复杂度，证明共享表示不会增加总体学习成本，多任务与单任务算子学习遵循相同缩放律，且MNO与多任务DeepONet在渐近率上等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多任务环境下算子集合学习的近似与统计复杂度，特别是验证共享表示是否会引入额外代价。

Method: 对Lipschitz多算子映射类推导近似与统计泛化的近优上界，同时建立参数复杂度诅咒并证明极小化极大下界。

Result: 多任务算子学习与单任务算子学习遵循相同的缩放律；MNO与多任务DeepONet在逼近复杂度上具有相同的渐近率。

Conclusion: 共享表示不会增加多任务算子学习的整体代价，两种架构在最坏情况下的逼近复杂度渐近等价。

Abstract: We study the approximation and statistical complexity of learning collections of operators in a shared multi-task setting, with a focus on the Multiple Neural Operators (MNO) architecture. For broad classes of Lipschitz multiple operator maps, we derive near-optimal upper bounds for approximation and statistical generalization. On the lower-bound side, we establish a curse of parametric complexity and prove corresponding minimax rates. Together, these results show that shared representations across tasks do not increase the overall cost: multi-task operator learning follows the same scaling laws as single operator learning. We also compare MNO with a multi-task extension of DeepONet based on concatenated task inputs and show that, from a worst-case approximation-complexity perspective, both architectures satisfy essentially the same asymptotic rates.

</details>


### [192] [Plug-in Losses for Evidential Deep Learning: A Simplified Framework for Uncertainty Estimation that Includes the Softmax Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22746)
*Berk Hayta,Hannah Laus,Simon Mittermaier,Felix Krahmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对证据深度学习（EDL）中Dirichlet期望目标计算复杂的问题，提出用Dirichlet均值的插件损失近似原始目标，证明了近似误差随证据积累而衰减，并验证了该方法在语音识别任务上与经典EDL相当的准确性和选择性预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证据深度学习（EDL）通过Dirichlet分布建模类别概率实现单次不确定性估计，但其Dirichlet期望目标比标准监督学习损失更复杂，导致分析和实现困难。本文旨在简化EDL目标函数，使其更易于使用标准深度学习损失和训练流程。

Method: 将EDL诱导的一阶经验风险最小化目标近似为在Dirichlet均值处评估的插件损失，分析了在温和假设下近似误差随证据增长而衰减的性质，涵盖了均方误差和交叉熵损失等广泛损失函数。该框架在特定证据到Dirichlet映射下包含标准softmax分类器。

Result: 在Google Speech Commands数据集上，所提出的简化目标在预测准确性和选择性预测性能上与经典EDL相当，同时更易于使用标准深度学习损失和训练流程实现。这是首次通过EDL获得语音识别任务的覆盖率-准确性权衡曲线。

Conclusion: 所提出的简化EDL目标在保持不确定性估计能力的同时，大幅降低了计算复杂度和实现难度，为EDL在实际传感器学习系统中的应用提供了更便捷的途径。

Abstract: Real-world sensor-based learning systems require uncertainty estimation that is both reliable and computationally efficient. Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) provides single-pass uncertainty estimation by modeling the class probabilities via Dirichlet distributions, where the Dirichlet parameters are predicted by a learned neural network mapping. However, this approach can lead to computational challenges, as Dirichlet expected objectives are more complex than standard supervised learning losses, complicating their analysis and implementation. We address this issue by approximating the objective of the first-order empirical risk minimization problem induced by EDL with a plug-in loss evaluated at the Dirichlet mean and show that, under mild assumptions, the approximation error decays with growing evidence for a broad class of loss functions, including mean-squared error and cross-entropy loss. As a special case, our analysis provides justification for the use of softmax in the context of uncertainty estimation, since under a particular evidence-to-Dirichlet mapping, our framework includes the standard softmax classifier. We validate the proposed simplified objectives on the Google Speech Commands dataset and show that they achieve predictive accuracy and selective prediction performance comparable to classical EDL, while being simpler to implement using standard deep learning losses and training pipelines. To the best of our knowledge, this empirical analysis is the first to obtain coverage-accuracy trade-offs for speech recognition tasks through EDL.

</details>


### [193] [Uniform Diffusion Models Revisited: Leave-One-Out Denoiser and Absorbing State Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22765)
*Samson Gourevitch,Yazid Janati,Dario Shariatian,Umut Simsekli,Eric Moulines,Eric P. Xing,Alain Durmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了均匀扩散模型（UDM）中标准桥参数化与去噪后验不匹配的问题，提出了leave-one-out后验参数化方法，并推导了去噪器、leave-one-out后验与score之间的精确转换关系。同时引入了吸收态重构的均匀扩散公式，在语言建模任务上，leave-one-out参数化持续改进UDM生成质量，吸收态构建方法可匹敌或超越掩码扩散模型。结果表明均匀扩散与掩码扩散之间的经验差距更多来自参数化和采样设计，而非边际分布本身的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型中，均匀扩散模型（UDM）的标准桥参数化未被去噪后验优化，而是被一种leave-one-out后验优化——该后验在预测每个干净token时不使用其自身的噪声观测。这揭示了插件ELBO与常规交叉熵去噪目标之间的不匹配。

Method: 1) 刻画leave-one-out后验目标，推导去噪器、leave-one-out后验与score之间的精确转换关系；2) 提出基于leave-one-out预测器的指导性预测-校正采样器和改进的温度采样方法，无需额外训练即可提升推理性能；3) 引入吸收态重构的均匀扩散公式，在保持UDM联合分布的同时将其分解为类似掩码扩散的采样操作。

Result: 在语言建模任务上，leave-one-out参数化持续改进UDM的生成质量；吸收态构建方法匹配或超越掩码扩散模型的性能。

Conclusion: 均匀扩散与掩码扩散之间的经验差距主要源于参数化和采样设计，而非边际分布本身的选择。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are often trained through clean-data prediction, but the prediction can be used in different ways to define the reverse dynamics. In Masked Diffusion Models (MDM) these choices largely coincide, whereas in Uniform Diffusion Models (UDM) they do not. We show that the standard plug-in bridge parameterization for UDM is not optimized by the denoising posterior, but by a leave-one-out posterior that predicts each clean token without using its own noisy observation. This identifies a mismatch between the plug-in ELBO and the usual cross-entropy denoising objective. We characterize the leave-one-out target and derive exact conversions between the denoiser, the leave-one-out posterior, and the score. These conversions allow us to disentangle parameterization and training objective. Our results also lead to inference improvements without any additional training through an informed predictor-corrector sampler and improved temperature sampling based on the leave-one-out predictor.
  We further introduce an absorbing-state reformulation of uniform diffusion that preserves the UDM joint law while decomposing it into masked-diffusion-like sampling operations, with simpler denoising posteriors, carry-over unmasking, and a natural remasking mechanism. On language modeling, leave-one-out parameterizations consistently improve UDM generation, while the absorbing construction matches or surpasses masked diffusion. These results suggest that the empirical gap between masked and uniform diffusion is driven less by the choice of marginals themselves than by parameterization and sampling design. The code and models can be found at https://github.com/samsongourevitch/rev_udm.

</details>


### [194] [SDPM: Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model for Continuous-Time Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22776)
*Stanislav R. Kirpichenko,Andrei V. Konstantinov,Lev V. Utkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出生存扩散概率模型(SDPM)，一种基于去噪扩散模型的连续时间生存分析方法，无需参数化假设或时间离散化，在十个真实数据集上取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生存分析方法要么对风险函数施加结构性假设，要么将时间轴离散化，这限制了灵活性并引入了近似误差。SDPM旨在克服这些限制，提供一种无需参数化假设且无需时间离散化的生成式连续时间生存分析方法。

Method: SDPM使用去噪扩散模型对生存结局的条件分布P(T, δ|x)进行建模。在条件独立删失假设下，将模型生成的条件样本通过Kaplan-Meier估计量转化为生存函数估计。模型采用标准化对数时间和删失指示器的连续高斯混合表示，在变换后的目标空间中运行。

Result: 在十个真实生存数据集上与五种强基线（包括基于树、基于提升和神经生存模型）比较，SDPM在C-index、时间依赖AUC和集成Brier评分上均达到竞争性表现。在合成Cox-Weibull数据上，当生成足够多样本时，SDPM比强非参数基线更准确地恢复底层连续生存分布的形状。消融研究证实了所提出的目标空间变换的重要性。

Conclusion: SDPM通过扩散模型实现了一种灵活、连续时间的生成式生存分析方法，有效避免了参数化假设和离散化带来的限制，在多项评估指标上表现优异。

Abstract: Survival analysis aims to estimate a time-to-event distribution from data with censored observations. Many existing methods either impose structural assumptions on the hazard function or discretize the time axis, which may limit flexibility and introduce approximation errors. We propose the Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model (SDPM), a generative approach to continuous-time survival analysis. SDPM models the conditional distribution of the survival outcome, represented by the pair of observed time and censoring indicator, $\mathbb{P}(T,δ\mid \mathbf{x})$, using a denoising diffusion model. Under the assumption of conditionally independent censoring, conditional samples generated by the model can be transformed into survival function estimates using the Kaplan-Meier estimator. This formulation avoids parametric assumptions on the event-time distribution and does not require a discretization of the output time space. The model operates in a transformed target space, using standardized log-times and a continuous Gaussian-mixture representation of the censoring indicator. We evaluate SDPM on ten real survival datasets and compare it with five strong baselines, including tree-based, boosting-based, and neural survival models. Results show that SDPM achieves competitive predictive performance across C-index, integrated time-dependent AUC, and integrated Brier score. A study on synthetic Cox-Weibull data demonstrates that SDPM can recover the shape of an underlying continuous survival distribution more accurately than a strong nonparametric baseline when sufficiently many samples are generated. An ablation study confirms the importance of the proposed target-space transformations, which improve event-rate calibration, reduce invalid generated times, and provide consistent gains in predictive discrimination. Codes implementing the proposed model are publicly available.

</details>


### [195] [The Matching Principle: A Geometric Theory of Loss Functions for Nuisance-Robust Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22800)
*Vishal Rajput*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出，鲁棒性、域适应、光度与遮挡不变性、组合泛化、时间鲁棒性、对齐安全性和经典各向异性正则化等看似独立的问题，共享同一个统计本质：估计标签保持的部署扰动的协方差，然后沿覆盖该协方差范围的矩阵正则化编码器雅可比矩阵（匹配原则）。CORAL、对抗训练、IRM、数据增强、度量学习、雅可比惩罚和对齐风格约束都是该对象的不同估计量。在线性高斯模型中给出了闭式最优性证明，并引入无标签的轨迹偏差指数（TDI）作为嵌入敏感性探针。13个预注册实验验证了预测的顺序，12个通过。主要贡献是命名了部署扰动协方差、阐明正则化器必须满足的条件，并提供了可证伪的闭式理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将鲁棒性、域适应、不变性、对齐安全性和正则化等视为独立问题，各自有独立的方法体系。作者认为它们共享相同的统计结构，即估计标签保持的部署扰动协方差并据此正则化，旨在统一这些看似不同的研究方向。

Method: 1) 提出匹配原则：估计标签保持的部署扰动协方差，然后用覆盖该协方差范围的矩阵正则化编码器雅可比矩阵。2) 在线性高斯模型中推导闭式最优解，包括 cube-root water-filling 和范围覆盖的必要性等定理。3) 引入 Trajectory Deviation Index (TDI) 作为无标签的嵌入敏感性探针。4) 在13个预注册实验（从经典ML到Qwen2.5-7B）中验证理论预测。

Result: 13个预注册实验中12个通过理论预测的 matched→isotropic→wrong-W 顺序；唯一的例外（Office-31）被预先认定为特征间隙失败。在7B规模下，matched style-PMH 提升了选择性诚实性并保持了 Style TDI，而标准 DPO 会降低该指标。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献不是在所有榜单上取得通用最优，而是命名了部署扰动协方差这一关键对象，阐明了正则化器必须满足的条件（匹配原则），并提供了一个可证伪的闭式理论框架。这为统一理解多种鲁棒性和正则化方法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Robustness, domain adaptation, photometric and occlusion invariance, compositional generalisation, temporal robustness, alignment safety, and classical anisotropic regularisation are usually treated as separate problems with separate method families. This paper argues that much of their shared structure is one statistical problem: estimate the covariance of label-preserving deployment nuisance, then regularise the encoder Jacobian along a matrix whose range covers that covariance (the matching principle). CORAL, adversarial training, IRM, augmentation, metric learning, Jacobian penalties, and alignment-style constraints are different estimators of that object, not independent robustness tricks.
  In the linear-Gaussian model we prove closed-form optimality (Theorem A), including cube-root water-filling within the matched range; necessity of range coverage for quadratic Jacobian penalties (Theorem G); the same range dichotomy at deep global minima; and two falsification controls (Lemma C; Corollaries E), with seven conditional consistency lemmas (D1-D7) for estimation under standard identifiability assumptions.
  We introduce the Trajectory Deviation Index (TDI), a label-free probe of embedding sensitivity when task accuracy or Jacobian Frobenius norm is insufficient.
  Thirteen pre-registered blocks from classical ML through Qwen2.5-7B test the predicted matched, then isotropic, then wrong-W ordering on geometry and deployment drift; twelve pass, and the sole exception (Office-31) is an eigengap failure named before the run. At 7B scale, matched style-PMH improves selective honesty and preserves Style TDI where standard DPO degrades it.
  The contribution is naming the deployment nuisance covariance, stating what the regulariser must do, and supplying a closed-form falsifiable theory once that object is identified, not universality on every leaderboard.

</details>


### [196] [Temporal Contrastive Transformer for Financial Crime Detection: Self-Supervised Sequence Embeddings via Predictive Contrastive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21490)
*Danny Butvinik,Yonit Marcus,Nitzan Tal,Gabrielle Azoulay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出时间对比变换器（TCT），一种自监督对比学习框架，用于从金融交易序列中提取行为嵌入表示。实验表明嵌入本身有预测能力（AUC 0.8644），但与手工特征结合后未带来显著提升（AUC 0.9205 vs 0.9245），说明学习到的表征与现有特征抽象高度重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少金融犯罪检测中对人工特征工程的依赖，探索自监督时序表征学习在金融交易序列中捕获行为动态的能力。

Method: 采用时间对比变换器（TCT），基于自监督对比学习目标训练，从金融交易序列中学习嵌入表示，下游使用梯度提升分类器进行欺诈检测评估。

Result: 嵌入单独使用时AUC达0.8644；与领域手工特征结合后AUC为0.9205，略低于纯手工特征的0.9245，未观察到可测量的提升。

Conclusion: TCT能捕获有意义的时序行为信号，但其学习到的表征与现有领域特征高度重叠，尚未在强特征基线上产生附加价值，仍需进一步研究模型架构、训练目标及集成策略。

Abstract: We introduce the Temporal Contrastive Transformer (TCT), a representation learning framework designed to capture contextual temporal dynamics in sequences of financial transactions. The model is trained using a self-supervised contrastive objective to produce embeddings that encode behavioral patterns over time, with the goal of supporting downstream fraud detection tasks. We evaluate TCT in a realistic setting by using the learned embeddings as input features to a gradient boosting classifier. Experimental results show that embeddings alone achieve meaningful predictive performance (AUC 0.8644), indicating that the model captures non-trivial temporal structure. However, when combined with domain-engineered features, no measurable improvement is observed over the baseline (AUC 0.9205 vs. 0.9245), suggesting that the learned representations largely overlap with existing feature abstractions. These findings position TCT as a promising representation learning approach that captures relevant behavioral signal, while highlighting the challenges of achieving additive value over strong domain features. The results reflect an intermediate stage in the development of temporal representation learning for financial crime detection and motivate further research on model architecture, training objectives, and integration strategies. At this early stage, achieving performance comparable to a strong feature-engineered baseline is itself a meaningful outcome, indicating that learned representations approximate domain-specific features without manual engineering. While not yet production-ready, these results point to a promising direction for reducing reliance on feature engineering in financial crime detection.

</details>


### [197] [Provable Robustness against Backdoor Attacks via the Primal-Dual Perspective on Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21780)
*Aman Saxena,Jan Schuchardt,Yan Scholten,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将随机平滑与差分隐私的隐私画像分析相联系，提出了一种模块化且紧致的端到端鲁棒性认证框架，能够联合认证训练时（如后门攻击）和推理时（如逃避攻击）的复合机制，并在DP-SGD和深度分区聚合上实例化，实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机平滑方法能分别认证针对训练阶段的投毒攻击和推理阶段的逃避攻击，但后门攻击同时扰动训练数据和测试数据，需要在一个统一的鲁棒性证书中联合分析训练时和测试时的随机化机制，现有方法难以处理这种复合威胁模型。

Method: 将随机平滑与差分隐私的隐私画像（privacy profiles）对偶视角相联系，利用隐私画像为异构机制的复合提供数值化计算流程，从而实现对复杂复合机制的模块化、紧致端到端认证；在DP-SGD和深度分区聚合（Deep Partition Aggregation）上实例化，结合推理时平滑推导联合鲁棒性保证。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上的实验验证了所提框架的有效性，能够为同时存在训练时攻击和推理时攻击的复杂威胁模型提供鲁棒性认证。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于复合机制的原则性通用框架，能够针对更贴近真实攻击者能力的复杂威胁模型认证鲁棒性。

Abstract: Randomized smoothing is a powerful tool for certifying robustness to adversarial perturbations, including poisoning attacks via randomized training and evasion attacks via randomized inference. Extending these guarantees to backdoor attacks, where training and test data are jointly perturbed, remains challenging because training- and test-time randomized mechanisms must be analyzed within a single robustness certificate. We address this by connecting randomized smoothing to the dual view of differential privacy through privacy profiles, which provide a numerical procedure for composing heterogeneous mechanisms. The resulting framework enables tight, modular, end-to-end certification of complex, composed mechanisms while leveraging existing analyses of differentially private mechanisms. We instantiate the framework for DP-SGD and Deep Partition Aggregation with inference-time smoothing, deriving joint robustness guarantees against both training-time and inference-time attacks. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework. Overall, we provide a principled and general framework for using composite mechanisms to certify robustness under complex threat models that better capture the capabilities of real-world adversaries.

</details>


### [198] [Optimal Guarantees for Auditing Rényi Differentially Private Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21938)
*Benjamin D. Kim,Lav R. Varshney,Daniel Alabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于假设检验的黑盒审计框架，通过Donsker-Varadhan变分估计器直接估计Rényi散度，用于审计声称满足Rényi差分隐私（RDP）的机器学习算法。该框架提供了非渐近置信区间，并证明了匹配的极小化最优样本复杂度下界。实验表明，在DP-SGD的完全黑盒审计中，该方法在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上相比现有方法有显著改进，尤其是在小到中等Rényi阶数下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒审计方法缺乏对RDP保证的严格验证，且样本复杂度缺乏理论最优性保证。需要一种既能提供非渐近统计保证、又具有信息论最优样本效率的审计框架。

Method: 基于假设检验框架，利用Donsker-Varadhan变分估计器直接估计相邻执行之间的Rényi散度；通过类别受限DV估计器推导出显式的非渐近置信区间，将统计估计误差与算法隐私泄漏分离；并给出了匹配的极小化极大下界以证明样本复杂度的最优性。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上，对DP-SGD进行完全黑盒审计，在广泛隐私预算范围内，相比现有最优黑盒方法，经验RDP下界有显著提升，尤其在小到中等Rényi阶数下改进最为明显。

Conclusion: 本文首次建立了RDP审计通过DV估计器的最优样本复杂度理论保证，提供了实际可用的严格审计工具，为隐私声明的可信验证提供了理论和实践基础。

Abstract: We study black-box auditing for machine learning algorithms that claim R \ 'enyi differential privacy (RDP) guarantees. We introduce an auditing framework, based on hypothesis testing, that directly estimates Rényi divergence between neighboring executions using the Donsker-Varadhan (DV) variational estimator. Our analysis yields explicit and non-asymptotic confidence intervals for RDP auditing via class-restricted DV estimators, separating statistical estimation error from algorithmic privacy leakage. We prove matching minimax lower bounds showing that, up to logarithmic factors, our sample-complexity guarantees are information-theoretically optimal, thereby establishing the first optimal guarantees for auditing RDP via DV estimators. Empirically, we instantiate our framework for auditing DP-SGD in a fully black-box setting. Across MNIST and CIFAR-10, and over a wide range of privacy regimes, our auditors produce a strong overall improvement on empirical RDP lower bounds compared to prior state-of-the-art black-box methods especially at small and moderate Rényi orders where accurate auditing is most challenging.

</details>


### [199] [A Reproducible Log-Driven AutoML Framework for Interpretable Pipeline Optimization in Healthcare Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21528)
*Rui Huang,Lican Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出yvsoucom-iterkit，一个确定性、日志驱动的自动机器学习框架，将流水线优化建模为完全可复现的配置级系统。在Pima Indian糖尿病和Stroke数据集上对超过18,000个流水线配置进行实验，揭示了结构化的、部分冗余的搜索空间，其中性能由少量交互组件主导。集成模型取得优异性能（Pima: Weighted-F1 0.89, Macro-F1 0.88; Stroke: Weighted-F1 0.94），但Stroke上Macro-F1仅0.67。随机森林重要性分析表明增强、模型选择和类别不平衡处理是关键驱动因素，组件相似性分析揭示了显著冗余。跨种子分析显示性能-鲁棒性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的、可复现的疾病风险预测仍因异构特征、有限样本和严重类别不平衡而具有挑战性。现有自动化机器学习方法在可解释性、可复现性和组件间交互分析方面存在不足。

Method: 提出yvsoucom-iterkit框架：将每个流水线编码为可追溯的日志实体，支持组件归因、交互、相似性和跨种子鲁棒性分析。实验在Pima Indians Diabetes和Stroke数据集上进行，探索超过18,000个流水线配置，使用随机森林重要性分析、组件相似性分析（RMS距离）和跨种子性能分析。

Result: 集成模型取得最好性能（Pima: Weighted-F1 0.89, Macro-F1 0.88; Stroke: Weighted-F1 0.94, Macro-F1 0.67）。增强(0.454)、模型选择(0.198)和不平衡处理(0.101)是Pima关键驱动因素；不平衡处理(0.406)主导Stroke。组件间存在显著冗余（如特征选择变体RMS距离0.0252，mixup与无增强距离0.0279）。集成模型跨种子变异性低(0.023-0.026)。

Conclusion: 有效的AutoML优化可聚焦于少量高影响组件，搜索空间存在结构化冗余。框架的确定性日志机制确保了完全可复现性，组件级分析有助于理解流水线行为模式。

Abstract: Accurate and reproducible disease risk prediction remains challenging due to heterogeneous features, limited samples, and severe class imbalance. This study introduces yvsoucom-iterkit, a deterministic and log-driven automated machine learning framework that formulates pipeline optimization as a fully reproducible, configuration-level system. Each pipeline is encoded as a traceable log entity, enabling analysis of component attribution, interactions, similarity, and cross-seed robustness. Experiments on the Pima Indians Diabetes and Stroke datasets across more than 18,000 pipeline configurations reveal a structured and partially redundant search space, where performance is governed by a small subset of interacting components. Random Forest importance analysis identifies augmentation (0.454), model choice (0.198), and imbalance handling (0.101) as key drivers on Pima, while imbalance handling dominates Stroke (0.406). Component similarity analysis shows strong redundancy, with feature selection variants (biMax-biMean) exhibiting low RMS distance (0.0252), mixup closely matching no augmentation (0.0279), and TomekLinks aligning with no imbalance handling (0.0325), whereas Gaussian noise shows greater divergence from no augmentation (0.10). The framework achieves strong and stable performance using ensemble models (Weighted-F1 0.89, Macro-F1 0.88 on Pima; Weighted-F1 0.94 on Stroke), while Macro-F1 remains lower on Stroke (0.67) due to class imbalance. Cross-seed analysis reveals a performance-robustness trade-off, with ensembles showing lower variability (0.023-0.026) than SVM. These results indicate that effective AutoML optimization can focus on a reduced set of high-impact components.

</details>


### [200] [When Are Teacher Tokens Reliable? Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21606)
*Xiaogeng Liu,Xinyan Wang,Yingzi Ma,Yechao Zhang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出位置加权在线策略自蒸馏（PW-OPSD）方法，通过诊断发现推理过程中教师token的可靠性具有轨迹层级结构（而非局部熵），利用位置权重提升自蒸馏效果，在多个推理基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）对所有token等权处理，隐式假设教师目标在所有学生访问前缀上同等可靠。基于熵的方法虽尝试调节，但在推理任务中高教师熵可能反映非可行不确定性或良性解多样性，含义模糊，缺乏可靠的token级教师可靠性指标。

Method: 1）提出分支可行性诊断（branch-viability diagnostic），通过强制替换学生前缀后的教师备选token并测试是否能恢复正确答案，评估每个位置的教师token可靠性；2）发现序列内位置得分（oriented within-sequence position score）是教师可靠性的最强预测器（AUROC=0.83），远优于局部熵（≤0.57）；3）据此提出PW-OPSD，在保持原有rollout、教师pass和clip前向KL目标框架下，对位置施加递增权重。

Result: PW-OPSD在AIME 2024和AIME 2025上Avg@12分别提升+1.0和+1.1分；在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B和Olmo-3-7B-Think两个更大规模、不同模型族上也取得一致的Avg@12提升。

Conclusion: 推理蒸馏中教师token可靠性具有轨迹层级结构，而非由局部熵决定，利用位置权重可在不增加教师计算开销的情况下有效提升蒸馏效果。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own rollouts using a privileged teacher, but its standard objective weights all generated tokens equally, implicitly treating the privileged teacher target as equally reliable at every student-visited prefix. Existing entropy-based OPD methods relax this uniformity by modulating token-level supervision with teacher entropy, but high teacher entropy in reasoning has an ambiguous reliability meaning: it can reflect either non-viable uncertainty or benign solution diversity. To identify this phenomenon, we introduce a branch-viability diagnostic. Specifically, we record next-token alternatives from the privileged-answer teacher prompt, force each alternative after the student prompt plus its on-policy spine prefix, and test whether the resulting student-template continuation recovers the correct answer. On Qwen3-4B, we find that an oriented within-sequence position score is the strongest tested predictor of teacher-token reliability, reaching an area-under-ROC-curve (AUROC) of 0.83; local uncertainty scores are at most 0.57. Motivated by this trajectory-level structure, we propose Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation (PW-OPSD), which applies an increasing position weight while keeping the same student rollout, privileged teacher pass, and clipped forward-KL target as OPSD. In our comprehensive evaluations with different random seeds, the diagnostic-derived PW-OPSD improves AIME 2024 and AIME 2025 Avg@12 by +1.0 and +1.1 points, and a generalization evaluation on two larger-scale models from different families, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B and Olmo-3-7B-Think, also demonstrates consistent aggregate Avg@12 improvements. These results show that teacher-token reliability in reasoning distillation is trajectory-structured and can be utilized without additional teacher computation.

</details>


### [201] [Amplifying, Not Learning: Fine-Tuned AI Text Detectors Amplify a Pretrained Direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21653)
*Alexander Smirnov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现AI文本检测器并非构建“AI vs. 人类”的边界，而是放大预训练中已有的典型性轴（typicality axis）。原始编码器在无需微调的情况下，通过质心投影即可达到甚至超过微调检测性能（RoBERTa-base上原始投影超越微调）。该轴在非母语ESL写作上效果反转（AUROC 0.06-0.20），验证了典型性假说。提出了24样本冻结探针、封闭形式Jacobian预测器等方法，实现了高精度检测与干预。多个操作上不同的探针在observer-invariance上达成一致，且发现大多数微调差异实为校准偏移而非表征学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战当前AI文本检测的主流假设（即检测器学习了一个AI vs. 人类的分类边界），提出并验证一个更简洁的假说：检测器只是放大了预训练模型中已有的典型性轴（typicality axis），而非构造新的判别边界。

Method: 1) 在原始编码器（无任务监督）上进行质心投影（centroid(AI)-centroid(HC3)）；2) 24样本冻结探针；3) 封闭形式Jacobian预测器进行轴操作干预；4) 三种操作上独立的探针（caps_rate残差化、几何signed-epsilon消融、封闭形式文本对预测器）验证observer-invariance；5) 校准分析区分表征学习与校准偏移。

Result: 1) 原始质心投影在三个架构上达到NYT-vs-HC3 AUROC 0.806/0.944/0.834，为微调判别上限的86-106%；RoBERTa-base上原始投影甚至超越微调。2) 在非母语ESL上AUROC降至0.06-0.20，验证典型性假说。3) 24样本冻结探针匹配全微调（0.900 vs 0.895）。4) 封闭形式Jacobian预测器R²=1.000，将ELECTRA-CE部署TPR从0.000提升至0.904（FPR=1%），并转移至三个第三方RoBERTa检测器（16/16 oracle等价）。5) 三种探针cosine相似度0.74/0.81/1.00，确认observer-invariance。6) 27个干预单元的|ΔAUROC|≤0.0081，LoRA→全微调偏差差距≥97%为校准偏移。

Conclusion: AI文本检测的本质是放大预训练典型性轴而非构建分类边界。该假说解释了检测器在非母语写作上的失败，且多个独立方法一致支持该结论。大多数微调带来的表观改进实为校准偏移，而非真正的表征学习。

Abstract: AI text detectors amplify a pretrained typicality axis; they do not construct an AI-vs-human boundary. On raw encoders before any task supervision, projecting onto centroid(AI)-centroid(HC3) achieves NYT-vs-HC3 AUROC 0.806/0.944/0.834 across three architectures (86-106% of the fine-tuned discrimination ceiling: on RoBERTa-base, raw projection exceeds fine-tuning); on RoBERTa-base, full fine-tuning reduces discrimination below raw on both fluent-formal populations tested. The same axis inverts on non-native ESL writing (AUROC 0.06-0.20) -- a falsifiable prediction unique to the typicality reading. A 24-example frozen probe matches full fine-tuning (0.900 vs 0.895). A closed-form Jacobian predictor parameterises axis-manipulating interventions with R^2 = 1.000 universal, lifts ELECTRA-CE deployment TPR from 0.000 to 0.904 at FPR = 1%, and transfers to three independently-trained third-party RoBERTa detectors at 16/16 oracle-equivalence (57% NYT-FPR reduction on the OpenAI detector). Scope: encoder family; mechanism magnitude HC3-anchored; population-level shared axis with per-text mechanisms varying across architectures. Three operationally distinct probes -- text-surface caps_rate residualisation, geometric signed-epsilon ablation, closed-form text-pair predictor -- agree at cos 0.74/0.81/1.00 across three architectures, confirming observer-invariance. Under matched-TPR-0.90 evaluation, the published intervention zoo (CC, dealign-f2c) is calibration-equivalent across 27 cells (|Delta AUROC| <= 0.0081), and >= 97% of the LoRA->full-FT bias gap on ELECTRA is calibration shift, not learned representation -- the central claim's prediction confirmed.

</details>


### [202] [Value-Gradient Hypothesis of RL for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21654)
*Arip Asadulaev,Daniil Ognev,Karim Salta,Martin Takac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从值梯度视角解释了无评论家强化学习方法（如PPO、GRPO）在LLM后训练中的有效性，揭示了其更新近似于值梯度，并提出了RL效果最优化判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为什么无评论家强化学习方法（PPO、GRPO）在LLM后训练中有效，以及它们在什么情况下能带来最大收益——这一问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: 1) 在可微展开和加性噪声参数化下，证明演员更新在期望上等价于值梯度；2) 对离散transformer策略，证明通过注意力机制的自微分产生的经验代价态近似值信号，误差由采样间隙和策略熵控制；3) 基于上述结果将RL影响分解为值梯度信号和可达奖励余量，导出RL最有效性的判据。

Result: 1) 无评论家RL的演员更新在期望上具有值梯度形式；2) 离散transformer的自微分能近似值信号；3) 提出RL效果分解及最优化判据。

Conclusion: 无评论家RL方法在LLM后训练中的有效性可从值梯度视角得到理论解释，并提供了预测RL何时最有效的判据。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning substantially improves pretrained language models, but it remains understudied why critic-free methods such as PPO and GRPO work as well as they do, and when they should provide the largest gains. We develop a value-gradient perspective of critic-free RL for LLM post-training. First, under a differentiable rollout and additive-noise parameterization, we show that the actor update is value-gradient-like in expectation: the backward pass propagates costates whose conditional expectation equals the value gradient. Second, for discrete transformer policies, we show that autodifferentiation through attention produces empirical costates that approximate this value signal, with an error controlled by the sampling gap and policy entropy. These results motivate a decomposition of RL impact into value gradient signal and reachable reward headroom, yielding a criterion for when RL should be most effective along a pretraining trajectory.

</details>


### [203] [Hierarchical Variational Policies for Reward-Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21661)
*Kushagra Pandey,Farrin Marouf Sofian,Jan Niklas Groeneveld,Felix Draxler,Stephan Mandt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层变分模型框架，通过学习轻量级随机策略实现预训练扩散模型在测试时的高效自适应，在保持样本质量的同时大幅降低推理成本，在4倍超分辨率等逆问题上实现了5倍以上加速，并进一步扩展到半摊销机制以追求最优感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练扩散模型在下游任务（如逆问题）中的测试时自适应通常需要昂贵的指导或优化，现有方法推理成本高，难以实现质量与速度的良好平衡。

Method: 将测试时自适应形式化为分层变分模型，将控制摊销为轻量级随机策略，支持大步长少步数扩散采样，并通过结构化逐歩控制维持样本质量；进一步扩展到半摊销机制，结合廉价摊销提议与有限测试时优化。

Result: 在4倍超分辨率任务上，相比最优基线，感知质量更好且推理速度提升5倍以上；在半摊销机制下，多个逆问题上达到最先进感知质量。

Conclusion: 该方法在质量-速度权衡上表现出色，全摊销采样器匹配或超越近期测试时扩展基线且计算量显著更少，半摊销扩展实现最优感知质量。

Abstract: Adapting pretrained diffusion models to downstream objectives such as inverse problems often requires expensive test-time guidance or optimization. We propose a principled framework for generating high-quality reward-aligned samples at substantially reduced inference cost. Our approach formulates test-time adaptation as a hierarchical variational model, where control is amortized into a lightweight yet expressive stochastic policy. This formulation naturally supports few-step diffusion sampling: large step sizes enable fast inference, while the learned policy maintains sample quality by providing structured per-step control. The resulting fully amortized sampler achieves a strong quality--speed tradeoff, matching or exceeding recent test-time scaling baselines while requiring significantly less compute. For example, on 4x super-resolution, our method achieves better perceptual quality with more than 5x faster inference compared to the best-performing baseline. We further extend our approach to a semi-amortized regime that combines cheap amortized proposals with limited test-time optimization, achieving state-of-the-art perceptual quality across several challenging inverse problems.

</details>


### [204] [Prototype-Guided Classification Sub-Task Decoupling Framework: Enhancing Generalization and Interpretability for Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22055)
*Xianhao Song,Yuang Zhang,Yuqi She,Liping Wang,Xuemin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PDFTime是一个原型引导的时序分类框架，将传统的直接特征到标签的黑箱映射重构为解耦的多阶段相似性推理过程，通过学习原型来逼近潜在空间中类条件特征分布，实现渐进判别，在UEA和UCR基准上达到SOTA性能，在UCR数据集的80/128上取得最高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度时序分类模型采用直接特征到标签的分类范式，通过全局池化后的单层线性投影将高维时序嵌入压缩为类别logits，这种范式将特征提取与决策逻辑混为一体，导致模型缺乏可解释性，且难以实现精确分类。

Method: 提出PDFTime原型引导框架，将时序分类重构为多阶段决策过程：学习可解释的原型来逼近潜在空间中的类条件特征分布，通过不同粒度的分类子任务实现渐进式判别，将直接特征到标签映射转变为解耦的多阶段相似性推理。

Result: 在UEA和UCR基准上达到SOTA性能，在UCR存档的128个数据集中有80个取得最高准确率，在一致性和泛化性上显著优于近期强基线方法。

Conclusion: PDFTime是首个将时序分类重构为解耦的多阶段相似性推理过程的框架，打破了长期存在的直接黑箱特征到标签映射范式，在准确性和可解释性之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Time Series Classification (TSC) is a long-standing research problem that has gained increasing attention in recent years with the rapid growth of large-scale temporal data. Despite substantial progress enabled by deep learning, designing TSC models that are both accurate and interpretable remains a challenging task. Many existing approaches adopt a direct feature-to-label classification paradigm, by collapsing high-dimensional temporal embeddings into class logits via a single linear projection (often after global pooling), the paradigm conflates feature extraction and decision logic into an inseparable mapping.
  To address these limitations, we propose PDFTime, a prototype-guided framework that reformulates time series classification as a multi-stage decision process. Instead of direct feature-to-label mapping, PDFTime leverages learned prototypes to approximate class-conditional feature distributions in the latent space, enabling progressive discrimination through classification sub-tasks of varying granularity. To our knowledge, PDFTime is the first framework to reformulate time series classification as a decoupled, multi-stage similarity-based reasoning process, breaking the long-standing paradigm of direct, black-box feature-to-label mapping. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that PDFTime achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across UEA and UCR benchmarks. Notably, it secures the top-$1$ accuracy on 80 out of 128 datasets in the UCR archive, significantly outperforming recent strong baselines in both consistency and generalization.

</details>


### [205] [From Reasoning Chains to Verifiable Subproblems: Curriculum Reinforcement Learning Enables Credit Assignment for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22074)
*Xitai Jiang,Zihan Tang,Wenze Lin,Yang Yue,Shenzhi Wang,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCRL是一种子问题课程强化学习框架，通过从参考推理链中提取可验证的子问题，将硬问题的部分进展转化为可验证的学习信号，并采用子问题级归一化实现细粒度信用分配，在多个数学推理基准上显著优于GRPO等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结果奖励的RLVR在困难推理问题上效率低下，因为正确最终答案的rollout稀少，且样本级信用分配无法利用失败尝试中的部分进展。

Method: 提出SCRL框架，从参考推理链中推导可验证的子问题并构建课程（最终子问题固定为原始问题），采用子问题级归一化独立标准化每个子问题位置的奖励，将优势值分配给对应的答案片段，实现细粒度信用分配。

Result: 在7个数学推理基准上，SCRL在Qwen3-4B-Base上平均准确率比GRPO提升+4.1点，在Qwen3-14B-Base上提升+1.9点；在AIME24/25和IMO-Bench上，pass@1提升+3.7点，pass@64提升+4.6点。

Conclusion: 子问题课程能够将硬问题从梯度死区中解放出来，问题越难相对收益越大，SCRL在困难推理问题上实现了更好的探索效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) has shown strong promise for LLM reasoning, but outcome-based RLVR remains inefficient on hard problems because correct final-answer rollouts are rare and sample-level credit assignment cannot use partial progress in failed attempts. We introduce SCRL (Subproblem Curriculum Reinforcement Learning), a curriculum RL framework that derives verifiable subproblems from reference reasoning chains and fixes the final subproblem as the original problem. This turns partial progress on hard problems into verifiable learning signals. Algorithmically, SCRL uses subproblem-level normalization, which normalizes rewards independently at each subproblem position and assigns the resulting advantages to the corresponding answer spans, enabling finer-grained credit assignment without external rubrics or reward models. Our analysis shows that subproblem curricula lift hard problems out of gradient dead zones, with larger relative gains as the original problem becomes harder. Across seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks, SCRL outperforms strong curriculum-learning baselines, improving average accuracy over GRPO by +4.1 points on Qwen3-4B-Base and +1.9 points on Qwen3-14B-Base. On AIME24, AIME25, and IMO-Bench, SCRL further improves pass@1 by +3.7 points and pass@64 by +4.6 points on Qwen3-4B-Base, indicating better exploration on hard reasoning problems.

</details>


### [206] [Short-Term-to-Long-Term Memory Transfer for Knowledge Graphs under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22142)
*Taewoon Kim,Vincent François-Lavet,Michael Cochez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于神经符号值函数决策的记忆转移方法，在每个观测三元组决定是否保留再存入长期记忆，使用逐项Q学习和时序差分更新。在RoomKG基准上，以128容量学习到的转移策略优于符号和神经基线，轻量短期策略表现最佳，实现了可解释的记忆决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测强化学习中，大多数基于记忆的方法没有显式建模符号观测从短期到长期的转移过程，缺乏对哪些信息该保留、哪些该丢弃的明确决策机制。

Method: 将记忆转移建模为神经符号值决策问题：对每个观测到的三元组，智能体在插入长期记忆前选择保留或丢弃；使用逐项Q学习设计（共享参数）处理可变大小的短期缓冲区，并通过跨连续步骤的匹配项进行时序差分更新。

Result: 在RoomKG基准上长期记忆容量128时，学习到的转移决策优于符号基线（含时间标注）和LSTM/Transformer等历史基线；消融实验中轻量局部短期变体表现最佳；策略在步骤层面保留导航和查询相关事实，丢弃低价值候选事实。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了在记忆约束下显式且可解释的记忆决策，为神经符号记忆管理提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning under partial observability requires deciding what information to retain, yet most memory-based approaches do not explicitly model short-term-to-long-term transfer of symbolic observations. We study this transfer process in a temporal knowledge-graph memory setting and cast it as a neuro-symbolic value-based decision problem: for each observed triple, the agent chooses whether to keep or drop it before long-term insertion. To handle variable-sized short-term buffers, we use a per-item Q-learning design with shared parameters and a practical temporal-difference update over matched items across consecutive steps. On the RoomKG benchmark at long-term memory capacity 128, learned transfer decisions outperform symbolic and neural baselines, including symbolic baselines with temporal annotations and history-based LSTM/Transformer baselines. Across transfer-policy ablations, a lightweight local short-term-only variant performs best, and step-level behavior shows that the policy keeps navigation- and query-relevant facts while discarding lower-value candidate facts, supporting explicit and interpretable memory decisions under memory constraints.

</details>


### [207] [One-Way Policy Optimization for Self-Evolving LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22156)
*Shuo Yang,Jinda Lu,Kexin Huang,Chiyu Ma,Shaohang Wei,Yuyang Liu,Guoyin Wang,Jingren Zhou,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RLVR中二元验证器奖励稀疏导致训练不稳定且传统方法对偏差无差别惩罚的问题，本文提出单向策略优化（OWPO），通过解耦优化方向与更新幅度，对劣势偏差加速对齐、优势偏差增益锁定，并引入迭代参考更新产生“棘轮效应”，在多个基准上超越DAPO、OPD和MOPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR中二元验证器奖励稀疏导致训练效率低且优化不稳定；现有方法通过参考策略施加token级约束，但无差别惩罚偏差，在策略试图超越参考时会反转验证器确定的优化方向，从而抑制性能提升。

Method: 提出OWPO，核心是解耦优化方向与更新幅度：验证器决定更新方向，参考策略仅调整幅度。具体采用非对称重加权——对劣势偏差（策略落后参考）进行加速对齐，对优势偏差（策略超越参考）进行增益锁定；并引入迭代参考更新，形成持续巩固增益的“棘轮效应”。

Result: OWPO在多个基准上优于DAPO、OPD、MOPD等强基线，突破了固定先验的瓶颈，实现了无需依赖外部参考模型的持续自我进化。

Conclusion: 通过解耦优化方向与幅度、非对称重加权及迭代参考更新，OWPO有效解决了RLVR中奖励稀疏和优化不稳定问题，实现了更高效的推理能力扩展。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a promising paradigm for scaling reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, the sparsity of binary verifier rewards often leads to low efficiency and optimization instability. To stabilize training, existing methods typically impose token-level constraints relative to a reference policy. We identify that such constraints penalize deviations indiscriminately; this can flip verifier-determined direction when the policy attempts to outperform the reference, thereby suppressing gains. To resolve this, we propose One-Way Policy Optimization (OWPO), a method based on the principle of decoupling optimization direction from update magnitude. In OWPO, the verifier dictates the update direction, while the reference policy serves only to adjust the magnitude. Specifically, OWPO applies asymmetric reweighting: it performs Accelerated Alignment for inferior deviations (where the policy lags behind the reference) and Gain Locking for superior deviations (where the policy surpasses the reference). Furthermore, by incorporating iterative reference updates, OWPO creates a ``Ratchet Effect'' that continuously consolidates gains. Experimental results demonstrate that OWPO outperforms strong baselines, including DAPO, OPD, and MOPD, breaking the bottleneck of fixed priors to enable continuous self-evolution without reliance on external reference models.

</details>


### [208] [Can Transformers Learn to Verify During Backtracking Search?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22221)
*Yin Jun Phua,Tony Ribeiro,Tuan Nguyen,Katsumi Inoue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，基于解码器仅变换器（decoder-only transformer）在累积轨迹上训练时，存在“分散检索”（scattered retrieval）和“历史纠缠”（history entanglement）两个根本性问题，导致模型无法根据当前搜索状态做出最优的继续/回溯决策。作者提出了局部化（localization）和选择性状态注意力（SSA）两种结构修复方法，在3-SAT、图着色、积木世界和回溯解析等多个任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的推理系统在回溯搜索中，使用自回归方式在离线求解器轨迹上训练。但输入是每一步之前的累积历史轨迹，而最优的继续/回溯决策只应取决于当前搜索状态，而非到达该状态的路径历史。作者发现因果训练下的解码器模型无法满足这一要求，存在分散检索和历史纠缠两个问题。

Method: 1）局部化（localization）：在轨迹层面重写每个决策块，使状态特征在局部暴露，解决分散检索问题。2）选择性状态注意力（SSA）：一种固定注意力掩码，从结构上强制模型基于当前状态做决策，无需修改训练数据、目标函数或模型参数。聚焦于传播已暴露矛盾后的反应式验证场景。

Result: 在3-SAT、图着色、积木世界和回溯解析任务上，对于历史不同但状态相同的样本对，SSA能够输出一致的决策，而基于累积训练的因果基线则不能。

Conclusion: 本文诊断了Transformer在序列化轨迹数据上的行为缺陷，并提供了结构性的修复方案。指出在自身推理步骤上进行搜索的预训练语言模型可能面临同样的问题，并提出了推理时上下文清除作为一种无需重新训练即可实现状态隔离的候选方法。

Abstract: Backtracking search underlies classical constraint solvers, planners, and theorem provers. Recent transformer-based reasoning systems explore search trees over their own intermediate steps. A common training recipe fits an autoregressive next-token loss on offline solver traces. The model's input at each step is a cumulative trace of all prior decisions. The optimal continue-or-backtrack predictor depends only on the current search state, since two trajectories reaching the same state admit the same viable continuations. We show that decoder-only transformers trained on cumulative traces fail this requirement in two ways: the trace can scatter state features across many positions (scattered retrieval), and the predictor can condition on the trajectory rather than the state (history entanglement). We address scattered retrieval with localization, a trace-level fix that rewrites each decision block to expose state features locally. We address history entanglement with Selective State Attention (SSA), a fixed attention mask that enforces state-based decisions structurally without modifying training data, objective, or parameters. We focus on reactive verification, after propagation has exposed a contradiction. We test SSA on 3-SAT, graph coloring, Blocks World, and backtracking parsing. On same-state pairs that differ only in prior history, SSA emits identical decisions while a cumulative-trained causal baseline does not. Our contribution is a diagnostic of transformer behavior on serialized trajectory data, paired with a structural fix. Pretrained language models that search over their own reasoning steps may face the same failure. Our analysis opens up inference-time context clearing as a candidate way to apply the same isolation without retraining.

</details>


### [209] [Explainable AI for Data-Driven Design of High-Dimensional Predictive Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22243)
*Junyu Yan,Damian Machlanski,Kurt Butler,Panagiotis Dimitrakopoulos,Ewen M Harrison,Bruce Guthrie,Sotirios A Tsaftaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种探索性AI推荐框架，利用可解释AI技术为传统统计模型（如Cox比例风险模型）提供特征排除、非线性项和特征交互三大类数据驱动推荐，从而在不牺牲可解释性的前提下提升高维预测模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维健康数据预测建模中，手动设计最优特征选择、变换和交互项极其困难；复杂机器学习模型虽性能优异但缺乏临床所需的透明性和可解释性，限制了临床信任与决策应用。

Method: 开发了一个探索性AI推荐框架，先用灵活AI建模捕捉数据复杂模式，再借助可解释AI技术（XAI）将模式转化为三类推荐：特征排除、非线性项、特征交互。将基线Cox比例风险模型与采纳推荐后的增强模型进行性能比较。

Result: 在245,614名患者的跌倒/损伤时间预测中，模型推荐排除23个特征、引入2个非线性项和221个特征交互，C-index从0.805提升至0.815，校准度（截距从-0.006到0.003；斜率从1.063到0.950）也得到改善，且所有推荐均有文献支持。在两个额外公开数据集上也验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 该探索性AI推荐框架展示了可解释AI和数据驱动研究设计的潜力，能够在保持模型透明性的同时提升高维预测模型的开发效率和性能，具有广泛的临床应用前景。

Abstract: Predictive modelling is important for health data analysis and data-driven clinical decision-making. However, predictive studies are challenging to design optimally by hand when tens or even hundreds of features require selection, transformation, or interaction modelling. While complex machine learning models offer high performance, their "black-box" nature limits the clinical trust, transparency, and interpretability required for decision-making. We developed and evaluated an Exploratory AI Recommender that provides data-driven recommendations to improve predictive performance of existing interpretable statistical models. The developed framework uses flexible AI modelling to capture complex data patterns and explainable AI techniques to translate the patterns into three recommendation types: feature exclusion, non-linear terms, and feature interactions. We evaluated the framework by comparing predictive performance of a baseline (i.e., no interactions or non-linear terms) Cox Proportional Hazards (CPH) model against an augmented CPH incorporating recommendations suggested by our method. The primary analysis predicts the time to the first occurrence of a fall or related injury in 245,614 patients. Our method recommended excluding 23 features, including non-linear terms for two features, and including 221 suggested feature interactions. The C-index improved from 0.805 (95% CI 0.798-0.812) to 0.815 (95% CI 0.809-0.822), and so did calibration (intercept: -0.006 to 0.003; slope: 1.063 to 0.950). All recommendations were supported by existing literature. The method also proved effective on two additional public datasets, demonstrating wider applicability. The proposed Exploratory AI Recommender demonstrates the potential of explainable AI and data-driven study design to improve the process of developing, and the performance of high-dimensional transparent predictive models.

</details>


### [210] [What are the Right Symmetries for Formal Theorem Proving?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22257)
*Krzysztof Olejniczak,Radoslav Dimitrov,Xingyue Huang,Bernardo Cuenca Grau,Jinwoo Kim,İsmail İlkan Ceylan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于大语言模型的形式化定理证明器对问题表征的表面变化高度敏感，同等语义的陈述成功率差异显著。作者引入重写范畴（Rewriting Categories）这一范畴论框架来形式化两种对称性：证明等变性（Proof Equivariance）和成功不变性（Success Invariance）。研究发现，基于状态的逐策略证明器天然满足证明等变性，而当代LLM证明器两者均不满足。为此提出测试时聚合等效重写的方法，理论上可在采样极限下恢复成功不变性，实验上在固定推理预算下提升了鲁棒性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM定理证明器对问题表示的表面变化极为敏感，语义等价的陈述成功率差异巨大，暴露出对形式数学中固有结构对称性的忽视。核心问题是：形式定理证明中正确的对称性是什么？

Method: 引入重写范畴（Rewriting Categories）的范畴论框架，刻画证明策略产生的组合性、通常不可逆的变换。形式化两种对称性：证明等变性（证明分布在重写下的变换规律）和成功不变性（等价语句具有相同解决概率）。提出测试时聚合方法，对输入的等效重写进行聚合推理。

Result: 基于状态的逐策略证明器天然满足证明等变性；而LLM证明器两种对称性均不满足，在等价表述间表现差异大。测试时聚合方法在理论上可于采样极限下恢复成功不变性，经验上在固定推理预算下提升了鲁棒性和性能。

Conclusion: 对称性是LLM定理证明中缺失的关键归纳偏置，测试时计算可作为近似该偏置的实用路径。

Abstract: Formal theorem provers based on large language models (LLMs) are highly sensitive to superficial variations in problem representation: semantically equivalent statements can exhibit drastically different proof success rates, revealing a failure to respect structural symmetries inherent in formal mathematics. This raises a central question: what are the right symmetries for formal theorem proving? We introduce rewriting categories, a category-theoretic framework capturing the compositional, generally non-invertible transformations induced by proof tactics, and use it to formalize two symmetry notions: proof equivariance, governing how proof distributions transform under rewrites, and success invariance (i.e., invariance of success probability), requiring equivalent statements to be solved with the same probability. We observe that state-based next-tactic provers naturally satisfy proof equivariance by operating on proof states. In contrast, state-of-the-art LLM-based provers satisfy neither property, exhibiting large performance variation across equivalent formulations. To mitigate this, we propose test-time methods that aggregate over equivalent rewritings of the input, showing theoretically that they recover success invariance in the sampling limit, and empirically, that they improve robustness and performance under fixed inference budgets. Our results highlight symmetry as a key missing inductive bias in LLM-based theorem proving and suggest test-time computation as a practical route to approximate it.

</details>


### [211] [Tailoring Teaching to Aptitude: Direction-Adaptive Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22263)
*Hongbin Zhang,Chaozheng Wang,Kehai Chen,Youcheng Pan,Yang Xiang,Jinpeng Wang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出方向自适应自蒸馏（DASD），通过熵路由方向监督解决同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）在复杂推理中因抑制预测不确定性而导致性能退化的问题，在六个数学推理基准上取得最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）方法在复杂推理任务中会抑制预测不确定性，从而削弱探索和假设修正能力。作者通过token级分析发现，OPSD对所有token施加统一的教师监督方向，导致高熵token的探索被抑制，低熵token的步骤准确性下降。

Method: 提出方向自适应自蒸馏（DASD），将统一的教师模仿重构为熵路由的方向监督：对高熵token推离特权教师以保留探索能力，对低熵token拉向教师以稳定步骤级执行。

Result: 在六个数学推理基准上，DASD在宏平均Avg@16指标上超越了强RLVR和自蒸馏基线。Pass@k、推理健康度和泛化分析表明，平均性能的提升源于在不牺牲步骤级执行的前提下保留探索能力。

Conclusion: 方向自适应的自蒸馏方法能够有效解决OPSD在复杂推理中因统一教师监督方向导致的问题，实现探索与执行精度的平衡。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is an emerging LLM post-training paradigm in which the model serves as its own teacher: conditioned on privileged information such as a reference trace or hint, the same policy provides dense token-level supervision on its own rollouts. However, recent studies show that OPSD degrades complex reasoning by suppressing predictive uncertainty, which supports exploration and hypothesis revision. Our token-level analysis shows that this failure arises from applying a uniform direction of teacher supervision across tokens with different uncertainty levels: conformity to the privileged self-teacher suppresses exploration at high entropy, while deviation from the teacher degrades step accuracy at low entropy. Accordingly, we propose \textbf{Direction-Adaptive Self-Distillation} (\textbf{DASD}), which reframes privileged self-distillation from uniform teacher imitation into entropy-routed directional supervision: high-entropy tokens are pushed away from the privileged teacher to preserve exploration, while low-entropy tokens are pulled toward the teacher to stabilize step-level execution. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, DASD achieves the best macro Avg@16 over strong RLVR and self-distillation baselines. Pass@$k$, reasoning-health, and generalization analyses show that these average gains come from preserving exploration without sacrificing step-level execution.

</details>


### [212] [Detecting Atypical Clients in Federated Learning via Representation-Level Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22266)
*Cristian Pérez-Corral,Jose I. Mestre,Alberto Fernández-Hernández,Manuel F. Dolz,Enrique S. Quitana-Ortí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级几何信号，通过评估客户端本地训练对输入空间激活划分的改变（而非比较参数或梯度），来量化客户端与全局模型的功能偏差。该指标可有效识别功能异常漂移的客户端，支持风险感知的联邦学习聚合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中数据异构性导致的不稳定更新和全局性能下降，以及实际部署中客户端更新可能因分布偏移或异常输入而偏离预期行为，现有方法缺乏对客户端功能偏差的有效量化手段。

Method: 提出基于激活诱导输入空间划分的几何度量：在共享探针集上，衡量各客户端本地训练如何改变模型对输入空间的划分方式，得到置换不变、可解释的客户端-全局偏差指标。

Result: 该信号可有效区分稳定异构客户端与功能显著偏离全局范式的客户端，为监控客户端行为提供简单工具。

Conclusion: 所提出的度量方法可作为联邦学习系统中风险感知聚合策略的轻量级监测手段，提升系统可靠性。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative training across distributed clients with heterogeneous data, but such heterogeneity often leads to unstable updates and degraded global performance. Moreover, in practical deployments, client updates may deviate from the expected behavior not only due to benign not i.i.d. distributions, but also due to distributional shifts or anomalous inputs, raising concerns about the reliability of the aggregation process. In this work, we propose a lightweight geometric signal to quantify the functional deviation of a client with respect to the global model. Instead of comparing model parameters or gradients, our approach measures how the local training of each client alters the activation-induced partition of the input space, evaluated on a shared probe set. This yields a permutation-invariant, interpretable metric of client--global divergence that captures differences in how data is processed by the model. We show that this signal effectively identifies clients that induce atypical functional changes, distinguishing stable yet heterogeneous clients from those whose updates significantly diverge from the global regime. As a result, the proposed metric provides a simple tool for monitoring client behavior and enabling risk-aware aggregation strategies in federated learning systems.

</details>


### [213] [EmoTrack: Robust Depression Tracking from Counseling Transcripts across Session Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22286)
*Zhaomin Wu,Jiayi Li,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LongCounsel多会话咨询数据集和EmoTrack抑郁追踪框架，通过结合LLM提取的临床信号与冻结的会话级语义嵌入，训练症状特异性预测器，实现跨单会话和多会话场景的PHQ-8可靠预测，在DAIC-WOZ上MAE降低13.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于文本的心理咨询是AI心理健康支持的重要接口，但现有方法在单会话和多会话场景下均面临挑战：微调方法在数据稀缺时泛化差，而基于提示的LLM方法通常整体处理转录本且难以利用纵向上下文。因此需要一种能兼顾数据效率与跨会话上下文建模的鲁棒抑郁追踪方法。

Method: 提出EmoTrack框架，核心思路是将LLM提取的临床信号与冻结的会话级语义嵌入相结合，在生成的转录表示上训练症状特异性预测器。当存在历史会话时，通过紧凑的跨会话记忆机制整合先前会话信息进行多会话追踪。

Result: 在LongCounsel和DAIC-WOZ数据集上的实验表明，EmoTrack在真实单会话基准上取得显著提升，相比DAIC-WOZ最强基线MAE降低13.5%；在多会话LongCounsel基准上与最强纵向基线性能相当。

Conclusion: EmoTrack通过融合LLM临床信号与语义嵌入、训练症状特异性预测器，在单会话和多会话抑郁追踪场景中均表现出竞争力，验证了该框架在实际心理健康监测中的有效性。

Abstract: Text-based counseling is an important interface for AI mental-health support, where transcripts may be used to monitor depression severity and flag sessions requiring timely human review. However, robust PHQ-8 prediction across session regimes remains challenging: fine-tuning-based methods can exploit richer supervision but may generalize poorly under data scarcity, while prompt-based LLM methods are data-efficient but usually treat each transcript holistically and provide limited support for longitudinal context. We study robust depression tracking from counseling transcripts across single-session and multi-session regimes. We introduce LongCounsel, a multi-session counseling dataset with session-level PHQ-8 supervision for evaluating repeated-session tracking under partial symptom disclosure and cross-session continuity. We further propose EmoTrack, a PHQ-8 prediction framework that combines LLM-extracted clinical signals with frozen turn-level semantic embeddings and trains symptom-specific predictors over the resulting transcript representation. When prior sessions are available, EmoTrack can further incorporate them through compact cross-session memory. Experiments on LongCounsel and DAIC-WOZ show that EmoTrack achieves a clear gain on the real single-session benchmark, including a 13.5% relative MAE reduction over the strongest DAIC-WOZ baseline, and remains competitive with the strongest longitudinal baseline on LongCounsel.

</details>


### [214] [One LR Doesn't Fit All: Heavy-Tail Guided Layerwise Learning Rates for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22297)
*Di He,Songjun Tu,Keyu Wang,Lu Yin,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Layerwise Learning Rate (LLR)方法，基于重尾自正则化理论为Transformer各层分配差异化学习率，弱重尾层用大学习率加速训练，强重尾层用小学习率促进平衡训练。实验表明该方法在60M-1B参数规模下实现1.5倍训练加速，零样本准确率从47.09%提升至49.02%，且调参开销低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习普遍对所有层使用统一学习率，忽略了Transformer架构的异质性，限制了LLM的性能潜力。

Method: 基于重尾自正则化理论，通过分析权重相关矩阵的经验谱密度量化各层的重尾程度，弱重尾层分配大学习率加速训练，强重尾层分配小学习率，实现逐层自适应学习率配置。

Result: 在LLaMA、GPT-nano等架构、AdamW和Muon优化器、60M-1B参数规模下，实现1.5倍训练加速，零样本准确率从47.09%提升至49.02%。

Conclusion: LLR方法通过层间差异化学习率促进了平衡训练，加速收敛并提升泛化能力，且可以从统一基线直接迁移近最优学习率配置，调参成本极低。

Abstract: Learning rate configuration is a fundamental aspect of modern deep learning. The prevailing practice of applying a uniform learning rate across all layers overlooks the structural heterogeneity of Transformers, potentially limiting their effectiveness as the backbone of Large Language Models (LLMs). In this paper, we introduce Layerwise Learning Rate (LLR), an adaptive scheme that assigns distinct learning rates to individual Transformer layers. Our method is grounded in Heavy-Tailed Self-Regularization (HT-SR) theory, which characterizes the empirical spectral density (ESD) of weight correlation matrices to quantify heavy-tailedness. Layers with weaker heavy-tailedness are assigned larger learning rates to accelerate their training, while layers with stronger heavy-tailedness receive smaller learning rates. By tailoring learning rates in this manner, LLR promotes balanced training across layers, leading to faster convergence and improved generalization. Extensive experiments across architectures (from LLaMA to GPT-nano), optimizers (AdamW and Muon), and parameter scales (60M-1B) demonstrate that LLR achieves up to 1.5x training speedup and outperforms baselines, notably raising average zero-shot accuracy from 47.09% to 49.02%. A key advantage of LLR is its low tuning overhead: it transfers nearly optimal LR settings directly from the uniform baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/hed-ucas/Layer-wise-Learning-Rate.

</details>


### [215] [SepsisAI Orchestrator: A Containerized and Scalable Platform for Deploying AI Models and Real-Time Monitoring in Early Sepsis Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22331)
*Santiago Ospitia,John Sanabria,John Garcia-Henao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SepsisAI-Orchestrator，一个用于早期脓毒症检测的开源模块化部署平台，重点解决临床机器学习模型从研究到床边的转化瓶颈。平台集成HL7 FHIR数据预处理、NoSQL存储、容器化LightGBM分类器（复用已发表模型，F1 0.87-0.94）、REST API及Streamlit仪表盘，通过Docker/Kubernetes编排。负载测试（50-1000并发用户）发现U型扩缩行为：副本数匹配CPU线程数时p95延迟降低57.3%（从3.3s到1.41s）且零失败，过度扩缩反而因调度竞争导致性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床机器学习模型预测性能虽强，但因数据表示异构、缺乏标准化部署工作流、研究原型与医院环境并发及延迟需求不匹配等系统级障碍，模型向床旁转化严重受限。现有工作多聚焦模型本身，缺乏对部署基础设施及负载特性的实证研究。

Method: 1) 平台架构：HL7 FHIR启发的CDA预处理 → NoSQL存储 → 容器化LightGBM分类器（通过REST API暴露） → Streamlit临床仪表盘，整体由Docker与Kubernetes编排；2) 复用已验证模型（PhysioNet 2019, F1 0.87-0.94）；3) 使用k6工具进行50-1000并发虚拟用户的负载测试，评估不同副本数（3/6/12/24/48）下的p95延迟和请求失败率。

Result: 在12线程CPU上，副本数从3扩至12时：p95延迟从3.3s降至1.41s（降57.3%），请求失败完全消除；但过度扩缩至24或48副本时，因调度竞争导致性能退化。这种U型扩缩行为在临床AI推理工作负载中此前未被量化报道。

Conclusion: 临床AI推理负载存在U型扩缩行为，副本数最优匹配点为物理CPU线程数；过度扩缩适得其反。平台本身无前瞻性临床验证，但为临床模型床旁部署提供了可复用的基础设施参考实现和负载表征方法。

Abstract: Despite strong predictive results in the clinical machine learning literature, the translation of these models into bedside use remains limited by systems-level barriers: heterogeneous data representations, the absence of standardized deployment workflows, and a mismatch between research prototypes and the concurrency and latency requirements of hospital environments. We present the SepsisAI-Orchestrator, an open-source modular platform that addresses this deployment gap for early sepsis detection. The platform integrates HL7 FHIR-inspired Clinical Document Architecture (CDA) preprocessing, NoSQL storage, a containerized LightGBM classifier served via REST APIs, and a Streamlit clinical dashboard, orchestrated with Docker and Kubernetes. A previously validated LightGBM model (F1 0.87-0.94 on PhysioNet 2019) is reused without modification; the contribution lies in the surrounding infrastructure and its empirical characterization under load.
